### [1] [Anomalies for compact simple Lie groups via $G$-kernels for loop group nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23321)
*Marcel Bischoff,Pradyut Karmakar*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文计算了跳跃环路（jump loops）所给出外同态 θ_k 的 Sutherland 障碍（Sutherland obstruction），证明其为 H^3_Borel(G, T) 中正基本类的 k 倍，且 θ_k 忠实，因而是 G-核（G-kernel）；由此推出紧致单连通半单群上 H^4(BG, Z) 的每个类都是超有限 III_1 型因子上某 G-核的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跳跃环路诱导的群到环群网（loop group net）局部因子的外自同构群的外同态，其障碍类与忠实性此前未明确确定；作者旨在精确识别这一不变量并刻画可实现的障碍类范围。

Method: 通过 Sutherland 障碍（Sutherland obstruction）框架研究外同态 θ_k；在 A、D、E 型中，将极大环面（maximal torus）的跳跃环路与姊妹论文中的格 α-诱导（lattice α-induction）构造在当前生成元与荷电生成元上进行比较，再用四次 Cartan 限制取积分；更高级别由张量可加性（tensor additivity）得到，其余类型沿指数一嵌入（index-one embedding）限制到单链群（simply laced groups）处理。

Result: θ_k 的 Sutherland 障碍等于 k 倍正基本类（positive basic class），且 θ_k 忠实，故构成 G-核；中心元素通过简单流（simple currents）的外自同构作用。由此，对每个紧致单连通半单 G，H^4(BG, Z) 中每个类均可作为超有限 III_1 型因子上 G-核的障碍实现。

Conclusion: 该工作精确刻画了跳跃环路外同态的障碍与忠实性，并给出紧致单连通半单群上四维 Borel 上同调类在超有限 III_1 型因子上作为 G-核障碍的完全可实现性结论。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a compact simply connected simple Lie group and $k \geq 1$. Jump loops give an outer homomorphism $θ_k$ from $G$ to the outer automorphisms of the local factor of the level-$k$ loop group net, which is the hyperfinite type $\mathrm{III}_1$ factor. We show that its Sutherland obstruction is $k$ times the positive basic class in $H^3_{\mathrm{Borel}}(G, \mathbb{T}) \cong H^{4}(BG, \mathbb{Z})$ and that $θ_k$ is faithful, so it is a $G$-kernel. Central elements act by the outer automorphisms of the simple currents. In types A, D, E the level-one class is computed by comparing maximal-torus jump loops with the lattice $α$-induction construction of the companion paper on both current and charged generators, followed by integral degree-four Cartan restriction. Higher levels follow by tensor additivity, and the remaining types by restriction along index-one embeddings into simply laced groups. As a consequence, for every compact simply connected semisimple $G$, every class in $H^4(BG, \mathbb{Z})$ is the obstruction of a $G$-kernel on the hyperfinite type $\mathrm{III}_1$ factor.

</details>

### [2] [The typical red cell makes too little vacuole material for coalescence to build the large vacuoles of pocked cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22646)
*Zhaojie Chai,Jianlu Zheng,He Li,Ming Dao,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本研究用高分辨率粒子模型（particle model）与群体模型（population model）检验“可见空泡由小空泡融合长大”的假说，发现融合并非体积增长的限速环节，物质的供给才是关键，并据此解释了陷窝细胞（pocked cells）在脾功能评估中的数量、尺寸与年龄分布特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 携带胞内空泡（vacuoles）的红细胞即陷窝细胞（pocked cells），是脾功能（splenic function）的敏感标志物：健康人约0.5%，脾切除后为20%–70%。肉眼不可见的微小空泡普遍存在，可见空泡被认为由小空泡合并（merge）而成，但该机制缺乏定量检验，其与脾清除（splenic clearance）的关系亦不明确。

Method: 构建可解析细胞膜（membrane）、胞内内容物及每个空泡的粒子模型（particle model），结合细胞可生成空泡体积的清单，模拟管流、剪切流（shear flow）与血管流（vascular flow）中的空泡相遇与融合。另建立群体模型（population model），设定空泡尺寸在形成时确定，主要产生于年轻细胞，且少数细胞以远高速度生成可供大空泡使用的物质；并将模型脾裂隙（splenic slit）用于检验细胞通过条件。

Result: 在管流、剪切流与血管流中，空泡在一秒内即相遇，若允许融合，十个可合并为一至五个；相遇并非生长限制因素，物质才是。按脾切除后测得的速率，一个平均细胞在120天寿命内产生的全部空泡融合成直径0.6 μm的单一结构，即健康细胞所携带的大小，但体积比无脾患者1.5 μm的结构小16倍。群体模型可再现已发表的计数、尺寸与年龄分布（age profiles），并在一手测量上支持脾清除随细胞老化而上升数倍；允许少数细胞的空泡融合时拟合效果几近相当。

Conclusion: 空泡生长受物质供给而非融合速率限制，可见空泡尺寸似在形成时即已设定。模型脾裂隙处，细胞能否通过取决于其剩余表面积（spare surface），空泡仅在其位于前端时才产生影响。作者提出可通过裂隙实验检验短暂相遇是否足以引发空泡融合，从而进一步区分上述机制。

Abstract: Red blood cells carrying intracellular vacuoles, called pocked cells, are a sensitive marker of splenic function: about 0.5% of cells in healthy people, 20 to 70% after splenectomy. Vacuoles too small to see are common, and visible ones are thought to grow when small ones merge. We tested that idea with a particle model that resolves the cell's membrane, its contents and every vacuole, and with an inventory of the vacuole volume a cell can make. In tube, shear and vascular flows, a cell's vacuoles met within a second, and, allowed to fuse, ten merged into one to five. Meeting is not what limits growth; material is. At the rate measured after splenectomy, all the vacuoles an average cell makes in its 120-day life fuse into one body 0.6 $μ$m across, the size healthy cells carry, but 16 times smaller in volume than the 1.5-$μ$m bodies of patients without a spleen. Taking vacuole size as set at formation, a population model in which vacuoles are made mainly in young cells, and far faster in a small minority that alone makes material for a large one, accounts for the published counts, sizes and age profiles and, on first-hand measurements, favours a splenic clearance that rises several-fold as cells age; letting that minority's vacuoles fuse fits nearly as well. At a model splenic slit, the cell's spare surface decided passage, and a vacuole mattered only when it led. A slit experiment would test whether brief encounters fuse vacuoles.

</details>

### [3] [Noise-Induced Restoration of Entropy Concavity Near Gaussian Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22386)
*Fayez Abu-Ajamieh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究一维非平衡态在噪声下熵凹性（entropy concavity）的定量恢复，对“部分热化态”混合模型给出端点曲率精确公式，并在领头非高斯 Hermite 模态上证明全混合区间（all-mixing）的严格凹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问题源于 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck/Fokker–Planck 弛豫过程中的熵几何（entropy geometry），其中相对 score 对应非平衡热力学力、相对 Fisher 信息对应熵产生率，故需判断混合态熵对混合参数是否凹。

Method: 以 $X_q=\sqrt q\,X+\sqrt{1-q}\,G$ 建模部分热化态，考察两份独立拷贝混合态 $h(\sqrt{1-t}\,X_q+\sqrt t\,Y_q)$ 的凹性；推导对称端点曲率公式，分离非负 OU 恢复项与四阶累积量修正，并推广至 $e^{-a|x|^p}$（$p>3/2$）广义高斯态，辅以后验方差恒等式与谱/MMSE 重构。

Result: 对对称对数凹输入的半热化给出定量外区定理，将端点障碍限制于两个显式有界的近平衡区；辅助不等式 $A\ge2\delta$ 在 Gaussian 平衡的显式非线性邻域内得证；对 $p_a=\phiG(1+a\He_4)$、$0<a\le10^{-2}$，证明所有 $0<t<1$、$0<q\le5/8$ 下熵严格凹，覆盖未解的对称区间 $0<q\le4/7$。

Conclusion: 熵凹性在噪声诱导下于该显式非平衡族中定量成立，端点曲率分解与 $A\ge2\delta$ 不等式共同收紧端点界；更广泛对数凹族的数值搜索仅作为辅助证据，不构成一般性结论。

Abstract: We study a quantitative form of noise-induced restoration of entropy concavity for one-dimensional nonequilibrium states relaxing toward a Gaussian thermal equilibrium. A partially thermalized state is modeled by $X_q=\sqrt q\,X+\sqrt{1-q}\,G$, where $G$ is Gaussian equilibrium noise and $q$ is the retained signal fraction. For two independently prepared copies we ask when the entropy of the mixed state $h(\sqrt{1-t}\,X_q+\sqrt t\,Y_q)$ is concave in the mixing parameter. The problem is motivated by entropy geometry along Ornstein--Uhlenbeck/Fokker--Planck relaxation, where the relative score is the nonequilibrium thermodynamic force and relative Fisher information is entropy-production rate. We derive an exact symmetric endpoint-curvature formula, separating a nonnegative Ornstein--Uhlenbeck restoring term from a fourth-cumulant correction, and extend it to nonsmooth generalized-Gaussian states $e^{-a|x|^p}$ for $p>3/2$. For half thermalization of a symmetric log-concave input, posterior-variance identities give a quantitative outer-region theorem confining any endpoint obstruction to two explicitly bounded near-equilibrium sectors. We also give spectral and MMSE reformulations of the auxiliary inequality $A\ge2δ$, prove it in an explicit nonlinear neighborhood of Gaussian equilibrium, and show how it sharpens the endpoint bounds. The main all-mixing result concerns the leading symmetric non-Gaussian Hermite mode: for $p_a=\phiG(1+a\He_4)$, $0<a\le10^{-2}$, we prove strict entropy concavity for every $0<t<1$ and every $0<q\le5/8$, hence throughout the currently unresolved symmetric range $0<q\le4/7$ for this explicit nonequilibrium family. Numerical searches over broader log-concave families are reported only as supporting evidence.

</details>


### [4] [Thermodynamics of coupled electromagnetic and mass fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22391)
*Fei Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将Gibbs–Duhem关系扩展至包含物质与场运动的动能自由能贡献，提出统一的动力学Gibbs–Duhem关系，为化学、力学与电磁输运提供共同的热力学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热力学框架对化学、力学与电磁输运分别处理，缺乏统一原则；作者希望在不引入各自独立热力学原理的前提下，将动能贡献一致地纳入Gibbs–Duhem框架。

Method: 将Gibbs–Duhem关系扩展为含动能自由能项的“动力学Gibbs–Duhem关系”，并在自由能演化的拉格朗日表述（Lagrangian formulation）中构建扩散、动量输运与电磁动力学的共同结构，同时区分耗散与非耗散输运。

Result: 对非均匀化学系统，框架重现Cahn–Hilliard动力学与由成分梯度引起的Korteweg应力的耦合；对电磁系统，将电、磁、电荷与力学贡献纳入同一热力学表述。在等熵极限（isentropic limit）下，特征传播速度由热力学压强对密度的导数决定，分别给出声速与光速。

Conclusion: 动能贡献可被一致地纳入Gibbs–Duhem框架，从而为耦合的化学、力学与电磁输运提供共同的热力学基础，无需为每种机制单独设立热力学原理。

Abstract: We formulate a unified thermodynamic framework for chemical, mechanical, and electromagnetic transport by extending the Gibbs--Duhem relation to include kinetic free-energy contributions associated with material and field motion. The resulting kinetic Gibbs--Duhem relation establishes a thermodynamic connection between free-energy variations, generalized chemical potentials, pressure, and transport momentum, while preserving the invariance of the system free energy under changes of reference frame. Within a Lagrangian formulation of free-energy evolution, the relation provides a common structure for diffusion, momentum transport, and electromagnetic dynamics. For nonuniform chemical systems, the framework recovers the coupling between Cahn--Hilliard dynamics and the Korteweg stress arising from composition gradients. Its extension to electromagnetic systems incorporates electric, magnetic, charge, and mechanical contributions into the same thermodynamic formulation, yielding a unified description of electromagnetic and momentum evolution. The framework further separates dissipative from nondissipative transport and establishes a common thermodynamic form for mechanical and electromagnetic propagation. In the isentropic limit, the characteristic propagation velocity is determined by the derivative of the thermodynamic pressure with respect to density, yielding the speed of sound for mechanical systems and the speed of light for electromagnetic systems. These results show that kinetic contributions can be incorporated consistently into the Gibbs--Duhem framework, providing a common thermodynamic basis for coupled chemical, mechanical, and electromagnetic transport without introducing separate thermodynamic principles for each mechanism.

</details>


### [5] [Parity-dependent sublattice structure of local $2\times2$ correlations in domino tilings of the Aztec diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22974)
*Chaeyeon Kim,Suhwan Yoo,Jeehoon Choi,Hee Young Kwon,Changyeon Won*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文利用 Kuo 图形凝聚（graphical condensation）恒等式与逆 Kasteleyn 矩阵（inverse Kasteleyn matrix），精确刻画 Aztec 菱形（Aztec diamond）随机多米诺铺砌中局部的 \(2\times2\) 关联，并揭示由 \(n\) 的奇偶性决定的子晶格（sublattice）选择规则与最大值位置规律，同时与方形棋盘格作对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解具有冻结角（frozen corners）与北极圈（Arctic circle）边界液体区域的二聚体模型（dimer model）中局部关联的空间结构，并将 Aztec 菱形几何与普通方形棋盘格（chessboard）上同类局部统计进行区分比较。

Method: 对每个基本方格，利用 \(P_H=P_V=P_1P_2+P_3P_4\) 这一 Kuo 图形凝聚恒等式，将平行多米诺覆盖概率的空间分布约化为单边概率；后者由 \(\mathrm{AZ}(n)\) 全部方格的逆 Kasteleyn 矩阵精确计算。方格按左下角格点颜色分为两个交错子晶格，并考察其平均对比度、全局最大值位置及模 4 的偏爱方格模式，与 \(L\times L\) 棋盘格结果对照。

Result: \(n\) 的奇偶性决定中心附近占优的子晶格：奇数 \(n\) 为包含中心方格的子晶格，偶数 \(n\) 为另一子晶格；该符号规则对 \(4\le n\le22\) 的平均子晶格对比度均成立。它同时决定 \(P_H\) 全局最大值的位置（奇数 \(n\) 位于中心或其邻近，偶数 \(n\) 偏离中心若干格距）；中心子晶格内的偏爱方格服从与中心精确钉扎平均高度（mean height）相符的模 4 模式。棋盘格上同一规则以 \(n\) 替换为半宽 \(L/2\) 后仍成立，但对比度弱 2 至 6 倍，中心边概率不等于 \(1/4\)，且 \(P_H\) 的最大值钉扎于角点。

Conclusion: 精确的 \(1/4\) 取值与由奇偶性选择的内点型最大值，属于 Aztec 几何所特有的性质，而非一般二聚体或棋盘格系统共有的特征。

Abstract: We study local $2\times2$ correlations in uniformly random domino tilings of the Aztec diamond $\mathrm{AZ}(n)$, a dimer model with frozen corners and a liquid region bounded by the Arctic circle, and compare them with the square chessboard. For every elementary square the probabilities $P_H$ and $P_V$ of the two coverings by a pair of parallel dominoes satisfy $P_H=P_V=P_1P_2+P_3P_4$, where $P_1,\dots,P_4$ are the probabilities of the four surrounding edges. This known identity, a case of Kuo's graphical condensation, reduces the spatial profile of $P_H$ to single-edge probabilities, which we compute exactly from the inverse Kasteleyn matrix for all squares of $\mathrm{AZ}(n)$. The squares form two interleaved sublattices, distinguished by the color of their lower-left cell. The parity of $n$ decides which sublattice dominates near the center: the one containing the central square for odd $n$, and the other one for even $n$. This sign rule holds for the averaged sublattice contrast for all $4\le n\le22$. It also fixes the location of the global maximum of $P_H$, which lies at or next to the center for odd $n$ and several lattice spacings away for even $n$. Within the central sublattice, the preferred square follows a mod-4 pattern that matches the exactly pinned mean height of the center. On the $L\times L$ chessboard the same rule holds with $n$ replaced by the half-width $L/2$, but the contrast is two to six times weaker, the central edge probability never equals $1/4$, and the maxima of $P_H$ are pinned to the corners. The exact $1/4$ values and the interior, parity-selected maxima are therefore features of the Aztec geometry.

</details>


### [6] [Pressure-Dependent hcp-to-fcc Phase Transition Path Derived from Exact Lattice Summations over Inverse Power Law Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22855)
*Tage W. Maltby,Andres Robles-Navarro,Jonathan K. Hartlaub,Andreas A. Buchheit,Øivind Wilhelmsen,Morten Hammer,Peter Schwerdtfeger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文沿 Burgers-Bain 转变路径系统研究了高压（约数百 GPa）下 hcp 与 fcc 之间的相变，采用从 Lennard-Jones 势到含三体 Axilrod-Teller-Muto 项的扩展势的分层势函数体系，并结合 DFT 计算固态氩。核心发现是相变伴随显著的对称性破缺，高压下 hcp→fcc 激活势垒随压力近似线性增长，且 DFT 揭示压缩下多体相互作用重要性上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: hcp-fcc 相变在高压极端条件下广泛存在，但其激活势垒、最低能量路径（minimum-energy path）以及不同层次原子间势函数（interatomic potentials）描述该过程的能力尚缺乏系统比较，尤其多体效应与色散力（dispersion forces）随压缩的变化规律有待厘清。

Method: 构建从 (n,m)-Lennard-Jones 势、逆幂展开（inverse-power expansions）两体相互作用到 Axilrod-Teller-Muto 三体项的分层势函数体系，并结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算固态氩。对逆幂势采用精确晶格求和与基于各向异性 Epstein zeta 函数推广的高效积分表示求自由能，随后进行晶格参数优化，从而确定压力依赖的 Burgers-Bain 最低能量路径及激活势垒。

Result: 该转变表现出连接 Burgers 与 Bain 路径的显著对称性破缺（symmetry breaking），并获 DFT 证实。高压下 hcp→fcc 激活势垒因压力-体积贡献主导而随压力近似线性增加；引入熵（entropy）与非谐效应（anharmonic effects）后，与实验温度依赖体积直至熔点高度一致。两体势仅在极高压力下预测路径经过体心立方（bcc）结构，而 DFT 通常预测绕过 bcc，Axilrod-Teller-Muto 项可略减小此差异；DFT 在色散力重要性降低的高压区表现更佳。

Conclusion: 研究确立了压力依赖 Burgers-Bain 路径与激活势垒的可靠计算方法，并表明压缩条件下多体相互作用日益重要，简单两体势在高压路径拓扑与精度上存在系统性局限，需借助含三体项或 DFT 的描述。

Abstract: We investigate the phase transition between hexagonal close-packed (hcp) and face-centered cubic (fcc) structures along the Burgers-Bain transformation path at pressures up to several hundred gigapascals. We consider a hierarchy of interatomic potentials, from $(n,m)$-Lennard-Jones potentials to extended Lennard-Jones two-body interactions in terms of inverse-power expansions and the Axilrod-Teller-Muto three-body term, alongside density functional theory (DFT) calculations for solid argon. For inverse-power potentials, free energies are evaluated through exact lattice sums and efficient integral representations based on generalizations of the Epstein zeta function to anisotropic interaction kernels, followed by lattice-parameter optimization. This methodology enables an accurate determination of the pressure-dependent Burgers-Bain minimum-energy path and the associated activation barrier for the phase transition. The transformation exhibits pronounced symmetry breaking connecting the Burgers and Bain paths, a feature confirmed by DFT calculations. At high pressure, the hcp-to-fcc activation barrier increases approximately linearly with pressure, owing to the dominant pressure-volume contribution. Including entropy and anharmonic effects yields excellent agreement with experimental temperature-dependent volumes up to the melting point. DFT results also highlight the increasing importance of many-body interactions under compression. Whereas two-body potentials predict that the path passes through the body-centered cubic (bcc) structure only at very high pressures, DFT generally predicts a pathway that bypasses bcc. The Axilrod-Teller-Muto term slightly reduces this discrepancy. Furthermore, DFT performs better at high pressures, where dispersion forces become less important.

</details>


### [7] [Diffusion-controlled reactions with time-dependent surface reactivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23017)
*Denis S. Grebenkov,Yilin Ye*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究反应性表面（reactive surface）上反应性随时间变化的扩散控制反应，量化反应性时间变化对单粒子存活概率（survival probability）与首次反应时间（first-reaction time, FRT）分布的影响，并用蒙特卡洛模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观描述通常采用带Robin边界条件（Robin boundary condition）的扩散方程，但反应性随时间变化如何影响单粒子的存活概率与首次反应时间尚缺乏定量刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用绝热近似（adiabatic description）识别首次反应时间分布的五个长时间区间（regime）；并给出该问题的概率重构（probabilistic reformulation），与遭遇依赖反应性（encounter-dependent reactivity）情形进行比较。

Result: 长时间FRT分布涵盖指数（exponential）、拉伸指数（stretched-exponential）至幂律衰减（power-law decay）等多类区间；当反应性衰减足够快时，即便在有界区域内扩散，也存在非零的“永不反应”概率。

Conclusion: 反应性的时间变化显著改变扩散控制反应的长时间统计特征，理论预测经大量蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）验证。

Abstract: We investigate diffusion-controlled reactions on a reactive surface with time-dependent reactivity. Starting from a conventional formulation via diffusion equation with Robin boundary condition for the macroscopic concentration of particles, we aim at quantifying how temporal variations of reactivity affect the survival probability and the associated first-reaction time (FRT) of a single particle on the reactive surface. Using an adiabatic description, we first identify five long-time regimes of the first-reaction time distribution, ranging from exponential and stretched-exponential to power-law decay. In particular, sufficiently rapid decay of the reactivity leads to a nonzero probability of never reacting, despite diffusion in a bounded domain. Second, we provide a probabilistic reformulation of this problem and compare it with a different scenario of encounter-dependent reactivity. Our theoretical predictions are validated via extensive Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


### [8] [Nonergodicity from Hilbert-Subspace Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23105)
*Yuuya Chiba,Ryusuke Hamazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文识别出一类由单一泡利串（Pauli strings）构成的非可积量子多体系统，具有大量非对易局域守恒量（local conserved quantities）并因指数简并谱（exponentially degenerate energy spectra）呈现部分遍历性破缺（partial ergodicity breaking）。其核心新机制为“希尔伯特子空间克隆（Hilbert-subspace cloning）”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探讨非可积量子多体系统中遍历性（ergodicity）如何被打破，以及在存在大量非对易局域守恒量时，热化（thermalization）与量子相干（quantum coherence）的长期行为如何被修正。

Method: 作者构造并分析了一类由单一泡利串（Pauli strings）组成的非可积模型，证明其拥有大量非对易局域守恒量，并将系统分解为指数多个互不连通的希尔伯特子空间（Hilbert subspaces），每个子空间上作用相同的混沌哈密顿量，形成“希尔伯特子空间克隆”结构。

Result: 每个克隆内部的动力学仍然热化，但不同克隆之间的量子相干性受到保护，并可被局域可观测量（local observables）检测；局域可观测量的长期行为由一种保持相干性的部分吉布斯系综（coherence-preserving partial Gibbs ensemble）描述，隐藏子系统保留其初始量子相干。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一种新的部分遍历性破缺机制：非对易局域守恒量与谱简并共同导致希尔伯特子空间克隆，使系统在内部热化的同时保留可观测的量子相干性，为理解量子多体系统的热化与相干保护提供了新框架。

Abstract: We identify a class of nonintegrable quantum many-body systems composed of single Pauli strings that possess an extensive number of noncommuting local conserved quantities and exhibit partial ergodicity breaking due to exponentially degenerate energy spectra. We show that such systems exhibit a novel mechanism, Hilbert-subspace cloning, where the identical chaotic Hamiltonian acts on each of exponentially many disconnected Hilbert subspaces. Although the dynamics within each clone thermalizes, quantum coherence between clones remains protected and detectable by local observables. Consequently, the long-time behavior of local observables is described by a coherence-preserving partial Gibbs ensemble, where the hidden subsystem retains its initial quantum coherence.

</details>


### [9] [Emergence of Correlations Between Records and Maximum in Random Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23217)
*Satya N. Majumdar,Sanjib Sabhapandit,Gregory Schehr*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作在离散时间序列中精确计算最大值 M 与记录数 K 的协方差，将其作为极端事件历史演化的定量探针，并证明在连续 IID（独立同分布）情形下二者不相关，但对含零处 delta 分量的分布则出现非平凡的协方差行为，且与德国降水数据吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过最大值 M 与记录数 K 的协方差刻画“导致极端事件的历史过程”，并检验这种不相关性（uncorrelatedness）在何种分布扰动下会失效，从而为极端事件的可预测性提供理论依据。

Method: 对 n 项离散时间序列精确解析计算协方差 C_q(n)；对连续 IID 序列给出任意 n≥1 下 M 与 K 不相关的结论；引入含 x=0 处幅度为 0<q<1 的 delta 分量的不连续概率密度函数（PDF）以建模降水序列中的无雨日。

Result: 发现存在临界值 q_c，使 C_q(n) 随 n 的行为由单调（q<q_c）转为非单调（q>q_c），并精确求出 q_c；对大 n，C_q(n) 呈幂律衰减（power-law decay），其指数依赖 p(x) 的尾部；与德国多个气象站的实测降水数据比较，理论与数据高度一致。

Conclusion: M 与 K 的不相关性是脆弱的，分布的不连续性即可使之显著改变；协方差 C_q(n) 可作为刻画极端事件历史依赖性的有效统计量，并在真实降水数据中得到验证。

Abstract: We compute exactly the covariance between the maximum $M$ and the number of records $K$ in a discrete time series of $n$ entries as a quantitative probe of the history leading to an extreme event. For continuous IID series, with a common probability distribution function (PDF) $p(x)$, we find the striking result that $M$ and $K$ are uncorrelated for any $n \geq 1$. We show that this independence is rather fragile and the covariance $C_q(n)$ between $M$ and $K$ becomes nonzero when $p(x)$ becomes discontinuous due to a delta-component at $x=0$ with an amplitude $0< q < 1$. This time series models the occurrence of dry days with probability $q$ in a precipitation time series when there is no rainfall. We show that there is an interesting shape transition at a critical value $q_c$ where the covariance $C_q(n)$, as a function of $n$, changes from monotonic ($q<q_c$) to non monotonic behavior (for $q>q_c$). We compute the critical strength $q_c$ exactly. Furthermore, for large $n$, we show that $C_q(n)$ decays as a power law, with an exponent that depends on the tail of $p(x)$. We then compare our theoretical predictions with real precipitation data from several weather stations across Germany, finding a very good agreement.

</details>


### [10] [Fundamental Bounds on the Oscillation Efficiency of Markov Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23310)
*Chen Jia*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在马尔可夫网络（Markov networks）中建立了振荡效率的普适动力学与热力学界（dynamical and thermodynamic bounds），指出有限状态空间对功率谱峰增强（spectral peak enhancement）的根本限制，并揭示振荡效率与模态相干性（modal coherence）并非同一目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机振荡（stochastic oscillations）通常仅通过功率谱（power spectra）中的峰来识别，但其强度与能量代价的根本限制尚不明确，且谱振荡强度与模态相干性之间的关系缺乏系统区分。

Method: 以马尔可夫网络为框架，推导振荡效率的普适动力学与热力学界，并建立将振荡效率与弛豫速度（relaxation speed）及稳态概率（stationary probabilities）相联系的耗散界（dissipation bounds）。

Result: 有限状态空间约束谱峰增强；最大化模态相干的网络未必最大化振荡效率；在既有显式谱耗散估计（explicit spectral dissipation estimates）为零的区域，所得界仍严格为正，且对正规网络（normal networks）与均匀环（uniform rings）约束更强。

Conclusion: 研究区分了谱振荡强度与模态相干性，并表明网络动力学与热力学耗散共同限制随机振荡，在给定弛豫时间尺度下维持显著振荡存在不可避免的能量代价。

Abstract: Stochastic oscillations are commonly identified through peaks in their power spectra, yet the fundamental limits on their strength and energetic cost remain unclear. Here we establish universal dynamical and thermodynamic bounds on oscillation efficiency in Markov networks, showing how finite state spaces constrain spectral peak enhancement. In particular, we demonstrate that networks maximizing modal coherence need not maximize oscillation efficiency. We further derive dissipation bounds that connect oscillation efficiency to the relaxation speed and stationary probabilities, revealing an unavoidable energetic cost of maintaining pronounced oscillations at a specified relaxation timescale. These bounds remain strictly positive in regimes where existing explicit spectral dissipation estimates vanish, with stronger constraints for normal networks and uniform rings. Our results distinguish spectral oscillatory strength from modal coherence and reveal how network dynamics and thermodynamic dissipation jointly limit stochastic oscillations.

</details>


### [11] [Transport and thermalization under noncommuting localized constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23781)
*Amir Nesturov,Attila Takács,Lenart Zadnik*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究空间局域守恒量（spatially localized conserved quantities）对量子与经典多体系统输运及热化（thermalization）的影响，发现其对输运系数基本无影响，但可导致局域各态遍历性（local ergodicity）的强烈破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域约束常与格点规范理论（lattice gauge theories）相联系，本文希望对不具备此类解释的模型考察局域守恒量对输运与热化的作用，填补相关认识空白。

Method: 考察可积量子 Volterra 模型、其非可积经典极限以及由后者导出的两种元胞自动机（cellular automata）；对量子情形建立每个希尔伯特空间碎片（Hilbert-space fragment）内动力学到量子手征 Potts 链（quantum chiral Potts chain）的精确映射。

Result: 离散自由度系统中约束导致希尔伯特空间或构型空间的均匀碎片化（uniform fragmentation）；量子 Volterra 模型中能量输运为弹道式（ballistic），各碎片输运系数相同；非可积经典极限中输运为扩散式（diffusive），扩散常数与约束是否固定无关；元胞自动机中局域约束不改变输运但强烈破坏局域各态遍历性。

Conclusion: 空间局域守恒量可在不改变输运行为的前提下显著影响热化性质，表明输运与局域各态遍历性的破坏机制可以相互解耦。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of spatially localized conserved quantities on transport and thermalization in quantum and classical many-body systems. We consider several related models: the integrable quantum Volterra model, its nonintegrable classical limit, and two cellular automata derived from the latter. Although localized constraints are often associated with lattice gauge theories, our models do not admit such an interpretation. In systems with discrete degrees of freedom, these constraints induce uniform fragmentation of the Hilbert or configuration space. In the quantum Volterra model, we derive an exact mapping of the dynamics within each Hilbert-space fragment onto a quantum chiral Potts chain. We find that energy transport is ballistic, with identical transport coefficients across all fragments. In the nonintegrable classical limit, energy transport is diffusive, and we similarly find the diffusion constant to be independent of whether or not the constraints are fixed (resolved). Finally, in the cellular automata, we show that, despite leaving transport unaffected, localized constraints lead to a strong breakdown of local ergodicity.

</details>

### [12] [QMBL: the Quantum Many-Body Leaderboard. A live record of published ground-state energies for lattice Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22387)
*Tristan Zerweck,Juan Carrasquilla*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文提出 QMBL（Quantum Many-Body Leaderboard），一个持续维护、交互式的格点哈密顿量（lattice Hamiltonians）基态能量公开记录平台，作为科学共识的“能量层”，最新 v0.4.0 版收录六大模型族、395 个实例上的 1434 个能量值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态物理（condensed matter physics）中对相图（phase diagrams）的认识依赖解析、数值与实验等多方法收敛；而量子多体基态（quantum many-body ground states）变分结果的质量主要仅以能量值及其误差评判，缺乏统一的汇总与对照视图。

Method: 构建并维护一个交互式排行榜（leaderboard），系统收录已发表的格点哈密顿量基态能量，按模型族与实例组织，形成社区成果的单一汇总视图。

Result: v0.4.0 版本收录六大模型族、395 个实例的 1434 个能量值；其中 153 个实例无精确解（exact solution），任何新纪录都直接构成对开放科学问题的改进。

Conclusion: QMBL 为量子多体基态能量提供共识性参照层；无精确解的 153 个实例构成配套基准 QMBL: Open Problems 的目标集合，推动开放问题的持续攻关。

Abstract: In condensed matter physics, scientists explore phase diagrams of matter through various methods (analysis, numerics, experiment) and achieve consensus when enough methods agree. The quality of variational results for quantum many-body ground states is mainly judged by energy values and respective errors. Here, we present QMBL, the Quantum Many-Body Leaderboard (https://www.qmbl.org), serving as the energy layer of the scientific consensus. It is a maintained, interactive record of published ground-state energies for lattice Hamiltonians. The latest release v0.4.0 holds 1434 energies on 395 instances of six model families and collects all of the community's efforts into one view. Of those instances 153 have no exact solution and any new record serves as a direct improvement to an open science problem. They are the targets of a companion benchmark, QMBL: Open Problems.

</details>


### [13] [Probing nonequilibrium transport and density profiles in tilted interacting fermion chains with local reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22463)
*Andrea Nava,N. Aucar Boidi,Reinhold Egger,Karen Hallberg,C. R. Proetto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究在倾斜的无自旋费米子链（tilted spinless fermion chains）中，如何利用局域粒子库（local particle reservoirs）探测输运与密度分布，并借助有限链 Lindblad 计算定义了依赖阈值的透射范围与弱耦合空间探针方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在建立电流测量与驱动相互作用链中空间透射及密度结构之间的联系，探索局域粒子库作为输运与密度分布探针的可行性。

Method: 采用有限链 Lindblad 计算（Lindblad calculations），在链两端分别注入与提取粒子；由稳态电流随链长变化定义阈值依赖的透射范围（transmission range），并引入第三个弱耦合提取库作为局域空间探针。

Result: 透射范围随倾斜增大而减小，并在所研究参数区间内与平衡态密度交叉宽度（equilibrium density-crossover width）的趋势一致；相互作用可通过补偿场致能量代价增强透射，而强极化构型抑制输运；弱探针电流与探针存在时的局域占据严格成正比，归一化电流在探针耦合趋零时趋于未扰动的非平衡密度。

Conclusion: 该方案可检测交错密度调制（staggered density modulations），并显示边界电流接近稳定后体密度仍可持续弛豫，从而将电流测量与驱动相互作用链的空间透射和密度结构相连接。

Abstract: We investigate how local particle reservoirs can probe transport and density profiles in tilted spinless fermion chains with nearest-neighbor interactions. Using finite-chain Lindblad calculations with particle injection and extraction at opposite ends, we define a threshold-dependent transmission range from the stationary current as the chain length is varied. This range decreases with increasing tilt and follows the trends of the equilibrium density-crossover width in the parameter regimes studied. Interactions can enhance transmission by compensating field-induced energy costs, whereas strongly polarized configurations suppress transport. A third, weakly coupled extraction reservoir provides a complementary spatial probe: its current is exactly proportional to the local occupation in the presence of the probe, and the normalized current approaches the unperturbed nonequilibrium density as the probe coupling vanishes. We illustrate the detection of staggered density modulations and show that bulk densities can continue to relax after boundary currents have nearly stabilized. These protocols connect current measurements to spatial transmission and density structure in driven interacting chains.

</details>


### [14] [Anyon bound states in MoTe$_2$ Fractional Chern Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22473)
*Tianhong Lu,Luiz H. Santos*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在扭角双层 MoTe$_2$ 分数陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator, FCI）中，可移动偶极激子（dipolar exciton）与 $e/3$ 准空穴（quasihole）可形成 meV 量级的束缚态（bound state），并展现强激子—准空穴纠缠；该复合体可用于在实空间探测任意子（anyon）的内部结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将任意子（anyon）与光学活性激子（exciton）绑定，可为操控分数激发（fractional excitations）及揭示任意子内部空间结构提供途径，而此前缺少对激子—任意子束缚态形成条件的系统性刻画。

Method: 采用基于连续介质的相互作用模型（continuum-based interacting model）进行精确对角化（exact diagonalization），考察激子迁移率与激子—FCI 间距离对束缚态的影响，并通过空间关联函数分析束缚态的实空间特征。

Result: 识别出 meV 量级束缚能与强激子—准空穴纠缠的参数区间；空间关联揭示激子周围存在携带约 $e/3$ 电荷的局域耗尽核（depletion core）；准空穴除分数电荷外还具有莫尔诱导（moiré-induced）的八极结构（octupolar structure），层间隧穿各向异性（interlayer tunneling anisotropy）显著形变该结构而几乎不改变其所含电荷。

Conclusion: 研究确立了莫尔异质结构（moiré heterostructures）中稳健激子—任意子绑定的条件，并表明此类复合体可提供超越总电荷的分数激发性质信息，将任意子内部轮廓与电子量子几何（quantum geometry）相联系。

Abstract: Binding anyons to optically active excitons offers a route to manipulate fractional excitations and reveal the internal spatial structure of anyons. We establish the formation of anyon bound states between a mobile dipolar exciton and an $e/3$ quasihole in a twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$ fractional Chern insulator. Using exact diagonalization of a continuum-based interacting model, we identify a regime of meV-scale binding and strong exciton-quasihole entanglement controlled by exciton mobility and the distance between the exciton and the FCI. Spatial correlations resolve a localized depletion core carrying approximately $e/3$ charge around the exciton, providing a real-space characterization of the bound state. Beyond its fractional charge, the quasihole exhibits a moiré-induced octupolar structure. Interlayer tunneling anisotropy strongly deforms this structure while leaving the enclosed charge nearly unchanged, linking the internal profile of the anyon to the underlying electronic quantum geometry. These findings establish conditions for robust exciton-anyon binding in moiré heterostructures and show how these composites provide access to properties of fractional excitations beyond their total charge.

</details>


### [15] [Fractional Chern insulators and composite Fermi liquids vs. charge order in twisted MoTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22479)

### [22] [Anyonic Neural Network Wavefunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23815)

Result: 在 N=4 时与精确对角化结果高度一致；对中间统计（intermediate statistics）可准确计算至 N=16；对近费米子任意子（almost-fermionic anyons）可研究至 N=20，并与微扰结果（perturbative results）在 0.4% 偏差内吻合。基于该方法研究了谐振子阱（harmonic trap）中的任意子以及作为任意子绑定杂质模型的“任意子原子”（anyonic atoms），并考察单粒子密度（single-particle density）、对密度（pair density）、角动量（angular momentum）及多任意子聚合（coalesce）时波函数的标度行为随统计参数（statistics parameter）的变化。

Abstract: The macroscopic behavior of a non-interacting gas of Abelian anyons has remained unsettled for many decades. Prior numerical methods employing exact diagonalization in the continuum and on the plane have been limited to small system sizes, at most $N = 4$ anyons. Using the rapidly developing technology of neural network wavefunctions, we develop variational ansatze appropriate for studying continuum models of the Abelian anyon gas. The neural network architecture, which we call "AnyNet", is capable of exactly enforcing the non-analytic short-distance behavior induced by the anyonic statistics. We demonstrate excellent agreement with exact diagonalization for $N=4$ anyons, obtain accurate results up to $N = 16$ for intermediate statistics, and study up to $N=20$ for almost-fermionic anyons, matching perturbative results within a 0.4% discrepancy. We use this methodology to study anyons in a harmonic trap, and anyonic atoms as a model for anyon binding to impurities. We study the structure of anyonic wavefunctions as the statistics parameter is varied. We focus on the single-particle density, the pair density, the angular momentum, and the scaling of the wavefunction as multiple anyons coalesce. This work opens doors to understanding the macroscopic behavior of anyons, for which rich phases have been predicted theoretically.

</details>

Motivation: 该模型对应由交换作用与 DMI 耦合的铁磁层体系，作者意在弄清易轴各向异性 A、DMI 强度 D、磁场大小与取向角 δ 如何共同控制孤子晶格（soliton lattice）基态及其向公度态的转变。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The nature of superconductivity in nonsymmorphic In2Bi remains an open question, with ongoing debate over whether it exhibits conventional (BCS-like) or unconventional superconductivity. Here we report the physical characteristics and superconducting properties of polycrystalline In2Bi. Measurements of electrical resistivity, magnetization, penetration depth, and specific heat demonstrate the superconducting transition temperature about 6 K, with a sharp transition in resistivity (Delta_Tc = 0.1 K) and RRR = 57.32, reflecting the high quality of the sample. The upper critical field Hc2(0) obtained from resistivity and magnetization measurements are in good agreement with each other and are smaller than the Pauli limit, suggesting orbital limited mechanism. The coherence length = 35.43 nm, penetration depth = 101.9 nm and electron-phonon coupling constant = 1.05 suggest strongly coupled type-II superconductivity. For T<Tc, Delta_lambda(T) is best described by an s-wave BCS model and does not have any power law dependency with T, which is expected for a nodeless superconducting order parameter. The superfluid density reveals a fully gapped superconductivity with a gap of 2Delta_0/kBTc = 4.02. In the superconducting state, the electronic specific heat is best described by an s-wave BCS model with a gap of 2Delta_0/kBTc = 4.05. All these results from the present study indicate that In2Bi is a typical type-II,BCS-like, strongly coupled, and fully gapped superconductor in the dirty limit.

</details>

Abstract: $η$ pairing describes a staggered superconducting condensate whose order parameter changes sign between neighboring unit cells while maintaining a uniform magnitude. Nearly four decades after its theoretical proposal by C. N. Yang, a material platform for stabilizing $η$ pairing has remained elusive. Here, we reveal that altermagnetism, despite being pair breaking for conventional BCS pairing, naturally favors $η$-like pairing in V$_2$Se$_2$O-family altermagnets. Their valley-contrasting spin structure gives rise to oppositely spin-polarized, symmetry-related valleys centered at $X=(π,0)$ and $Y=(0,π)$, which form spin-singlet pairing partners separated by the commensurate momentum $\mathbf Q=(π,π)$, thereby favoring an $η$-like condensate. Self-consistent calculations demonstrate that the resulting $η$-like state remains robust over broad variations of chemical potential, hopping parameters, and Fermi-surface topology. To detect this hidden staggered order, we propose a phase-sensitive Josephson measurement with a uniform superconductor, where destructive interference suppresses single-Cooper-pair tunneling while preserving two-Cooper-pair processes. This constitutes a charge-$4e$-like Josephson response despite a bulk condensate composed of charge-$2e$ Cooper pairs, providing a direct phase-sensitive signature of the otherwise elusive $η$-like condensate in V$_2$Se$_2$O-family altermagnets.

</details>

Motivation: 研究动机在于：type 1.5超导性此前通常与多分量超导体（multicomponent superconductors）相联系，作者希望探究单分量体系能否在具有两个竞争超导迈斯纳态（Meissner states）时重现类似行为。

Conclusion: 结论是：类似type 1.5的超导性可在单分量体系中实现，无需多分量结构，其物理源于高阶势下两个竞争迈斯纳态的双重长度尺度竞争。

Abstract: We demonstrate the existence of type 1.5 like superconductivity in a single component Ginzburg-Landau model with a higher order potential. The model exhibits two competing superconducting Meissner states (MS), each of which has its own pair of length scales (coherence length and penetration depth). The solutions take the form of bags that contain vortices of one vacuum inside the other. This gives rise to four different types of vortex interaction, where one set of length scales determines vortex interactions inside the bags and the other set between the bags. Hence, within the bag the vortices can repel or attract and the bags themselves can repel or attract depending on the hierarchy of four length scales. When vortices repel within the bag, solutions act similarly to those in type 1.5 multicomponent superconductors, but in a single component system.

</details>

In Einstein gravity, we find an exact balance law relating the entropy on any two cuts of the null boundary to a signed flux, so the entropy decreases only when this flux is non-negative. We obtain a linearised physical process first law without assuming a background Killing symmetry, both in the presence and absence of external matter; and extend it for semiclassical gravity based on the improved quantum null energy condition. We also examine causal diamonds in maximally symmetric spacetimes and in Einstein-scalar gravity.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 多数结果已属已知，本文的价值在于证明一条单一、初等的代数路线即可统一导出全部结论，体现了该方法的教学与整合意义。

Abstract: This monograph classifies the geodesics of the Schwarzschild, Kerr and Schwarzschild-de Sitter black holes without solving for their turning points. In all three spacetimes, the radial and polar motions are governed by polynomials whose coefficients depend only on the constants of motion, and the qualitative behavior of a geodesic is fixed by the pattern of their roots. We read this pattern directly off the coefficients, using Vieta's formulae together with the discriminant, Descartes' rule of signs, and the values of the polynomials at the horizons and at infinity. In Schwarzschild spacetime, the reciprocals of the radial roots of every non-radial geodesic add up to $1/(2M)$, which gives the third root of a bound orbit in closed form and the separatrix $p=6+2e$. In Kerr spacetime, the signs of the coefficients show that no radially bound orbit has $\varepsilon^2\geq1$ and that vortical geodesics, timelike or null, have no radial turning point; a positive discriminant is necessary but not sufficient for bound motion, and Descartes' rule applied at the event horizon gives the exact criterion. Spherical orbits and their stability, the equatorial ISCO and light rings, and the photon shell follow from the same relations. In Schwarzschild-de Sitter spacetime, the timelike radial polynomial is a quintic with exactly one negative root, a bound branch requires $\varepsilon^2+ΛL^2/3<1$, and both marginally stable circular orbits lie beyond $6M$. Most of these results are known; the aim is to show that a single, elementary algebraic route leads to all of them. The text is meant for graduate students who have taken a first course in general relativity, and each chapter ends with worked examples.

</details>

</details>

Abstract: Extreme mass-ratio inspirals offer a powerful probe of massive black holes and their environments, but interpreting their signals requires accurate models of orbital dynamics and radiation. The phase contributions from gravitational-wave flux corrections and background-induced orbital shifts each exceed the scalar contribution in magnitude and partially cancel, highlighting the need for coupled matter-gravity modeling. But, in the end, the process of isotropization occurs.

Conclusion: $v_0(p_T)$ 的分析需考虑低 $p_T$ 探测阈值带来的偏差；经流体力学模拟给出的修正可显著改善实验间的一致性，提示此前部分表观差异可能源于该未修正的系统效应。

Abstract: We show that the analysis of $v_0(p_T)$ is biased when low-$p_T$ particles are not detected. The low-$p_T$ threshold shifts $v_0(p_T)$ by a constant negative value, which depends on the threshold. We evaluate the shift using hydrodynamic simulations. Correcting for this bias significantly improves agreement between ALICE and ATLAS results for $v_0(p_T)$ in Pb+Pb collisions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: γ=0时固体与流体方程在极限中解耦；γ∈(0,+∞]时宏观速度满足带记忆的Stokes系统。γ=∞时微观问题化为纯保守弹性动力学方程，核可经谱分解（spectral decomposition）显式算出，呈振荡、几乎周期（almost-periodic）且不衰减；γ∈(0,+∞)时微观弹性动力学方程耦合局部Stokes问题，黏性耗散使核在无穷远处衰减为零。

Conclusion: 参数γ刻画了从解耦到带记忆Stokes系统的转变，并决定记忆核是持续不衰减（γ=∞）还是渐近衰减（γ∈(0,+∞)）。

Abstract: This work deals with a fluid-structure interaction problem involving an incompressible viscous fluid and a periodic array of tiny elastic inclusions in a three-dimensional domain. We focus on a critical regime where the volume fraction of the inclusions tends to zero, but their density and stiffness are scaled so that they still influence the macroscopic behavior. The asymptotic regime depends on the parameter $γ=\lim_{\varepsilon\to 0}r_\varepsilon/\varepsilon^3\in [0,+\infty]$, where $\varepsilon$ is the period and $r_\varepsilon$ the inclusion size.
  For $γ=0$ the solid and fluid equations uncouple in the limit. For $γ\in (0,+\infty]$, we show that the macroscopic fluid velocity satisfies a Stokes system with memory: the standard Stokes equations are augmented by convolution-in-time terms arising from the elastic vibrations of the inclusions. The existence of the convolution kernels is established for both $γ\in(0,+\infty)$ and $γ=\infty$ using semigroup theory. For $γ=\infty$ the microscopic problem reduces to a purely conservative elastodynamic equation and the kernels can be computed explicitly via spectral decomposition. They are oscillatory and almost-periodic in time, yielding a persistent, non-decaying memory. For $γ\in(0,+\infty)$ the microscopic elastodynamic equation is coupled to a local Stokes problem, whose viscous dissipation yields memory kernels that decay to zero at infinity.

</details>


### [50] [Uniform restriction lower bounds and nongeodesic common nodal curves in integrable systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23836)
*Xianchao Wu,Xiao Xiao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 通过在双环面（two-torus）上构造一族实解析度量（real analytic metrics），使一条固定的嵌入非测地线圆成为无穷多个拉普拉斯特征函数（Laplace eigenfunctions）的节点分量（nodal component），从而给出对 Toth–Zelditch 与 Han 所提问题——无穷多节点集共有的曲线是否必为测地线（geodesic）——的否定回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文回应 Bourgain–Rudnick 提出的问题，并针对 Toth–Zelditch 与 Han 关于“被无穷多个节点集共有的曲线是否必须是测地线”的猜测给出反例，说明该问题的答案是否定的。

Method: 为解释限制性障碍（restriction obstruction），证明量子完全可积系统（quantum completely integrable, QCI）联合特征函数在切向特征分支（tangential characteristic branch）横截相交与孤立条件下的统一 L^2 下界，并推广到多分支（multi-branch）情形；据此刻画 Liouville 环面（Liouville tori）中可能的联合节点曲线以缩小候选范围。

Result: 构造出实解析度量族，使一条固定的嵌入非测地线圆成为无穷多个实拉普拉斯特征函数的节点分量；所证 L^2 下界刻画了 Liouville 环面中可能的联合节点曲线，且反例实现了消去（cancellation）恰发生在非测地线曲线上这一可能性。

Conclusion: 无穷多节点集共有的曲线不必为测地线，Toth–Zelditch 与 Han 的相关猜测被否定；QCI 系统的 L^2 下界为筛选可能的联合节点曲线提供了限制性判据。

Abstract: We construct a family of real analytic metrics on the two-torus, for which a fixed embedded nongeodesic circle is a nodal component of infinitely many real Laplace eigenfunctions. The examples address a question asked by Bourgain-Rudnick \cite{BR11}, and give a negative answer to the question asked by Toth-Zelditch \cite{TZ21} and Han \cite{Han}: whether a curve shared by infinitely many nodal sets must be geodesic.
  To explain the restriction obstruction, we prove uniform $L^2$ lower bounds for joint eigenfunctions of quantum completely integrable (QCI) systems under transverse crossing and isolation of a tangential characteristic branch, then extend to multi-branch setting. The results are used to characterize the possible joint nodal curves in the Liouville tori, narrowing down the choices. Finally, the counterexample realizes one possibility for which cancellation happens exactly on a nongeodesic curve.

</details>

### [51] [Emergent Discrimination and Polarization in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22701)
*Nestor Caticha,João Henrique Sant'Ana,Felippe Alves,Davi Bastos Costa,Mariana Mercucci,Juan P. Neirotti,Otavio Citton,Renato Vicente*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究用学习型智能体（learning agents）的局部贝叶斯更新模型，结合统计力学（statistical mechanics）序参量，揭示偏见强度与普遍程度如何驱动群体涌现出四类极化与歧视的集体状态（collective states）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在理论上解释：在由学习型个体组成的群体中，歧视（discrimination）与极化（polarization）如何从简单的局部学习动力学中自发涌现，并强调多智能体系统（multi-agent systems）需要群体层面的安全分析。

Method: 每个智能体用自适应线性决策层（adaptive linear decision layer）将议题的语言模型嵌入（language-model embedding）映射为二元观点，并维护对其他智能体的信任估计；通过成对交互，依据基于噪声信道师生学习（teacher-student learning over a noisy channel）推导的在线贝叶斯规则（online Bayesian rule）更新决策权重与信任。随后引入A/B两组任意标签，令比例为 f_b 的智能体按是否同标签对感知一致性施加强度为 b 的偏移以建模群体偏见，并用统计力学序参量连接局部动力学与集体态。

Result: 与大型语言模型（large language models）的上下文学习（in-context learning）实验定性复现了所预测的观点与信任更新。从均匀随机初值出发的仿真在 (b, f_b) 平面给出丰富相图（phase diagram），包含四种集体状态：自旋玻璃式受挫的外群体偏好（spin-glass frustrated out-group favoritism）、无歧视的极化（nondiscriminatory polarization）、伴随观点对齐的歧视、以及不伴随观点对齐的歧视；具体状态取决于偏见的强度与普遍程度。

Conclusion: 结果表明简单的局部学习动力学可产生性质上截然不同的极化与歧视形式，凸显多智能体系统群体层面安全分析的必要性；同时提示可用可测量的序参量（order parameters）刻画人类社会中的类似集体状态。

Abstract: We study theoretically how discrimination and polarization can emerge in a population of learning agents. Each agent uses an adaptive linear decision layer to map a language-model embedding of an issue to a binary opinion and maintains an evolving estimate of how much it trusts every other agent. Through pairwise interactions, agents update both their decision weights and trust estimates using an online Bayesian rule derived from teacher-student learning over a noisy channel. In-context-learning experiments with large language models qualitatively reproduce the predicted opinion and trust updates. We then assign each agent one of two arbitrary group labels, A or B, and model group bias by allowing a fraction $f_b$ of agents to shift its perceived agreement with each message by a strength $b$, a bias steering prejudice, depending on whether the sender and receiver share a label. We use order parameters from statistical mechanics to connect these local dynamics to collective states and transitions. Starting from uniformly random initial conditions, simulations reveal a rich phase diagram in the $(b,f_b)$ plane, comprising four collective states: spin-glass frustrated out-group favoritism, nondiscriminatory polarization, discrimination with opinion alignment, and discrimination without opinion alignment. Which state emerges depends on both the strength and prevalence of the bias. These results show how simple local learning dynamics can generate qualitatively distinct forms of polarization and discrimination, underscoring the need for population-level safety analysis of multi-agent systems. They also suggest measurable order parameters for characterizing analogous collective states in human societies.

</details>

*Shelby Klomp,Shafaq Gulzar Elahi,Scott Grudichak,Chethn Galla,Zhiyuan Wang,Mark Nguyen,George Winstone,Katarina Boskovic Guy,Emma Derrick,Maddox Wroblewski,Jacob Sprague,Andrew Laeuger,Jackson Larsen,Nancy Aggarwal,Shane L. Larson,Andrew A. Geraci*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作实现了将光刻制备的高纵横比、直径10 μm、厚400 nm的二氧化硅圆盘加载至真空中光驻波陷阱，并用干涉与成像方法测得圆盘质心运动谱，展示了少量粒子高效装载与晶圆级可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盘状粒子光阱被认定为高频引力波探测或高灵敏加速度计的潜在候选体系，因此需要可扩展、可定制几何与尺寸的制备与装载方法。

Method: 采用光刻（lithography）图案化的高纵横比二氧化硅圆盘，将其装入真空中光驻波陷阱（optical standing wave trap），并结合干涉测量（interferometry）与成像方法获取圆盘质心运动的运动谱（motional spectra）。

Result: 实现了高效率装载，仅需数个粒子量级即可完成光捕获，且所用几何与尺寸可定制并与晶圆级纳米制备（wafer scale nanofabrication）兼容；成功获得圆盘质心运动谱。

Conclusion: 该制备与装载方案为盘状粒子光阱体系提供了可扩展的实验平台，支持其作为高频引力波探测器或灵敏加速度计的应用前景。

Abstract: We describe the nanofabrication and loading of lithographically patterned, high aspect ratio $10$ $μ$m diameter silicon dioxide discs with thickness of $400$ nm into an optical standing wave trap in vacuum. We obtain motional spectra of the center of mass motion of the discs using a combination of interferometric and imaging methods. The method is capable of high-efficiency loading and optical trapping using small numbers of objects, of the order of a few particles, with customized geometries and sizes compatible with wafer scale nanofabrication methods. Disc-like particles in optical traps have been identified as promising candidates for detectors of high frequency gravitational waves or as sensitive accelerometers.

</details>


### [53] [Programming the Nonlinear Optics of a Nanoporous Photonic Metamaterial by Confined Crystallization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22801)
*Andrii Z. Maxym,Junhui Lang,Bouchta Sahraoui,Anatoliy S. Andrushchak,Yaroslav Shchur,Olha Kityk,Robert Wielgosz,Monika Lelonek,Piotr Pawlik,Patrick Huber,Jaroslaw Jedryka,Andriy V. Kityk*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过在多孔阳极氧化铝（anodic aluminum oxide, AAO）纳米通道中限域结晶苯偶酰（benzil, BZL），可将其转化为非线性光学超原子（meta-atom），并借由结晶路径编程其取向，从而在加工阶段"写入"有效二阶非线性极化率张量（effective susceptibility tensor）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由远小于光波长的纳米通道构成的膜可作为光子超材料（photonic metamaterial），其光学响应由结构而非组成材料决定；本文旨在利用这一架构特性，将每个通道转变为可编程的非线性光学超原子，并探究其取向关联与宏观各向异性的调控途径。

Method: 用非中心对称分子晶体（noncentrosymmetric molecular crystal）苯偶酰填充AAO，对比两种结晶路径：自支撑膜中每个通道独立固化、取向互不相关；以及从块体苯偶酰层引晶使超原子在亚毫米畴区（submillimeter domains）内取向一致。同时考察32–270 nm的通道直径范围以跨越亚波长（subwavelength）区间之外。

Result: 独立结晶时有效二次谐波产生（second-harmonic generation, SHG）响应近乎偏振各向同性，其幅度符合核-壳模型（core-shell model）：2.6 nm的光学非活性界面层削减了每个超原子的有效体积，且与烷基接枝二氧化硅上的既有结果一致，提示存在对主体不敏感的极限。引晶结晶则产生宏观各向异性的有效极化率，并使此处报道的反常定向SHG发射局限于超材料极限内。

Conclusion: 限域结晶（confined crystallization）为制备非线性光学超材料提供了一条途径，其有效极化率张量可在加工过程中被"书写"；反常定向SHG发射仅在亚波长超材料区制下出现，而通道尺寸的增大则标志着该区制的退出。

Abstract: A membrane traversed by a dense array of nanochannels acts as a photonic metamaterial: with channels far smaller than the wavelength of light, the optical response is set by the architecture rather than by the constituent materials. Here each nanochannel is turned into a nonlinear optical meta-atom by filling anodic aluminum oxide (AAO) with benzil (BZL), a noncentrosymmetric molecular crystal, and the crystallization pathway programs their orientation. In free-standing membranes every channel solidifies independently, the orientations remain uncorrelated, and the effective second-harmonic generation (SHG) response is nearly polarization isotropic; its magnitude follows a core - shell model in which an optically inactive interfacial layer of 2.6 nm reduces the active volume of each meta-atom, reproducing on bare alumina the value previously found on alkyl-grafted silica and indicating a host-insensitive limit. Seeding crystallization from a bulk benzil layer instead aligns the meta-atoms over submillimeter domains and produces a macroscopically anisotropic effective susceptibility. Channel diameters from 32 to 270 nm span the transition out of the subwavelength regime, and the anomalous directional SHG emission reported here is confined to the metamaterial limit. Confined crystallization thus offers a route to nonlinear optical metamaterials whose effective susceptibility tensor is written during processing.

</details>

### [54] [Streamwise dispersion with interphase mass transfer through a tube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22963)
*Mingyang Guan,Zhan Wang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该工作在惰性溶液流向弥散理论（streamwise dispersion theory）基础上，构建了一阶不可逆表面反应活性溶质的解析弥散模型，并给出任意反应活性下的局部矩、浓度与输运系数的显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪切流中活性溶质输运在化工、液固色谱与环境力学中广泛存在，但已有理论多针对惰性溶质；反应边界破坏质量守恒并耦合流向与径向导数，使已有结果无法通过简单扰动直接推广。

Method: 以 Guan & Chen (2024, J. Fluid Mech.) 的流向弥散理论为框架，引入一阶不可逆表面反应边界条件，将微观反应动力学与径向本征模（radial eigenmodes）结合，定义新的（有效）局部交换速率，并推导局部矩、浓度及唯象输运系数的显式解析解，辅以小/大 Damköhler 数渐近分析。

Result: 解析解在全部区间与数值模拟定量一致；在管式 Poiseuille 流（Poiseuille flow）中揭示变号径向交换、反应增强漂移、反应削弱弥散性（dispersivity）及非高斯浓度剖面等预渐近行为；Damköhler 数（Damköhler number）渐近给出反应受限与扩散受限区间，并将保守情形与无限反应活性的 Dirichlet 极限相衔接。

Conclusion: 流向视角为化学活性系统的宏观输运提供了统一解析描述，深化了对反应-剪切耦合机制的理解，并可能推广至更广泛的反应流动体系。

Abstract: Reactive solute transport in shear flows is of broad importance in chemical engineering, liquid--solid chromatography, and environmental mechanics, particularly in flow reactors and fluidic devices involving interphase mass transfer. Based on the recently developed streamwise dispersion theory for inert solutions (Guan & Chen, 2024, J. Fluid Mech., vol. 980, A33), we present an analytical model for reactive dispersion of chemically active solutes characterized by first-order, irreversible surface reactions. The extension is not a simple perturbation because the reactive boundary destroys mass conservation with streamwise and radial derivatives coupled. The model establishes how the microscopic kinetic reactivity and radial eigenmodes combine to determine a newly defined (effective) local exchange rate. We derive rigorous explicit solutions for the local moments, concentration, and phenomenological transport coefficients at arbitrary reactivity, in quantitative agreement with numerical simulations across all regimes. For tube Poiseuille flows, we reveal pre-asymptotic behaviours including sign-changing radial exchange, reactivity-enhanced drift, reactivity-reduced dispersivity, and non-Gaussian concentration profiles. Small- and large-Damköhler-number asymptotics for the local and global transport coefficients identify the reaction-limited and diffusion-limited regimes, connecting the conservative case to the Dirichlet limit at infinite reactivity. This streamwise perspective could advance our understanding of macroscale transport in chemically active systems.

</details>


### [55] [A GENERIC-Informed Neural Network for Generative Rheology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23352)
*Imanol García de Beristain,David Nieto Simavilla,Marco Ellero*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: GINNGER 将本构方程发现重构为对热力学生成元的学习，并嵌入 GENERIC 形式体系与谱表示，从而由微观结构流动数据中直接获得满足热力学定律、跨变形协议可泛化的本构模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接学习本构算子（constitutive operator），既需先验给定唯象本构方程，又难以保证热力学一致性，且在不同流动运动学下泛化能力有限。

Method: 将 GENERIC 形式体系（GENERIC formalism）嵌入学习过程，把本构发现转化为对底层热力学生成元（thermodynamic generators）的学习；并在聚合物构象张量（polymer conformation tensor）的本征特征空间中给出谱形式（spectral formulation），构造与运动学无关的热力学状态空间，使剪切、拉伸与混合流动数据共同服务于单一模型的发现。

Result: 基于高保真模拟的验证表明，该框架能准确重构本构行为，并在先前未见几何中预测复杂流场。

Conclusion: GINNGER 以构造方式保证热力学一致性、材料客观性（material objectivity）与物理可解释性，同时降低发现问题的复杂度，为复杂流体数据驱动建模提供了统一的新范式。

Abstract: We present GINNGER (GENERIC-Informed Neural Network for GEnerative Rheology), a framework for discovering thermodynamically admissible constitutive equations from microstructural flow data. Unlike existing approaches that learn constitutive operators, GINNGER reformulates constitutive discovery as the learning of the underlying thermodynamic generators from which constitutive equations emerge. Embedding the GENERIC formalism into the learning process restricts the search to constitutive models that satisfy the first and second laws of thermodynamics by construction, eliminating the need to prescribe phenomenological constitutive equations a priori. A second conceptual advance is a spectral formulation of constitutive discovery in the intrinsic eigenspace of the polymer conformation tensor. This provides a kinematics-independent thermodynamic state space in which the thermodynamic generators depend only on the instantaneous microstructural state, while flow kinematics acts solely as an external driver transporting the system through this space. Consequently, data from shear, extensional, and mixed flows contribute to the discovery of a single constitutive model that generalizes across deformation protocols and flow geometries. By learning thermodynamic generators rather than high-dimensional constitutive operators, GINNGER substantially reduces the complexity of the discovery problem while preserving thermodynamic consistency, material objectivity, and physical interpretability by construction. The framework is validated using high-fidelity simulations, accurately reconstructing constitutive behavior and predicting complex flow fields across previously unseen geometries. GINNGER establishes a new paradigm for constitutive discovery by combining generative, spectral, and thermodynamically grounded learning within a unified framework for data-driven modeling of complex fluids.

</details>

### [56] [MIXSim: A multiclass first order traffic model with composition-dependent dynamics for mixed-autonomy urban networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23714)
*Muhammad Haris,Claudio Roncoli*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出MIXSim，一种面向信号化城市网络混合交通的多类一阶宏观交通模型，将人工驾驶车辆（HDV）与网联自动驾驶车辆（CAV）作为独立类别建模，用于评估不同CAV渗透水平与信号控制策略下的网络性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合自动驾驶（mixed-autonomy）环境下的城市信号网络需要兼顾车辆类别构成与信号约束的宏观建模工具，而现有模型难以在保留起讫点（origin-destination）信息的同时刻画类别相关驾驶行为对交通流参数的影响。

Method: 模型将人工驾驶车辆（human driven vehicles, HDVs）与网联自动驾驶车辆（connected and automated vehicles, CAVs）作为独立类别，并通过货品化状态（commodity specific states）保留起讫点信息。局部车辆类别构成与类别特定驾驶行为共同决定依赖于构成的三角基本图（composition-dependent triangular fundamental diagram）参数；在信号交叉口，交通流向需求构成决定饱和流率（saturation flow rate），实际流向流量受需求、有效绿灯时间与下游接收能力约束。

Result: 数值实验考察了不同CAV渗透率、CAV时间间隔（time gap）及备选信号控制策略的影响，结果表明在测试条件下CAV渗透率与时间间隔的变化会改变网络性能，并验证了MIXSim用于比较不同混合交通状况下信号控制策略的能力。

Conclusion: MIXSim可作为分析混合自动驾驶城市信号网络性能与信号控制策略的宏观仿真框架。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel multiclass first order macroscopic traffic model for mixed-autonomy in signalized urban networks, called MIXSim. Human driven vehicles (HDVs) and Connected and Automated Vehicles (CAVs) are represented as separate classes while origin-destination information is retained through commodity specific states. The local vehicle class composition and class specific driving behaviour characteristics determine the parameters of a composition-dependent triangular fundamental diagram. At signalized intersections, the composition of movement demand additionally determines the saturation flow rate, while the realized movement flow is constrained by demand, effective green time, and downstream receiving capacity. Numerical experiments investigate different CAV penetration levels and CAV time gaps, together with alternative signal control strategies. The numerical experiments illustrate how changes in CAV penetration and CAV time gap affect network performance under the tested conditions, and demonstrate the use of MIXSim for comparing signal control strategies across different mixed traffic regimes.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 提出一种基于 Transformer 的变分自回归网络（variational autoregressive network），用于对连续自由度的格点系统（lattice systems）进行采样，并在二维 XY 模型（two-dimensional XY model）的 BKT 相变（Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transition）附近进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经采样器（neural samplers）在连续自由度格点系统上的表现不足，需要更强的架构来高效采样此类系统。

Method: 将 Transformer 架构与物理启发要素（physics-informed ingredients）结合，引入局部性感知注意力（locality-informed attention）与近似概率（approximate probabilities）；训练规模可达 96×96 格点，并用模式丢弃估计器（mode-dropping estimator）检验配置空间覆盖情况。

Conclusion: 基于 Transformer 的变分自回归网络结合物理启发设计，可作为连续自由度格点系统（如 BKT 相变附近二维 XY 模型）的有效采样方法，并达到与传统 MCMC 一致的结果。

Abstract: We present a transformer-based variational autoregressive network for sampling lattice systems with continuous degrees of freedom and apply it to the two-dimensional XY model near the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transition. The architecture is combined with physics-informed ingredients: locality-informed attention and approximate probabilities. The resulting sampler significantly outperforms existing neural samplers. We train the model on lattices up to $96\times96$, verify with a mode-dropping estimator that no parts of the configuration space are missed, and reproduce the energy, magnetization and helicity modulus obtained with the traditional Markov Chain Monte Carlo algorithm.

</details>

### [58] [Analytic and numerical study of dark matter halos in the Horndeski theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22819)
*Kohei Onoda,Shun Arai,Daisuke Yamauchi,Shuichiro Yokoyama,Shogo Masaki,Kenji Hasegawa*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 在Horndeski理论中，暗物质晕的Vainshtein屏蔽（Vainshtein screening）在晕中心趋近核心状结构时会失效，第五力（fifth force）特征可在晕动力学演化中留存；通过N体模拟与多种质量代理（mass proxy）比较，可在晕尺度上检验引力模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修正引力（modified gravity）模型需通过局域引力检验，通常借助屏蔽机制（screening mechanism）在恒星尺度压制第五力，但延展的暗物质晕可能在小尺度上呈现屏蔽转变，从而成为探测修正引力与晚期加速膨胀起源的探针。

Method: 假设冷暗物质（cold dark matter）与球对称晕，解析厘清Vainshtein屏蔽有效运作所需的晕密度剖面条件；并对孤立晕开展N体模拟（N-body simulation），采用牛顿两体相互作用加球对称外部第五力，比较流体静力学（hydrostatic dynamics）、Jeans方程与引力透镜（gravitational lensing）三种质量代理。

Result: 解析结果表明，即使位于Vainshtein半径（Vainshtein radius）以内，当剖面在晕中心附近趋近核心状结构时屏蔽效果减弱。数值模拟显示，在（准）平衡态下三种质量代理彼此存在偏差，定量表明基准引力模型并非牛顿引力。

Conclusion: 研究肯定地表明，来自观测的多种质量代理能够用于在晕尺度上检验引力；第五力效应可能在晕动力学演化过程中留存。

Abstract: The models of modified gravity that can explain the late-time accelerated expansion of the Universe must pass the local tests of gravity, introducing a screening mechanism so that any additional fifth force in gravity is suppressed at stellar scales. Dark matter halos, in contrast, by their extendedness in scale, may exhibit a transition of screening to small scales, providing a probe of modified gravity and the origin of the late-time acceleration. We perform comprehensive studies of dark matter halos with Vainshtein screening in the Horndeski theory. Given that dark matter is cold and a dark matter halo is spherically symmetric, we analytically clarify the conditions for the halo profile under which the Vainshtein screening works effectively. We find that the Vainshtein screening is less effective even within the Vainshtein radius as the profile approaches a core-like structure around the center of a halo, which motivates us to postulate that some features owing to the fifth force can survive during the dynamical evolution of a halo. We numerically examine our postulate by a $N$-body simulation for an isolated halo with the assumption of Newtonian two-body interactions with the spherical external fifth force. We make use of three proxies of the mass through hydrostatic dynamics, the Jeans equation, and gravitational lensing, all of which must be identical in the case of Newtonian gravity as a weak-field approximation to General Relativity. We find that these proxies can be biased relative to each other at a (quasi) equilibrium, quantitatively indicating that the fiducial model of gravity is not Newtonian. Our result affirmatively suggests that multiple mass proxies from observations are capable of testing gravity on halo scales.

</details>


### [59] [Can coupled thawing quintessence reproduce DESI DR2? A test with two potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23497)
*Jhananii Yuvaraj Babu,Luca Amendola*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文检验耦合精质（coupled quintessence）机制能否重现 DESI DR2 所偏好的暗能量状态方程（equation of state）在过去的幽灵跨越（phantom crossing），结论为数据支持但尚未要求该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI DR2 偏好状态方程在过去穿越 w=-1 的演化暗能量。耦合精质可在无幽灵场（phantom field）的情况下产生表观幽灵跨越：在假设暗扇区（dark sector）分别守恒时，其有效状态方程可低于 -1。论文旨在检验该机制能否复现 DESI 结果。

Method: 采用两个融化型势（thawing potentials）——余弦势与双指数山顶势（double-exponential hilltop），与 DESI DR2 BAO、Planck PR4 及三组超新星（supernova）汇编数据联合拟合，并与 ΛCDM 和 w0waCDM 对比。

Result: 两模型较 ΛCDM 改善 Δχ² = 5–15，达到 w0waCDM 改善量的 64%–92%；在 z≲0.4 处追随 w0waCDM 状态方程，但幽灵跨越发生更晚（z≃1，而非 z≃0.4–0.5）。耦合受限 |β|<0.073（95% 置信度），两势在耦合上一致至 0.15σ 以内；在可达状态方程上存在差异，余弦势更接近 DESI 偏好的演化并被拟合弱偏好。耦合后验呈双峰（β≃±0.05），仅负模产生跨越，且在各分析中均为较高模。

Conclusion: 数据支持耦合精质的幽灵跨越机制，但尚未要求其存在。

Abstract: DESI DR2 favours dark energy whose equation of state evolves crossing $w=-1$ in the past. Coupled quintessence can produce such an apparent phantom crossing without a phantom field, since a coupled dark sector analysed under the assumption of separate conservation yields an effective equation of state below $-1$. We test whether this mechanism can reproduce the DESI result, using two thawing potentials, a cosine and a double-exponential hilltop, confronted with DESI DR2 BAO, Planck PR4 and three supernova compilations. Both models improve on $Λ$CDM by $Δχ^2 = 5$--$15$, recovering $64$--$92\%$ of the improvement achieved by $w_0w_a$CDM. They track the $w_0w_a$CDM equation of state at $z\lesssim0.4$ but cross the phantom divide later, near $z\simeq1$ rather than $z\simeq0.4$--$0.5$. The coupling is bounded at $|β|<0.073$ ($95\%$), and the two potentials agree on it to within $0.15σ$. They differ instead in the equation of state they can reach: the cosine comes closer to the evolution preferred by DESI and is weakly preferred by the fit. The coupling posterior is bimodal, with modes at $β\simeq\pm0.05$; only the negative mode produces the crossing, and it is the higher of the two in every analysis. In conclusion, the data favour the coupled quintessence phantom crossing but do not yet require it.

</details>


### [60] [Clustering of JWST LRDs. Implications for PBHs and other early-forming seeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23806)
*Gert Hütsi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 利用JWST小红点（little red dots, LRDs）在z=4–8的丰度与空间成团性，检验"暗物质晕内成团形成"与"原初黑洞（primordial black holes, PBHs）播种"两种形成情景，结果强烈支持标准晕形成图像、不支持PBH随机播种。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST发现的LRDs起源不明，需判别其究竟形成于维里化的暗物质晕（dark-matter halos）中，还是由动力学上占比极小的原初黑洞（PBHs）等早期种子所播种，两类情景对可观测量（丰度与成团）的预言不同。

Method: 以z=4–8的LRD观测丰度与空间成团性（spatial clustering）为约束，比较标准晕形成情景与PBH播种情景；并对PBH的泊松等曲率（Poisson isocurvature）成分做功率谱（power-spectrum）分析以预言成团信号。

Result: 晕形成情景无需精细调节即可自然解释两项观测量；PBH情景需极小的PBH质量分数才能匹配丰度，且其泊松等曲率成分预言的成团信号远低于观测值。该结论对任何动力学上占比很小的早期形成种子同样成立，除非其初始成团被精细调节或活动与大质量宿主晕绑定。

Conclusion: LRD的空间成团性强烈支持标准晕形成图像，不支持PBHs或类似早期种子作为随机激活的LRD族群；仅当PBH活动由大质量宿主晕主导时仍可行，但此时单靠成团性无法将其与天体物理种子区分。

Abstract: We use the observed abundance and spatial clustering of JWST little red dots (LRDs) over z = 4--8 to confront two formation scenarios: the standard picture in which LRDs form in virialized dark-matter halos, and one in which they are seeded by a dynamically subdominant population of primordial black holes (PBHs). The halo formation scenario accounts for both observables naturally and without fine-tuning. In the PBH scenario, matching the observed LRD abundance requires a negligibly small PBH mass fraction, and a power-spectrum analysis shows that such a Poisson isocurvature component predicts a clustering signal far below what is observed. The same conclusion holds for any population of dynamically subdominant early-forming seeds, unless their initial clustering is finely tuned or their activity is tied to massive host halos. We conclude that the spatial clustering of JWST LRDs strongly favours the standard halo formation picture and disfavours PBHs, or similar early-forming seeds, as a randomly activated LRD population; PBHs remain viable as seeds only if their activity is governed by massive host halos, in which case clustering alone cannot distinguish them from astrophysical seeds.

</details>

### [61] [Universal Threshold Laws for Dipolar Evaporation of Molecular Fermi Gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22639)
*Kevin B. Xu,John L. Bohn,Reuben R. W. Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究指出，屏蔽型单组分超冷偶极分子费米气体在Wigner阈值区（Wigner threshold regime）的再热化抑制由有效维度决定的普适阈值定律控制：3D中随温度平方标度，2D中仅线性标度，故2D热化-损失比更有利，更适合达到深度简并，并获KRb分子实验支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸发冷却（evaporative cooling）中费米统计（Fermi statistics）抑制弹性碰撞，造成制备量子简并气体（quantum degenerate gases）的低温瓶颈，需要寻找规避该瓶颈的途径。

Method: 针对屏蔽型单组分超冷偶极分子费米气体在Wigner阈值区（Wigner threshold regime）的弹性碰撞与损失过程，结合由气体有效维度（effective dimensionality）固定的普适阈值定律进行理论分析，并开展从头算（ab initio）密耦（close-coupling）计算。

Result: 再热化的费米抑制在3D中随温度二次标度、在2D中仅线性标度，使2D的热化-损失比（thermalization-to-loss ratio）优于3D；密耦计算结果与KRb分子费米简并气体实验测量吻合良好。

Conclusion: 费米简并瓶颈的严重程度由有效维度决定，2D几何（2D geometries）更适合达到深度简并（deep degeneracy），且结论得到KRb分子实验证据支持。

Abstract: Fermi statistics suppress the elastic collisions that rethermalize a Fermi gas during evaporative cooling, creating a fundamental low-temperature bottleneck for producing quantum degenerate gases. We show that, for shielded single-component ultracold dipolar molecular Fermi gases in the Wigner threshold regime, this bottleneck is governed by universal threshold laws fixed by the effective dimensionality of the gas. In this regime, Fermi suppression of rethermalization scales quadratically with temperature in 3D but only linearly in 2D. Consequently, the thermalization-to-loss ratio is more favorable in 2D than in 3D, and these results suggest that 2D geometries are better suited for reaching deep degeneracy. We also present ab initio close-coupling calculations that agree well with experimental measurements on Fermi degenerate gases of KRb molecules, supporting our conclusions.

</details>


### [62] [Universal Long-distance Tail of Particle-number Density near a Wall in Superfluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23165)
*Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文用半空间超流有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）证明，零温下不可渗透壁附近的声子量子涨落导致粒子数密度呈 z^{-(d+1)} 尾部，其系数由体态方程（equation of state）决定而与壁的微观细节无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究伽利略不变超流体（Galilean-invariant superfluid）中静态壁面对密度分布的普适长程修正，并判断该效应是否独立于壁面微观性质。

Method: 在半空间上构建超流有效场论，计算量子声子（phonon）涨落对光滑密度剖面的贡献；温度效应以热长度（thermal length）c_s/T 作为尺度分界，并对三维 BCS–BEC 渡越（crossover）用实验声速数据估算归一化尾部振幅。

Result: 零温密度尾部为 z^{-(d+1)}，振幅系数由体态方程固定；低 T 下，中间距离保持零温行为，更远处在 d>1 时为热尾部 Tz^{-d}；d=1 时声子贡献按 exp(-4πTz/c_s) 衰减。

Conclusion: 该有效场论同时适用于玻色与费米超流，包括幺正极限（unitarity）的强相互作用费米气体，给出壁附近密度尾部的普适刻画并可给出三维定量振幅估计。

Abstract: We investigate the long-distance recovery of the particle-number density near a static, impermeable wall in a $d$-dimensional Galilean-invariant superfluid. Using superfluid effective field theory (EFT) on a half-space, we show that quantum phonon fluctuations produce a $z^{-(d+1)}$ tail in the smooth density profile at zero temperature, where $z$ is the distance from the wall. The tail coefficient is fixed by the bulk equation of state and is independent of microscopic wall details. At low temperature $T$, the thermal length $c_s/T$, with $c_s$ the bulk sound speed, separates the zero-temperature behavior at intermediate distances from a thermal $Tz^{-d}$ tail at larger distances for $d>1$. For $d=1$, the long-distance phonon contribution instead decays as $\exp(-4πTz/c_s)$. The EFT applies to both Bose and Fermi superfluids, including strongly interacting Fermi gases at unitarity. We estimate the normalized tail amplitude in three dimensions across the BCS--BEC crossover using experimental sound-speed data.

</details>


### [63] [Interfacial Faraday instability driven by interspecies-interaction modulation in a phase-separated binary Bose-Einstein condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23314)
*Yating Huang,Yan Li*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 通过周期性调制种间散射长度激发准二维相分离双组分玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的界面法拉第不稳定性，结合界面毛细模型与Floquet分析预测不稳定舌，并用GP模拟观测到三至八重对称的星形界面图案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强不混溶区种间相互作用能集中于内界面时，能否通过调制种间散射长度将驱动作用于界面张力与毛细回复力，从而可控地激发界面毛细模式。

Method: 采用锐界面毛细模型结合Floquet分析，量化一阶张力响应对数敏感度并计入有限调制幅度下的非线性张力变化，同时以Gross-Pitaevskii模拟验证。

Result: Floquet分析给出离散方位角模式频率及主不稳定舌，且模式阶数越高不稳定舌越向高频移动；GP模拟揭示三至八重旋转对称的星形界面图案，八重模式呈次谐波振荡并在小变形区间近似指数增长。

Conclusion: 种间相互作用调控为界面毛细模式的频率选择性激发提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We investigate interfacial Faraday instabilities in a quasi-two-dimensional, phase-separated binary Bose-Einstein condensate driven by periodic modulation of the interspecies scattering length. In the strongly immiscible regime, the interspecies interaction energy is concentrated near the internal interface, linking the modulation to interfacial tension and the capillary restoring force. We quantify the first-order tension response through its logarithmic sensitivity and account for nonlinear tension variations at finite modulation amplitudes. A sharp-interface capillary model and Floquet analysis yield discrete azimuthal-mode frequencies and primary instability tongues that shift toward higher driving frequencies with increasing mode order. Gross-Pitaevskii simulations reveal star-shaped interface patterns with threefold to eightfold rotational symmetry. The eightfold mode exhibits subharmonic oscillations and approximately exponential growth of its period-averaged amplitude over a small-deformation interval. These results provide a route to frequency-selective excitation of interfacial capillary modes through interspecies-interaction control.

</details>


### [64] [Statistics-controlled topological phases in a Su-Schrieffer-Heeger ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23366)
*Rajashri Parida,Biswajit Paul,Smitha Vishveshwara,Diptiman Sen,Tapan Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作在双腿 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）梯子（two-leg SSH ladder）中研究粒子统计（particle statistics）对拓扑相变（topological phase transition）的影响，发现硬核玻色子（hard-core bosons）出现重入拓扑相变（reentrant topological phase transition），而无自旋费米子（spinless fermions）无此现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维格点中自由费米子与硬核玻色子具有统计等价性，但该等价性在高于一维时通常失效；本文旨在考察这种差异如何影响拓扑相变行为。

Method: 从两条具有相反跃迁二聚化（staggered dimerization）的退耦 SSH 链出发，引入腿间耦合（inter-leg coupling），比较无自旋费米子与硬核玻色子的相变边界。

Result: 拓扑—平庸相变边界强烈依赖粒子统计：硬核玻色子情形下，平庸相被夹在两类相反拓扑相之间，形成重入拓扑相变；无自旋费米子情形下该重入现象缺失，拓扑相之间由无能隙线（gapless line）分隔而非插入平庸相。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了粒子统计与拓扑特征之间的相互作用，并在实验可实现的装置中展示了二者导致的本质差异。

Abstract: In strictly one-dimensional lattices, free fermions and hard-core bosons exhibit equivalent physical properties in many-particle systems. However, this statistical equivalence generally breaks down upon moving to higher dimensions. Our work investigates the significant effects of this difference on the topological phase transition in a minimal system lying away from one dimension: the two-leg Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) ladder. Starting in the limit of two decoupled SSH chains having opposite hopping dimerization patterns (staggered dimerization), we demonstrate that introducing inter-leg coupling drives a transition from a topological to a trivial phase for both spinless fermions and hard-core bosons. However, the topological to trivial phase transition boundary is strongly sensitive to the underlying particle statistics. We find that this intriguing effect favors a trivial phase sandwiched between the two topological phases with opposite characters for the case of hard-core bosons, resulting in a reentrant topological phase transition. In contrast, this reentrant phenomenon is absent for spinless fermions, with the topological phases being separated by a gapless line instead of an intervening trivial phase. This study demonstrates the interplay between particle statistics and topological features, revealing fundamentally different properties in an experimentally accessible set-up.

</details>


### [65] [Finite-temperature phase diagram of dipolar bosons in a cylindrical setup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23687)
*Juan Sánchez-Baena,Raúl Bombín,Jordi Boronat*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 采用路径积分蒙特卡罗方法（path integral Monte Carlo, PIMC）研究无限长圆柱几何中偶极玻色子在有限温度下的相图，发现存在温度先破坏空间序、后破坏超流性的参数区域，且超流分数随温度呈非单调变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温度在偶极体系（dipolar systems）中可诱导或破坏空间密度调制，热效应与量子关联（quantum correlations）之间的相互作用尚需澄清；既有描述多依赖博戈留博夫理论（Bogoliubov theory），需推广至更高温度与热力学极限。

Method: 在无限长圆柱几何中，对固定总密度改变相互作用散射长度（scattering length）与温度，使用路径积分蒙特卡罗方法（PIMC）计算相图；该方法在所有阶上计入热效应与量子关联，并可定量估计体系的超流分数（superfluid fraction）。

Result: 刻画了各出现相的结构与超流性；发现一个参数区域中，体系自超固相（supersolid）出发，空间序随温度升高先于超流性消失；另有一区域超流分数随温度呈非单调（non-monotonous）依赖。

Conclusion: 结果将基于博戈留博夫理论的既有描述推广至热力学极限下的更高温度，并说明空间调制（spatial modulations）与超流性随温度升高如何逐步丧失。

Abstract: Temperature plays a non-trivial role in dipolar systems, being able to either induce or destroy spatial density modulations. In order to shed light into the interplay between thermal effects and quantum correlations, we employ the path integral Monte Carlo method (PIMC) to obtain the phase diagram of a system of dipolar bosons in an infinite cylindrical geometry and at finite temperatures. For a fixed total density, we vary the scattering length of the interaction and the temperature, and characterize the structure and superfluidity of the emerging phases. The PIMC method accounts for thermal effects and quantum correlations at all orders, and can be used to quantitatively estimate the superfluid fraction of the system. Remarkably, we find a regime of parameters where, starting from a supersolid, the spatial order vanishes with temperature before the superfluidity does. We also find a region of the phase diagram where the superfluid fraction displays a non-monotonous dependence with temperature. Our results extend previous descriptions, relying on Bogoliubov theory, to larger temperatures in the thermodynamic limit and illustrate how spatial modulations and superfluidity are lost as temperature is increased.

</details>
