### [66] [Rapid mixing within a phase for Ising models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23777)
*Vasyl Paliy,Youngtak Sohn*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文针对条件化（conditioned）Ising 测度的 Glauber 动力学，在稀疏谱宽条件下从磁化漂移条件导出 Poincaré 不等式与修正对数 Sobolev 不等式，并应用于同步问题的后验采样、随机正则图铁磁 Ising 模型的快速相序及 SK 模型混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的是在相互作用矩阵满足稀疏谱宽条件时，为具有大比例 +1 自旋的 Ising 条件测度建立函数不等式（Poincaré 与对数 Sobolev），从而刻画 Glauber 动力学的收敛与混合性质。

Method: 方法基于磁化漂移条件（magnetization drift condition）推导条件测度的 Poincaré 不等式（Poincaré inequality），并由更强的熵加权条件（entropy weighted condition）得到修正对数 Sobolev 不等式（modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality）；随后将不等式扩展到条件区域外满足内向漂移（inward drift condition）的无条件测度。

Result: 所得结果包括：在高信噪比下以谱初始化出发，用 O(n log n) 次 Glauber 更新实现 Z_2 同步（Z_2 synchronization）的近似后验采样（total variation 距离）；对随机 d-正则图上的铁磁 Ising 模型证明逆温度低至 1/d 量级的快速相序（rapid phase ordering），扩展了 Gheissari、Sly 与 Sohn (2025) 的温度范围；并得到外场足够大时 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick 模型在任意固定逆温度下 Glauber 动力学 O(n log n) 混合。

Conclusion: 条件化 Ising 测度的漂移条件可作为导出函数不等式的有效工具，并统一地给出同步采样、随机正则图相序与 SK 模型混合的复杂度保证；无条件情形的扩展依赖条件区域外的内向漂移条件。

Abstract: We study Glauber dynamics for Ising measures conditioned to have a sufficiently large majority of $+1$ spins. Under a sparse spectral width condition on the interaction matrix, we derive a Poincaré inequality for the conditioned measures from a magnetization drift condition, and a modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality from a stronger, entropy weighted condition. As applications, we obtain approximate posterior sampling in total variation distance for $\mathbb{Z}_2$ synchronization at high enough signal-to-noise ratio using $O(n\log n)$ Glauber updates from a spectral initialization. For ferromagnetic Ising models on random $d$-regular graphs, we prove rapid phase ordering down to inverse temperatures of order $1/d$, extending the temperature range of Gheissari, Sly, and Sohn (2025). Finally, we extend these inequalities to unconditioned Ising measures under an inward drift condition outside the conditioned region. This yields $O(n\log n)$ mixing of Glauber dynamics for the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model at every fixed inverse temperature when the external field is sufficiently large.

</details>


### [67] [A Universal Lower Bound for the Complexity of Gaussian-Divisible Spin Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23823)
*Pax Kivimae,Benjamin McKenna*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 在球面混合自旋玻璃模型（spherical mixed spin glass model）中，将标准高斯相互作用系数替换为可高斯分解（Gaussian-divisible）的非高斯系数，证明当非高斯部分中心化且次指数时，退火复杂度（annealed complexity）不低于标准高斯模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非高斯无序（non-Gaussian disorder）如何影响球面混合自旋玻璃模型的复杂度，扩展经典的纯高斯相互作用设定。

Method: 把每个相互作用系数写成标准高斯随机变量与独立非高斯随机变量之和（即高斯可分解系数），并在非高斯部分中心化（centered）且次指数（subexponential）的条件下分析退火复杂度。

Result: 该模型的退火复杂度至少等于标准高斯模型的退火复杂度，即非高斯扰动不会降低退火复杂度。

Conclusion: 在中心化次指数非高斯扰动下，标准高斯模型的退火复杂度构成下界，说明该量对这类非高斯无序具有一定的稳健性。

Abstract: We consider a variant of the spherical mixed spin glass model with non-Gaussian disorder. Specifically, we replace the usual standard Gaussian interaction coefficients with Gaussian-divisible ones, meaning that each coefficient can be expressed as the sum of a standard Gaussian random variable and an independent, non-Gaussian random variable. We show that the annealed complexity of this model is at least the annealed complexity of the standard Gaussian model, if the non-Gaussian part is centered and subexponential.

</details>


### [68] [Analyticity of the correlation length in subcritical FK-percolation and Potts models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23843)
*Lucas D'Alimonte,Loïc Gassmann*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明FK渗流（FK-percolation）与Potts模型在Z^d上整个亚临界参数区域内的关联长度（correlation length）关于参数解析，并由此推出亚临界相中关联长度只可能在可数个温度值处具有旋转不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联长度是刻画亚临界相的核心量，但其解析性此前未被完整建立；该工作旨在填补这一空白，并借此研究关联长度的旋转不变性这一几何性质。

Method: 利用Campanino、Ioffe与Velenik在Ornstein–Zernike理论中引入的做法，将质量（mass）的极对偶（polar dual）等同于涉及FK–Ruelle算子（FK–Ruelle operator）主特征值（leading eigenvalue）的方程的解，并关键依赖Kato的线性算子解析扰动理论（analytic perturbation of linear operators）。

Result: 证明了FK渗流与Potts模型的关联长度在整个亚临界参数区域（subcritical regime）内解析；作为推论，亚临界相中关联长度除可数个温度参数值外不能具有旋转不变性（rotation invariance）。

Conclusion: 关联长度的解析性由FK–Ruelle算子主特征值的解析扰动性质导出，并直接蕴含旋转不变性仅在可数个温度点可能成立这一刚性结论。

Abstract: We prove that the correlation length of FK-percolation and Potts models on $\mathbb{Z}^d$ is analytic in the whole subcritical regime of parameters. Our proof makes use of the identification of the polar dual of the mass with the solution of an equation involving the leading eigenvalue of the FK--Ruelle operator, introduced in the context of Ornstein--Zernike theory in Campanino, Ioffe and Velenik (Probab. Theory Relat. Fields, 2003), and crucially relies on Kato's theory of analytic perturbation of linear operators. As a consequence of this result, we prove that the correlation length cannot be invariant by rotation except at countably many values of the temperature parameter in the subcritical phase.

</details>

### [69] [Involutions on $(\infty,n)$-categories of bordisms and reflection TQFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23712)
*Pedro Brunialti*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该文在配边（bordism）的 $(\infty,n)$-范畴 $\mathbf{Bord}_n(H_n)$ 上构造严格对合（strict involution，即严格 $\mathbb{Z}/2$-作用），并据此定义反射型拓扑量子场论（reflection TQFT），证明其对应于同伦不动点空间（homotopy fixed point spaces）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在配边范畴框架下严格处理反射对称性（reflection symmetry），需要一个在 $(\infty,n)$-范畴层面上的严格 $\mathbb{Z}/2$-作用，以给出反射 TQFT 的合适定义。

Method: 采用完备 $n$-重 Segal 空间模型（complete $n$-fold Segal space model）构造 $\mathbf{Bord}_n(H_n)$ 上的严格对合，其中 $H_n$ 为 TQFT 的对称类型（symmetry type）；据此定义反射 TQFT，并借助同伦不动点刻画其结构。

Result: 反射 TQFT 对应于特定的同伦不动点空间；在超代数（super algebras）实例中，依对称类型不同，同伦不动点数据对应于反射理论赋予点的代数上的 stellar 结构或实结构（real structure）。

Conclusion: 该工作将反射对称性纳入配边 $(\infty,n)$-范畴的严格框架，并通过超代数实例表明所得同伦不动点数据具有具体的代数含义。

Abstract: We construct a strict involution, that is, a strict $\mathbb{Z}/2$-action, on the $(\infty,n)$-category $\mathbf{Bord}_n(H_n)$ of bordisms with $H_n$-structure, where $H_n$ is the symmetry type of a TQFT, using the complete $n$-fold Segal space model. Using this involution, we develop an appropriate definition of reflection TQFTs in this setting and show that they correspond to certain homotopy fixed point spaces. Finally, we apply the machinery to the example of super algebras, where, depending on the symmetry type, the homotopy fixed point data corresponds to either a stellar structure or a real structure on the algebra that the reflection theory assigns to the point.

</details>

### [70] [Semiclassical Analysis of Wave Dynamics via Wigner Transforms: Applications to the Quantum Harmonic, Anharmonic, and Dirac Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22414)
*August Burg*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本硕士论文对单带与多带体系中的波动力学进行了半经典分析（semiclassical analysis）的教学式介绍，借助 Wigner 函数（Wigner function）的半经典演化近似波包中心与形变，并系统考察一阶半经典修正的来源、精度条件及与 Sundaram-Niu 形式的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机在于厘清半经典近似的来源与适用边界：既需说明 Wigner 函数半经典演化如何刻画波包中心及形变，又需明确一阶半经典修正（Moyal、Littlejohn-Flynn、Berry）的物理起源、有效条件，并判定其相对 Sundaram-Niu 形式的描述能力差异。

Method: 选取复杂度递增的三个体系：量子谐振子（quantum harmonic oscillator）、量子非谐振子（quantum anharmonic oscillator）作为单带体系，狄拉克振子（Dirac oscillator）作为双带体系。对非谐振子与狄拉克振子各识别一个控制近似精度的无量纲参数（dimensionless parameter）；多带情形要求态处于单一绝热子空间（adiabatic subspace），据此构造单支近相干态（single-branch almost coherent states），并将半经典 Wigner 函数演化与 Sundaram-Niu 形式进行对比。

Result: 单支近相干态表现为由单一能支态叠加而成的近似高斯波包（approximately Gaussian wave packets），其中心振荡带非周期包络（aperiodic envelope）。半经典 Wigner 函数演化能够刻画与形变相关的动力学，包括振荡的初始衰减（initial decay of oscillations），而 Sundaram-Niu 形式在构造上无法描述该效应。精确量子演化（exact quantum evolution）还显示这些态出现部分重局域化（partial relocalization），该现象无法被半经典演化捕捉。

Conclusion: 论文通过三个体系的教学式分析，阐明了半经典修正的起源与精度控制条件，并表明半经典 Wigner 函数演化在描述形变动力学方面优于 Sundaram-Niu 形式，但部分重局域化等纯量子效应超出半经典框架的描述范围。

Abstract: In this Master's thesis, we give a pedagogical introduction to the semiclassical analysis of wave dynamics in single-band and multi-band systems. We show how the semiclassical evolutions of Wigner functions can approximate the dynamics of wave packet centers and wave packet deformations. Three systems of increasing complexity are considered: the quantum harmonic oscillator and the quantum anharmonic oscillator as single-band systems, and the Dirac oscillator as a two-band system. We explain the origins of the first-order semiclassical corrections (Moyal, Littlejohn-Flynn, and Berry) in the equations of motion and examine their effects on predictive accuracy.
  Another focus is set on the conditions for validity of the semiclassical analysis. For both the quantum anharmonic and the Dirac oscillators, a dimensionless parameter controlling the accuracy of the approximation is identified. In multi-band systems, the formalism applies only to states within a single adiabatic subspace, which, for the Dirac oscillator, corresponds to an energy branch. This motivates the construction of single-branch almost coherent states: approximately Gaussian wave packets formed by superpositions of states from a single energy branch. Their centers undergo oscillations with an aperiodic envelope. These states enable comparison between semiclassical Wigner function evolution and the commonly used Sundaram-Niu formalism. We show that semiclassical Wigner function evolution captures deformation-related dynamics, including the initial decay of oscillations, which the Sundaram-Niu formalism cannot describe by construction.
  Additionally, exact quantum evolution reveals partial relocalization of these single-branch almost coherent states, a phenomenon that cannot be captured by the semiclassical evolution.

</details>


### [71] [Quantum-interference correction to conductivity in disordered electronic bands with non-uniform quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22500)
*Yip Chun Wong,Asimpunya Mitra,Yong Baek Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在基于自旋与谷极化菱方N层石墨烯（RNG）的能带投影框架中，非均匀量子几何（nonuniform quantum geometry）可连续调节直流电导量子干涉修正的符号，并在特定参数点于微观时间反演对称性（TRS）破缺下重新出现弱局域化（WL）或弱反局域化（WAL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弱局域化源于时间反演散射路径间的相长干涉，通常一旦时间反演对称性（TRS）破缺即被抑制；本文旨在探究多轨道体系中量子几何是否可定性地改变这一量子干涉修正。

Method: 采用受自旋与谷极化菱方N层石墨烯（RNG）启发的能带投影框架（band-projected framework），考察非均匀量子几何对直流输运量子干涉修正的影响，并分析Bloch态轨道结构与Cooperon模式的关系。

Result: 连续调节量子几何可使电导修正变号，对应局域化与反局域化倾向之间的转变；在控制量子几何的参数取特殊值时，即使微观TRS破缺，WL或WAL仍可重新出现，其机制是Bloch态轨道结构产生涌现的反幺正对称性（emergent antiunitary symmetry），保护无隙Cooperon模式并恢复对数发散的量子干涉修正。

Conclusion: 非均匀量子几何可作为调控多轨道电子体系中量子干涉与电子局域化（electron localization）的机制。

Abstract: Weak localization in two-dimensional disordered electron systems originates from constructive interference between time-reversed scattering paths, giving rise to a logarithmically divergent quantum-interference correction to the dc conductivity. This behavior is generally suppressed when time-reversal symmetry (TRS) is broken. In this study, using a band-projected framework motivated by spin- and valley-polarized rhombohedral $N$-layer graphene (RNG), we show that nonuniform quantum geometry can qualitatively modify quantum-interference corrections to dc transport. In particular, continuously tuning the quantum geometry can change the sign of the conductivity correction, corresponding to a crossover between localization and antilocalization tendencies. Remarkably, at special values of the parameter controlling the quantum geometry, weak-localization (WL) or weak-antilocalization (WAL) can re-emerge despite broken microscopic TRS. At these special points, the orbital structure of the Bloch states gives rise to an emergent antiunitary symmetry that protects a gapless Cooperon mode and restores the logarithmically divergent quantum-interference correction characteristic of WL or WAL. Our results establish nonuniform quantum geometry as a mechanism for controlling quantum interference and electron localization in multiorbital electronic systems.

</details>


### [72] [Valley-Selective Transport through Structural Domain Walls in Rhombohedral Graphene Quarter Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22521)
*Vo Tien Phong,Pablo San-Jose,Bing Cheng,Zhengguang Lu,Elsa Prada,Long Ju,Eugene Mele,Cyprian Lewandowski,Francisco Guinea*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作研究菱面石墨烯多层中两类畴壁（domain wall）对外磁场的响应差异：应变诱导的结构畴壁（structural domain walls）在磁场下变得电子学不透明，而自发谷对称破缺形成的本征畴壁（intrinsic domain walls）在磁场下不稳定并可被消除，从而可通过磁输运实验区分两类畴壁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在菱面石墨烯多层（rhombohedral graphene multilayers）中成像了分隔谷极化相反的“四分之一金属”（quarter metals）的畴壁，其对外磁探针的响应取决于形成机制。因此厘清畴壁行为对解释实验结果至关重要。

Method: 理论考察由应变诱导的一维结构缺陷（strain-induced one-dimensional structural defects）所形成的畴壁，此类畴壁分隔具有互补菱面堆垛序（complementary rhombohedral stacking orders）的畴；分析其跨壁输运与磁场耦合的物理机制。

Result: 跨结构畴壁的输运由谷自由度（valley degree of freedom）控制，而与磁场的耦合由哈密顿量中编码的动量空间手性（momentum-space chirality）决定。因此施加磁场使结构畴壁在电子学上不透明（electronically opaque），而本征畴壁在有序场存在下不稳定并可被消除。

Conclusion: 两类畴壁在磁场下表现出二分性（dichotomy）响应，这一差异使得可通过磁输运实验（magnetotransport experiment）对二者加以区分。

Abstract: Domain walls separating quarter metals with opposite valley polarizations have recently been imaged in rhombohedral graphene multilayers. Depending on their formation mechanism, these walls respond in distinct ways to external magnetic probes. Therefore, understanding their behaviors is critical to the interpretation of experimental results. In this work, we examine domain walls formed from strain-induced one-dimensional structural defects that separate domains with complementary rhombohedral stacking orders. We show that while transport across such domain walls is controlled by the valley degree of freedom, the coupling to a magnetic field is dictated by the momentum-space chirality encoded in the Hamiltonian. As a consequence, structural domain walls are rendered electronically opaque by an applied magnetic field, while intrinsic domain walls formed by spontaneous valley symmetry breaking are unstable in the presence of such an ordering field and can be eliminated. This dichotomy allows the two types of domain walls to be distinguished in a magnetotransport experiment.

</details>


### [73] [Zeta potential of magnetic-field-controlled silver nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22611)
*Y. Tawalbeh,M. F. Pereira*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文将纳米颗粒的“结构表面、有效电荷面与流体滑移面”三者区分，把球密堆积描述扩展为结构-动电耦合模型，用以分析磁场依赖的银纳米颗粒zeta电位。结果表明，仅靠粒径变化无法独立确定界面电荷参数，但模型可同时识别zeta电位的粒径依赖贡献与解读所需的界面信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场依赖的纳米颗粒粒径本身并不能决定zeta电位，后者还受界面电荷、电解质屏蔽（electrolyte screening）与滑移面（slipping plane）位置影响；已有球密堆积描述未区分这些表面，需加以扩展。

Method: 区分结构表面、有效电荷面与流体动力学滑移面，采用零场与250 mT下结构半径20 nm和5 nm、zeta电位-13.2 mV和-10.0 mV，将有效电荷与缺陷壳层原子数关联；并比较线性响应、非线性Poisson–Boltzmann静电学（Poisson–Boltzmann electrostatics）及不同电荷标定方案。

Result: 线性响应归一化形式仅依赖两个和式，无法从场依赖zeta曲线独立确定三个动电长度（electrokinetic lengths）；非线性静电学使拟合壳层系数由0.316变为0.299。恒定总电荷预测zeta幅值增大，与观测趋势相反；两种电荷律端点差小于0.05 mV，端点吻合不足以区分机制。传播初始粒径分布所得端点尺寸分布比实测更窄，电荷参数定标后预测平均zeta为-9.131 mV、标准差0.994 mV。

Conclusion: 结构-动电联合模型可同时识别zeta电位中的粒径依赖贡献与解释所需的界面信息；以实测均值为目标重新标定电荷并不能消除尺寸宽度上的偏差。

Abstract: Magnetic-field-dependent nanoparticle size does not by itself determine zeta potential, which also depends on interfacial charge, electrolyte screening, and the location of the slipping plane. We extend the sphere-packing description of silver nanoparticles by distinguishing the structural surface, the effective charge plane, and the hydrodynamic slipping plane. The model uses structural radii of \SI{20}{nm} and \SI{5}{nm} at zero field and \SI{250}{mT}, with reported zeta potentials of \SI{-13.2}{mV} and \SI{-10.0}{mV}. Effective charge is related to the defective-shell atom count. For field-independent interfacial parameters, the linear response without additional charge weighting decreases with decreasing radius. Its normalized form depends on three electrokinetic lengths through only two sums, preventing their independent determination from a field-dependent zeta curve. At \((κ^{-1},t_{\mathrm{lig}},δ_{\mathrm{slip}})=(3,0,1)\,\mathrm{nm}\), nonlinear Poisson--Boltzmann electrostatics changes the fitted shell coefficient from 0.316 to 0.299. Constant total charge still predicts an increase in zeta magnitude, opposite to the observed trend in the evaluated scenarios. Independently calibrated shell and area charge laws differ by less than \SI{0.05}{mV}, so the endpoint agreement does not distinguish these mechanisms. Propagating the initial size frequencies gives a narrower endpoint size distribution than measured. With charge parameters determined from the measured size populations, its predicted mean zeta potential is \SI{-9.131}{mV}, with standard deviation \SI{0.994}{mV}. Imposing the measured mean by charge recalibration does not remove the discrepancy in size width. The combined structural and electrokinetic model thus identifies both the size-dependent contribution to zeta potential and the interfacial information needed to interpret it.

</details>


### [74] [Chirality-resolved imaging of spin waves with a quantum spin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22867)

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过金刚石氮-空位（NV）色心的两个激发态跃迁作为手性（chirality）通道，实现了对钇铁石榴石（YIG）中Damon-Eshbach自旋波传播方向的单点、无相位参考成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋波（spin wave）的传播方向编码在其圆偏振杂散场的手性中，而标量磁强计无法获取这一信息，因此需要能分辨手性的探测手段。

Method: 利用NV色心作为内禀偏振分析器：绕NV轴右手场驱动一个自旋跃迁、左手场驱动另一个；采用位于YIG自旋波频带内的两个激发态跃迁作为手性通道，对手性通道分别成像，并考察蚀刻边界反射导致的手性反转。

Result: 入射波与反射波在同一谱中以两条谱线出现并可分别成像；基态下同样规则可驱动拉比振荡（Rabi oscillation），实现磁振子（magnon）对手性的选择性自旋控制；读取两个通道即可在单点确定自旋波方向，且无需相位参考。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了手性分辨量子传感（chirality-resolved quantum sensing），并为手性自旋波动力学提供了新的认识途径。

Abstract: Spin waves carry their propagation direction in the handedness of their circularly polarized stray field, a property inaccessible to scalar magnetometers. In the Damon-Eshbach surface wave of an in-plane magnetized film, the dipolar field above the film rotates with a handedness fixed by the direction of travel. The nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centre in diamond is an intrinsic polarization analyser: a right-handed field about the NV axis drives one spin transition, and a left-handed field drives the other, in both the ground and the excited state. Here, we exploit the two excited-state transitions, which lie inside the spin-wave band of yttrium iron garnet (YIG), as a pair of chirality channels and image Damon-Eshbach waves in both directions. Because reflection from an etched boundary reverses the handedness, the incident and reflected waves appear as two lines in one spectrum and are imaged separately. In the ground state, the same rule applies, and the selected field drives Rabi oscillations, a route to chirality-selective control of the NV spin by magnons. Reading the two channels gives the direction of a spin wave at one point, without a phase reference. This advances chirality-resolved quantum sensing and provides insight into chiral spin-wave dynamics.

</details>

*Klaus D. Jöns,Dorian A. Gangloff,Doris E. Reiter*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

Method: 采用视角综述方式，按时间顺序回顾量子点光子源的发展，并将其定位为从21世纪初的量子发射体（quantum emitters）到当今先进光子器件（photonic devices）的演进。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验结果；文中突出了基础研究（fundamental research）与产业化（industrial aspects）之间的相互作用。

Conclusion: 在讨论基础与产业互动之后，文章展望了近未来预期出现的光子量子技术。

Abstract: Photonic quantum technologies are based on controlled photon states. To generate these, quantum dots have emerged as excellent sources of single and entangled photons. In this Perspective, we briefly review the chronological development of quantum dots as photon sources, from their beginnings as quantum emitters in the early 2000s to the advanced photonic devices being developed today. We highlight the interplay between fundamental research and industrial aspects before discussing the photonic quantum technologies we expect to emerge in the near future.

</details>


### [76] [Non-Adiabatic Spin-Induced Currents in Magnetic Weyl Semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23197)
*Kota Watanabe,Akihiro Ozawa,Hiroaki Ishizuka,Hiroki Isobe,Kentaro Nomura*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文用Floquet–Keldysh格林函数理论研究了非中心对称磁性外尔半金属中快速自旋进动诱导的直流电流，并以贝里曲率、量子度规与位移矢量刻画电流的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋动力学可作为含时手性规范场，但其在外尔半金属中诱导直流电流的几何与频率依赖机制尚需理论澄清，尤其是外尔锥倾斜与手性节点贡献的效应。

Method: 采用Floquet–Keldysh格林函数形式（Floquet–Keldysh Green's-function formalism），将自旋进动视为含时手性规范场（chiral gauge field），并用贝里曲率（Berry curvature）、量子度规（quantum metric）和位移矢量（shift vector）表达电流的几何结构。

Result: 各向同性外尔锥仅产生注入电流（injection current），其随驱动频率二次增长、随与费米浴耦合相关的弛豫时间线性增长；有限外尔锥倾斜（Weyl-cone tilt）改变带间跃迁相空间、平滑频率依赖，并产生位移电流（shift current）及另一类注入电流。

Conclusion: 当相反手性外尔节点的跃迁相空间不等价时，各节点贡献求和后仍可残留有限净电流。

Abstract: We theoretically study dc electric currents induced by rapid spin precession in noncentrosymmetric magnetic Weyl semimetals using the Floquet--Keldysh Green's-function formalism. The spin dynamics act as a time-dependent chiral gauge field and the geometric structure of electric currents is expressed in terms of the Berry curvature, the quantum metric, and the shift vector. For an isotropic Weyl cone, only an injection current is generated. This current scales quadratically with the driving frequency and linearly with the relaxation time associated with coupling to a fermionic bath. We further show that a finite Weyl-cone tilt modifies the available interband-transition phase space, smooths the frequency dependence of the current, and allows for a shift current and another type of injection current, both of which flow in directions determined by the tilt and the direction of the precession. A finite net current can remain after summing contributions from Weyl nodes of opposite chiralities when their transition phase spaces are inequivalent.

</details>


### [77] [Topological edge and domain-wall phonons in the mass-dimerized diatomic chain: complex band structure, Zak phase, and spectral unfolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23300)
*Vincent Meunier*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文以复能带结构（complex band structure）为基础，对交替质量一维链的局域振动给出完全解析的描述：无需转移矩阵，可直接由衰减布洛赫波（evanescent Bloch waves）与自由端或畴壁处的力平衡匹配得到各局域模式的闭式频率与衰减长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替质量一维链是具有拓扑能带结构的最简单力学系统，但既往分析多依赖转移矩阵等数值或半解析手段；作者希望建立完全解析的框架，以统一刻画自由端模式与畴壁模式及其拓扑性质。

Method: 将各频率下可用的衰减布洛赫波直接与自由端或畴壁处的力平衡条件匹配，避开转移矩阵（transfer matrices），求得所有局域模式的闭式频率与衰减长度；并结合绝热构造（adiabatic construction）、谱展开（spectral unfolding）、Zak相位与隐藏的手性对称性（chiral symmetry）进行模式分类。

Result: 自由端模式位于复能带结构的分支点（branch point），此处声学支与光学支相接且衰减最快。均匀弹簧情形下，两种质量排序之间的锐壁各承载一个带隙内模式：light|light 壁处对称、heavy|heavy 壁处反对称，绝热构造表明二者为同一拓扑模式；light|light 壁还在光学支上方束缚一个非拓扑模式。谱展开显示布洛赫因子的符号决定模式的带心或带边特征、幅值决定其宽度；由反演量子化的 Zak 相位识别拓扑来源模式，并对每个能带在壁处固定半整数模式计数；应变坐标中隐藏的手性对称性解释了哪些频率被钉扎。

Conclusion: 复能带结构为交替质量一维链的局域振动提供了统一而严格的解析描述，并将自由端模式、畴壁拓扑模式与非拓扑模式的频率、衰减与动量空间特征纳入同一框架。

Abstract: The one-dimensional chain with alternating masses is the simplest mechanical system with a topological band structure. We give a fully analytical account of its localized vibrations built on the complex band structure. The evanescent Bloch waves available at each frequency are matched directly to force balance at a free end or a domain wall, without transfer matrices, which yields closed-form frequencies and decay lengths for every localized mode. The free-end mode sits at a branch point of the complex band structure, where the acoustic and optical sheets join and the decay is fastest. For uniform springs, a sharp wall between the two mass orderings hosts one in-gap mode, symmetric at the light$\mid$light wall and antisymmetric at the heavy$\mid$heavy wall, and an adiabatic construction shows that these are the same topological mode; the light$\mid$light wall also binds a non-topological mode above the optical branch. Spectral unfolding makes this classification visible in momentum space: the sign of the Bloch factor sets the zone-center or zone-boundary character of each mode, and its magnitude sets the width. The Zak phase, quantized by inversion, identifies the modes of topological origin and fixes a half-integer mode count per band at a wall, while the chiral symmetry known to be hidden in strain coordinates explains which frequencies are pinned.

</details>


### [78] [Kinematics of Inverse Compton Scattering from Dirac Carriers in Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23358)
*Dar Faheem Ul Haq,Naveed Ahmad Shah,SSZ Ashraf*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究了石墨烯中狄拉克载流子（Dirac carriers）的康普顿与逆康普顿散射运动学，指出因载流子特征速度vF远小于光速c，标准公式不可简单替换c为vF；核心结果为由能量守恒、色散关系与面内动量守恒导出的散射光子能量二次方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯载流子以特征速度vF传播且vF远小于真空光速c，这一差异使得标准康普顿散射（Compton scattering）公式不能通过将c替换为vF直接套用，因此需要建立适用于石墨烯的运动学框架。

Method: 分别考察位于狄拉克点（Dirac point）的无能隙载流子与带边有隙载流子（面内动量为零）的类康普顿散射，并进一步处理高能无能隙狄拉克载流子的散射；通过联合能量守恒、石墨烯色散关系（dispersion）与面内动量守恒进行运动学推导。

Result: 在所用模型内得到关于散射光子能量的二次方程，此为工作的主要运动学结果；并识别出载流子向光子转移能量、同时满足能量与面内动量守恒的类逆康普顿散射（inverse Compton-like scattering）。

Conclusion: 该过程在运动学上区别于多普勒频移发射（Doppler-shifted emission）、类切伦科夫辐射（Cerenkov-like radiation）、谐波产生（harmonic generation）、光子拖曳（photon drag）等非线性光学过程；文中提出以散射光子谱为主要观测量、以时间与角分辨光电子能谱（TR-ARPES）独立探测非平衡载流子分布的时间分辨泵浦-探测（pump-probe）方案，为分析石墨烯类逆康普顿散射及能量转移条件提供运动学框架。

Abstract: We examine the kinematics of Compton and inverse Compton scattering by Dirac carriers in graphene. Unlike photons in vacuum, which propagate at the speed of light c, graphene carriers have a characteristic velocity vF, with vF much less than c. This difference means that the standard Compton scattering formula cannot be applied to graphene simply by replacing c with vF. We first examine Compton-like scattering for a gapless Dirac carrier initially at the Dirac point and for a gapped carrier at the band edge, both corresponding to zero in-plane momentum. We then consider scattering from an energetic gapless Dirac carrier. By combining energy conservation with the graphene dispersion and in-plane momentum conservation, we obtain a quadratic equation for the scattered photon energy within the model considered. This relation is the main kinematic result of the work. We identify inverse Compton-like scattering by the transfer of energy from the carrier to the photon, subject to the simultaneous conservation of energy and in-plane momentum. We also discuss how this process differs kinematically from Doppler-shifted emission, Cerenkov-like radiation, harmonic generation, photon drag, and other nonlinear optical processes. Finally, we propose a time-resolved pump-probe arrangement in which the scattered photon spectrum is the primary observable, while time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (TR-ARPES) provides an independent probe of the nonequilibrium carrier distribution. These results provide a kinematic framework for analysing inverse Compton-like scattering in graphene and for identifying the conditions under which energy can be transferred from energetic Dirac carriers to photons.

</details>


### [79] [Symmetry constraints on magnon-induced dynamics of skyrmion helicity in frustrated magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23699)
*Gabriel Mayor,Christina Psaroudaki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文以微观Keldysh方法处理磁性斯格明子螺旋度（helicity）与磁振子（magnon）环境的耦合，揭示了螺旋度动力学的重整化与耗散机制，并给出按磁振子模式分辨的选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心对称阻挫磁体（centrosymmetric frustrated magnet）中斯格明子的螺旋度是软集体坐标及潜在量子信息编码变量，其相干性受磁振子环境耦合影响，但以往多仅在唯象层面处理该耦合。

Method: 采用微观Keldysh处理螺旋度—磁振子耦合，分辨各磁振子模式对有效螺旋度动力学的贡献，并分别在U(1)对称模型与U(1)对称破缺情形下分析耦合与耗散。

Result: U(1)对称情形下磁振子浴在最低阶产生反应性螺旋度质量重整化（reactive helicity mass renormalization）；U(1)破缺后出现额外直接耦合，在有限磁振子寿命下产生Ohmic低频耗散，且扰动的空间对称性决定哪些磁振子扇区参与阻尼。

Conclusion: 研究建立了螺旋度动力学与阻挫斯格明子激发谱之间的微观联系，并识别出支配螺旋度基量子态耗散的对称性约束。

Abstract: The helicity of a magnetic skyrmion in a centrosymmetric frustrated magnet is a soft collective coordinate and a candidate variable for encoding quantum information. Its coherence is ultimately influenced by coupling to the magnon environment, which is commonly incorporated only phenomenologically. Here we combine a microscopic Keldysh treatment of the helicity--magnon coupling to resolve the contribution of individual magnon modes to the effective helicity dynamics. In the U(1)-symmetric model, the magnon bath generates, to leading order, a reactive helicity mass renormalization. When U(1) symmetry is broken, additional direct helicity--magnon couplings become allowed. In the presence of finite magnon lifetimes, these couplings generate an Ohmic contribution to the low-frequency helicity dynamics. The spatial symmetry of the perturbation determines which magnon sectors contribute to this damping, providing mode-resolved selection rules for the helicity--magnon coupling. Thus, we establish a microscopic connection between helicity dynamics and the excitation spectrum of frustrated skyrmions and identify the symmetry constraints governing dissipation of helicity-based quantum states.

</details>


### [80] [Limits from nonlinear loss of spin-orbit-torque control of magnon transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23763)
*Lerato Takana,Katya Mikhailova,Charles Zheng,Sauviz Alaei,Xiangcheng Liu,Junwei Tong,Kwangyul Hu,Michael Flatté,Xiaoqin Li,Yuri Suzuki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用Pt栅极的自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit torque, SOT）调控低阻尼锂铝铁氧体（lithium aluminum ferrite, LAFO）三端器件中的有效磁振子阻尼（magnon damping），并发现栅极响应最终受非线性阻尼（nonlinear damping）限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子流（magnon current）调控是磁振子器件（magnonic devices）发展的核心，可实现无电荷流动的信息传输；研究旨在明确SOT调控磁振子阻尼的极限机制。

Method: 在低阻尼LAFO薄膜三端器件中，用Pt栅极施加SOT调控有效磁振子阻尼，结合非局域磁振子信号测量、布里渊光散射显微（Brillouin light scattering microscopy）与微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulations）分析栅极响应。

Result: 随栅极电流增大，非局域磁振子信号先增强、随后饱和并在临界栅极电流密度以上下降；该转折伴随栅极下方局域SOT驱动的自振荡（auto-oscillation）模式出现，其频率随栅极电流红移（redshift），且栅极外传播磁振子布居被抑制；仅当引入幅度依赖的有效阻尼时，微磁模拟才能复现非单调响应。

Conclusion: SOT对磁振子阻尼的补偿最终受自振荡导致的非线性阻尼限制；非线性损耗（nonlinear losses）而非自旋注入饱和（saturation of spin injection）是磁振子调制程度的主要限制因素，为理解磁性绝缘体中磁振子流调制极限提供了框架。

Abstract: Controlling magnon currents is central to the development of magnonic devices, which exploit spin excitations for information transport without charge flow. Here, we use the spin-orbit torque (SOT) from a Pt gate to control the effective magnon damping in three-terminal devices fabricated from low-damping lithium aluminum ferrite (LAFO) thin films and identify the nonlinear damping that ultimately limits the gate response. With increasing gate current, the nonlocal magnon signal initially increases, but saturates and subsequently decreases above a critical gate current density. Brillouin light scattering microscopy reveals that this turnover coincides with the emergence of an SOT-driven auto-oscillation mode localized beneath the gate, whose frequency continuously redshifts with increasing gate current, characteristic of large-amplitude nonlinear dynamics, and with a suppression of the propagating magnon population away from the gate. Micromagnetic simulations reproduce the observed nonmonotonic gate response only when an amplitude-dependent effective damping is included, indicating that nonlinear losses, rather than saturation of spin injection, limit the degree of magnon modulation. Our results show that SOT compensation of magnon damping is ultimately limited by nonlinear damping attributed to auto-oscillations, providing a framework for understanding the limits of magnon current modulation in magnetic insulators.

</details>


### [81] [Engineering nonrelativistic spin splitting in semiconductors by altermagnetic proximity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23773)
*Sing Lam Wong,Marcel Franz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出通过近邻工程（proximity engineering）将交错磁（altermagnet）的自旋劈裂转移到常规半导体中，理论上证明可在此过程中分离非相对论性与相对论性自旋劈裂，从而在成熟半导体平台实现可调控的交错磁自旋劈裂电子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用化交错磁受限于同时满足交错磁对称性、半导体电子结构与弱自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的材料稀缺，作者希望将上述要求分散到不同组成材料中以突破该限制。

Method: 建立常规半导体与有能隙的d波交错磁体耦合的解析理论，分析虚隧穿（virtual tunneling）引起的自旋劈裂；并对完整异质结模型进行精确对角化（exact diagonalization）以验证解析结果。

Result: 虚过程可将交错磁自旋劈裂转移到半导体；诱导的非相对论性与相对论性自旋劈裂对能带对齐（band alignment）的依赖在定性上不同，价带与导带的虚过程可增强交错磁劈裂但对界面Rashba耦合贡献相反符号。静电调控可获得Rashba劈裂消失而d波自旋劈裂仍显著存在的区间；精确对角化证实了该机制在微扰区之外的稳健性。

Conclusion: 交错磁近邻效应为在成熟半导体平台中工程化非相对论性自旋劈裂电子态提供了途径，并为在量子异质结中解耦磁性与相对论性自旋相互作用提供了一般策略。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine vanishing net magnetization with momentum-dependent, nonrelativistic spin splitting, offering functionalities attractive for spintronics and quantum devices. Their practical use, however, is constrained by the limited availability of materials that simultaneously possess the required altermagnetic symmetry, semiconducting electronic structure, and weak spin-orbit coupling. Here we show that these requirements can instead be separated between constituent materials through proximity engineering. We develop an analytical theory for a conventional semiconductor coupled to a gapped $d$-wave altermagnet and demonstrate that virtual tunneling transfers the altermagnetic spin splitting to the semiconductor. Crucially, the induced nonrelativistic and relativistic spin splittings exhibit qualitatively different dependences on band alignment. Virtual processes involving the valence and conduction bands of the altermagnet can reinforce the altermagnetic splitting while contributing with opposite signs to the interfacial Rashba coupling. Consequently, electrostatic tuning can access a regime in which the Rashba splitting vanishes while a sizable $d$-wave spin splitting persists. Exact diagonalization of the full heterostructure model confirms the analytical theory and establishes the robustness of this mechanism beyond the perturbative regime. Our results identify altermagnetic proximity as a route for engineering nonrelativistic spin-split electronic states in established semiconductor platforms, providing a general strategy for disentangling magnetic and relativistic spin interactions in quantum heterostructures.

</details>


### [82] [Orbital Angular Momentum Currents and Textures in a Multiorbital Double Helix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23814)
*Danny Cordova,Edwin Vásconez,Ernesto Medina*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于B型DNA几何参数化的六带多轨道双螺旋模型，揭示双螺旋结构中的原子轨道角动量（OAM）纹理与巡游轨道Edelstein响应及其不同的微观起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）传统上以自旋轨道耦合和自旋角动量解释，而轨道角动量（OAM）被视为对分子手性更直接的电子响应；但仅由s轨道构成的手性跳跃网络缺乏局域原子OAM，忽略了活性p轨道间相干性的贡献，故需建立多轨道双螺旋框架。

Method: 构建六带多轨道双螺旋模型，以B型DNA特征几何与跳跃尺度参数化，纳入主/小沟不对称、轴向碱基对错位（stagger）及两条链的组分不等价作为结构调控参数；采用布洛赫波包（Bloch wave packet）轨道磁化公式分别评估巡游OAM。

Result: 链间杂化（inter-strand hybridization）在共转局域螺旋坐标系中产生完整的三分量原子OAM纹理，包含单螺旋模型中缺失的径向分量；并识别出双螺旋连接性独有的额外链间环流（inter-strand circulation）通道。原子响应与巡游响应在微观起源、量级和温度依赖性上均有差异。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了多轨道框架，可用于研究手性分子导体中几何调控的角动量产生与输运，并将原子OAM与巡游OAM区分为互补但机制不同的两类响应。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of chirality induced spin selectivity (CISS) in biomolecular structures has traditionally been discussed in terms of spin orbit coupling and spin angular momentum. However, orbital angular momentum (OAM) is increasingly recognized as a more direct electronic response to molecular chirality. A helical hopping network built only from s orbitals can support an itinerant orbital moment and an orbital Edelstein response, but such a basis has no local atomic OAM and therefore omits the complementary contribution arising from coherence among active p orbitals. Here we construct a six-band multiorbital double helix model parameterized by characteristic B form DNA geometry and hopping scales. The model incorporates major minor groove asymmetry, an axial base pair offset representing stagger, and compositional inequivalence between the two strands, providing structural control parameters with which the orbital response can be tuned. Inter strand hybridization generates a full three component atomic OAM texture in the corotating local helical frame, including a radial component absent in the corresponding single helix model. We separately evaluate the itinerant OAM using the Bloch wave packet formulation of orbital magnetization and identify an additional inter-strand circulation channel unique to the double-helix connectivity. The resulting atomic and itinerant responses have distinct microscopic origins, magnitudes, and temperature dependences, establishing a multiorbital framework for geometry controlled angular momentum generation and transport in chiral molecular conductors.

</details>

### [83] [Electrochemical Molecular Dynamics within the Finite-Field Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23291)
*Krystof Brezina,Ludwig Ahrens-Iwers,Mariana Rossi*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种纯基于有限场（finite-field）三维周期性边界条件的恒压分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）方法，名为 electrostat 控制器件，用于在原子尺度模拟电化学界面；其在大体系中与简单恒场模拟给出相同平衡性质，差异仅体现在小尺度，从而界定了电压涨落建模的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电界面的电化学过程（electrochemical processes）是现代技术的核心，其改进依赖对原子尺度过程的理解；但现有在 MD 中施加恒压（constant-voltage）条件的工具通常直接作用于电极电荷，难以推广到超越经验性金属描述的方法。因此需要一种在有限场框架内、不依赖经验金属模型即可实现电压控制的方案。

Method: 在 LAMMPS 的 ELECTRODE 包中推导并实现一个称为 electrostat 的电压控制器件，构建仅在三维周期性边界条件（3D periodic boundary conditions）下、以有限场模拟（finite-field simulations）形式表述的热力学系综（thermodynamic ensemble），并将该理论与既有方法（如恒电位器 potentiostat）系统对比。

Result: 基准测试表明所推导理论与其他已建立方法等价（例如与恒电位器等价），并量化了正确计入系综涨落（ensemble fluctuations）的重要性；对数千原子体系，electrostat 与更简单的恒场模拟获得相同平衡性质，二者差异仅在更小尺度上显现，从而给出在原子模拟中引入电压涨落的实际界限。

Conclusion: 该框架的优势在于其简洁且易于扩展到第一性原理（ab initio）与反应性机器学习势（reactive machine-learning potentials），为电化学体系的原子尺度模拟提供了可推广的恒压控制手段。

Abstract: Electrochemical processes at charged interfaces lie at the heart of modern technologies and their continued improvement depends on insights into the relevant atomistic processes. However, the necessary tools to impose constant-voltage conditions in molecular dynamics (MD) simulations typically directly target the electrode charge, making their extension to methods that go beyond an empirical description of metals more cumbersome. In this work, we propose an MD protocol to realize a thermodynamic ensemble in which voltage control is attained, but which is formulated purely within the framework of finite-field simulations in 3D periodic boundary conditions. We show a derivation and implementation of such a control device, which we coin an electrostat, in the ELECTRODE package within LAMMPS. Our simulations systematically benchmark the derived theory against other established approaches, for example, showing its equivalence to a potentiostat, and quantify the importance of properly accounting for ensemble fluctuations. We find that for systems of several thousands of atoms, one obtains the same equilibrium properties with the electrostat as with a simpler constant-field simulation. The difference between the two methods only manifests itself at smaller length scales, thereby establishing practical limits for the inclusion of voltage fluctuations in atomistic simulations of electrochemical systems. The strength of this framework also lies in its simplicity of extension to first-principles ab initio and reactive machine-learning potentials.

</details>

### [84] [Leading-twist proton parton distribution amplitude from scalar and axial-vector diquark correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22994)
*B. Almeida-Zamora,L. Albino,C. Mezrag,J. Segovia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用基于微扰论积分表示构造的核子Faddeev波函数代数表示，计算质子领头twist部分子分布振幅（PDA），得到宽而凹、峰值偏离对称点的分布，并给出μ₀=0.51 GeV处的紧凑参数化及演化至2 GeV的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子部分子分布振幅（parton distribution amplitude, PDA）是描述硬遍举重子过程的关键非微扰输入，需要厘清夸克—双夸克（quark–diquark）关联如何塑造其光锥结构。

Method: 采用受微扰论积分表示启发的核子Faddeev波函数代数表示，通过对协变夸克—双夸克Faddeev振幅作光锥投影获得PDA，并分离其标量双夸克与轴矢双夸克分量，推导相应双夸克分布振幅的解析表达式；进一步计算低阶Mellin矩并做领头阶QCD演化。

Result: 所得分布宽而凹，峰值位于(x₁,x₂,x₃)≈(0.55,0.20,0.25)，偏离微扰QCD渐近形式对应的对称点(1/3,1/3,1/3)；标量双夸克关联造成纵向动量分布的强烈不对称，轴矢双夸克关联则在价夸克间重新分配动量并显著削弱该不对称。在μ₀=0.51 GeV处得到紧凑参数化，演化至μ=2 GeV后动量不对称减小，但仍保留非微扰关联的清晰印记。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了夸克—双夸克关联在塑造质子光锥结构中的作用，并为硬遍举重子过程研究提供非微扰输入。

Abstract: We compute the leading-twist proton parton distribution amplitude (PDA) using algebraic representations of the nucleon Faddeev wave function inspired by perturbation-theory integral representations. The PDA is obtained from a light-front projection of the covariant quark--diquark Faddeev amplitude and analysed in terms of its scalar- and axial-vector-diquark components. We derive analytic expressions for the corresponding diquark distribution amplitudes and their contributions to the proton PDA. The resulting distribution is broad and concave, with its maximum $(x_1,x_2,x_3)\approx(0.55,0.20,0.25)$ displaced from the symmetric configuration $(1/3,1/3,1/3)$, which corresponds to the maximum of the perturbative-QCD asymptotic form. Scalar-diquark correlations generate a strong asymmetry in the longitudinal momentum distribution, whereas axial-vector correlations redistribute momentum among the valence quarks and significantly reduce this asymmetry. We calculate low-order Mellin moments and obtain a compact parametrisation of the proton PDA at the hadronic scale, $μ_0=0.51\,\mathrm{GeV}$. We then perform the leading-order QCD evolution of its normalized shape to $μ=2\,\mathrm{GeV}$, finding that evolution reduces the momentum asymmetry while preserving a clear imprint of the non-perturbative quark--diquark correlations. The results provide insight into the role of such correlations in shaping the proton light-front structure and furnish non-perturbative input for studies of hard exclusive baryonic processes.

</details>


### [85] [Equiprobability Anomaly Detector Using Angular Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23667)
*Alex Whitten,John Cicero,Tae Min Hong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用半轻子末态中顶夸克对（top quark pair, $t\bar{t}$）的角分布构建异常探测器（anomaly detector），以区分非 $t\bar{t}$ 事件；方法在 parton 层面性能与归一化流（normalizing flows）相当，但受重建层面的喷注分配（jet assignments）限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是识别非 $t\bar{t}$ 事件，即构建针对非 $t\bar{t}$ 背景的异常探测方案，借助 $t\bar{t}$ 角分布的差异实现事件分离。

Method: 采用 Wigner $D$ 函数（Wigner $D$ functions）展开 $t\bar{t}$ 角分布，其系数由正交级数密度估计（orthogonal series density estimation）确定；以等概率面（equiprobable surfaces）对事件分箱（bin），并用 Kullback-Leibler 散度（Kullback-Leibler divergence）量化分离度，进而与变分自编码器（variational autoencoders）建立联系。

Result: 非 $t\bar{t}$ 事件集中于 $t\bar{t}$ 密度较低区域，从而实现与大 $t\bar{t}$ 密度事件的分离；在 parton 层面性能与归一化流（normalizing flows）相当，但在重建层面受喷注分配（jet assignments）限制。

Conclusion: 基于角分布的异常探测方法是可行的，其密度估计框架与变分自编码器存在理论联系；实际应用的主要瓶颈在于重建层面的喷注分配问题。

Abstract: We use the angular distribution of top quark pairs ($t\bar{t}$) in the semileptonic final state $b\bar{b}\,\ellνq\bar{q}'$ to construct an anomaly detector for non-$t\bar{t}$ events. Wigner $D$ functions expand the distribution with coefficients determined by orthogonal series density estimation. Its equiprobable surfaces bin the events, the non-$t\bar{t}$ concentrate at low $t\bar{t}$ density. The separation is quantified by the Kullback-Leibler divergence, which makes connection to variational autoencoders. The performance is comparable to normalizing flows at parton level, but is limited by jet assignments at reconstruction level.

</details>


### [86] [A Levinson's theorem for particle form factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09207)
*Francesco Rosini,Simone Pacetti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本工作提出并演示了一种专用于形状因子（form factor）相位渐近行为的Levinson定理版本。该定理将时间类区域上相位趋于π整数倍的值与强子电磁相互作用动力学所决定的形状因子性质一一对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由解析性（analyticity）要求，形状因子是平方四动量（square four-momentum）的多值复函数，其复平面上沿正实轴有割线，相位在割线上沿（时间类区域）渐近趋于π的整数倍；需要一个定理来刻画这一整数倍与物理动力学之间的联系。

Method: 提出并论证一个针对形状因子相位渐近行为的Levinson定理版本，该版本以解析性所要求的割线结构及时间类区域上的相位渐近整数倍行为为出发点。

Result: 该Levinson定理建立了相位渐近趋于的π整数倍与相应强子（hadron）电磁相互作用动力学相关形状因子性质之间的唯一（univocal）关系。

Conclusion: 形状因子相位的渐近π整数倍并非任意，而是由强子电磁相互作用动力学唯一确定，从而为该渐近行为提供了一种定理化的刻画方式。

Abstract: We present and demonstrate a version of Levinson's theorem especially dedicated to the asymptotic behavior of form factor phases. Indeed, as required by analyticity, form factors are multi-valued complex functions of a square four-momentum defined in the complex plane with a cut along the positive real axis. Their phases evaluated on the upper edge of this cut, i.e., on the time-like region, tend asymptotically to integer multiples of $π$ radians. The Levinson's theorem establishes a univocal relation between such multiples and properties of form factors related to the dynamics of the electromagnetic interaction of the corresponding hadrons.

</details>


### [87] [The Singular Structure of Rapidity Distributions for Color-Singlet Production at N3LO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22533)
*Federica Devoto,Bernhard Mistlberger,Gherardo Vita*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文给出了强子对撞中色单态（color-singlet）产生过程在N3LO精度下快度分布（rapidity distributions）的普适奇异结构，核心新要素是三圈夸克与胶子快度束函数匹配核（rapidity beam-function matching kernels）的完整计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为将色单态产生过程部分子系数函数的微扰精度推进至N3LO，需要掌握部分子动量分数趋于一时快度分布的完整奇异贡献，此前缺少三圈快度束函数匹配核这一关键构件。

Method: 基于对希格斯玻色子（Higgs boson）与Drell-Yan产生部分子截面（partonic cross sections）的严格共线展开（strict collinear expansion），完成三圈夸克与胶子快度束函数匹配核的计算，并以广义多重对数（generalized polylogarithms）及数值级数展开表示结果。

Result: 所得结果完整刻画了任一变量趋于一时的全部奇异贡献（含delta函数项），同时保留对另一变量的精确依赖；N3LO部分子系数函数适用于任意色单态产生过程，过程依赖性仅通过硬函数（hard function）进入。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了N3LO快度分布的普适奇异结构，使色单态产生过程在快度空间的微扰计算可借由硬函数统一表述，并为后续高阶微分截面计算奠定基础。

Abstract: We determine the universal singular structure of rapidity distributions for color-singlet production in hadron collisions through next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order (N3LO) in QCD. Our result captures the full singular contributions as either partonic momentum fraction approaches one, including the delta-function terms, while retaining the exact dependence on the other. The essential new ingredient is the complete calculation of the three-loop quark and gluon rapidity beam-function matching kernels. Our calculation is based on the strict collinear expansion of the partonic cross sections for Higgs boson and Drell-Yan production. We represent our results in terms of generalized polylogarithms as well as numerical series expansions. The resulting N3LO partonic coefficient functions apply to arbitrary color-singlet production processes with the process dependence entering through the hard function, which encodes the virtual corrections to the Born process.

</details>


### [88] [Principle of no paradoxical Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23606)
*Ngo Phuc Duc Loc*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文基于DESI暗示的动力学暗能量（dynamical dark energy）与有限宇宙寿命（可能发生"大挤压" Big Crunch），提出一个猜想：宇宙或选择使黑洞信息悖论（black hole information paradox）在原则上无法实际发生的构型，并给出黑洞质量与宇宙寿命之间的下界关系及若干可检验预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准ΛCDM模型中宇宙寿命无限，最重超大质量黑洞终将蒸发，信息悖论不可避免；而DESI结果表明暗能量可能是动力学演化的，宇宙或在数十亿年后经历大挤压而终结，从而最轻的约一个太阳质量黑洞可能来不及蒸发。这一反差促使作者追问：宇宙是否通过参数选择从根源上规避信息悖论。

Method: 工作方式为理论推演与猜想构建：以宇宙有限寿命为前提，结合黑洞蒸发时标与寿命的比较，推导出使信息悖论在自然界无法实现的黑洞质量下界，该下界表示为宇宙寿命的函数，并据此给出相关预言。

Result: 得到了黑洞质量随宇宙寿命变化的下界关系，使得信息悖论在现实宇宙中不可实现；同时给出了若干由此产生的可检验预言，并简要讨论了其对物理学其他相关重要构想的深层含义。

Conclusion: 若宇宙寿命有限，则黑洞蒸发与信息悖论的实际发生会受到质量—寿命关系的限制；作者据此提出的"宇宙规避信息悖论"猜想为连接宇宙学（cosmology）与黑洞信息理论提供了一个新的视角，并对若干重大物理问题具有潜在启示。

Abstract: In the standard $Λ\rm CDM$ model, the Universe will keep expanding forever and its lifetime is infinite. Therefore, even the heaviest supermassive black hole will eventually evaporate and the universal occurrence of the black hole information paradox is unavoidable. However, the recent DESI results seem to indicate that dark energy is dynamical and, more importantly, it is possible that the Universe has a finite lifetime and will experience a ``Big Crunch" in some billion years from now. In light of this, the lightest confirmed black hole with mass of order one solar mass may not have a chance to evaporate before the Universe ends. Inspired by these observations, we thus raise an intriguing conjecture: Does the Universe choose a configuration such that the ``in-principle" black hole information paradox can never actually happen in reality? We derive a lower bound on black hole mass as a function of the Universe's lifetime such that the information paradox cannot be realized in nature and some resulting predictions are made. Some profound implications for other related big ideas in physics are briefly discussed.

</details>


### [89] [Complete $g^6$ pressure of hot Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23675)
*Keijo Kajantie,Pablo Navarrete,Risto Paatelainen,Leon Sandbote,York Schröder,Kaapo Seppänen,Aleksi Vuorinen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出热$\mathrm{SU}(3)$ Yang-Mills理论压强弱耦合展开的完整$g^6$阶项，解决了悬置二十余年的问题，并与四维格点模拟数据定量吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热$\mathrm{SU}(3)$ Yang-Mills理论压强弱耦合展开的$g^6$阶项长期未定，此前研究无法完成该阶的完整计算，制约了对微扰展开与降维有效场论（dimensionally reduced effective-field-theory）范式的检验。

Method: 采用热圈-树对偶（thermal loop-tree duality）方法，结合新颖的局域紫外与红外减除（local ultraviolet and infrared subtractions），计算了九个此前未知的非因子化四圈求和-积分（nonfactorizing four-loop sum-integrals）。

Result: 完成了对一般$\mathrm{SU}(N_c)$压强硬标度（hard-scale）贡献的计算；对$N_c=3$结合已知色电与色磁（chromoelectric and chromomagnetic）贡献，证明了来自不同动量标度的红外发散（infrared divergences）的解析抵消，并得到完整的$O(g^6)$压强。

Conclusion: 所得结果在较宽温度范围内与四维格点模拟（lattice simulations）数据定量一致，为降维有效场论范式提供了严格检验。

Abstract: We present the full order $g^6$ term in the weak-coupling expansion of the pressure of hot $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ Yang-Mills theory, resolving a problem that has remained open for more than two decades. To achieve this, we evaluate nine previously unknown nonfactorizing four-loop sum-integrals using the thermal loop-tree duality method with novel local ultraviolet and infrared subtractions, thereby completing the hard-scale contribution to the pressure for general $\mathrm{SU}(N_c)$. Combining this result with known chromoelectric and chromomagnetic contributions, available for $N_c=3$, we demonstrate the analytic cancellation of infrared divergences originating from different momentum scales and obtain the complete $O(g^6)$ pressure for $\mathrm{SU}(3)$. Our result displays quantitative agreement with data from four-dimensional lattice simulations over a broad range of temperatures, providing a stringent test of the dimensionally reduced effective-field-theory paradigm.

</details>


### [90] [The pressure of the gluon plasma at $\mathcal{O}(g_s^6)$, perturbatively](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23676)
*Mikael Chala,Luis Gil,Javier López Miras*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了高温胶子等离子体压强（pressure of the gluon plasma）的四圈硬标度匹配修正（four-loop hard-scale matching correction），从而完成了长期悬而未决的至 O(g_s^6) 阶的微扰计算，并首次在该阶对弱耦合展开（weak-coupling expansion）进行了直接检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温胶子等离子体压强的微扰展开至 O(g_s^6) 阶的计算长期未完成，此前仅有通过将微扰级数拟合到连续外推的格点数据（continuum-extrapolated lattice data）间接得到的该阶数值，缺乏直接的理论验证。

Method: 在微扰 QCD 框架下，计算胶子等离子体压强中四圈硬标度匹配修正项，该修正补齐了展开至 O(g_s^6) 阶（g_s 为强耦合）所需的最后一块。

Result: 所得结果与 arXiv:1204.6184 中通过拟合微扰级数到连续外推格点数据所获得的值在不确定度范围内一致，构成对该阶弱耦合展开的首次直接检验。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了至 O(g_s^6) 阶的压强微扰计算，其与格点拟合结果的吻合支持了高温胶子等离子体压强弱耦合展开在此阶的有效性。

Abstract: We determine the four-loop hard-scale matching correction to the pressure of the gluon plasma at high temperature. This result completes the long-standing calculation through $\mathcal{O}(g_s^6)$, where $g_s$ is the strong coupling. Our result agrees, within uncertainties, with the value obtained in arXiv:1204.6184 by fitting the perturbative series to continuum-extrapolated lattice data, thereby providing the first direct test of the weak-coupling expansion at this order.

</details>


### [91] [Entropy Production and Thermalization in Heavy-Quark Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22501)
*A. M. Silva*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文把重夸克（heavy quark）视为夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma, QGP）中的布朗探针（Brownian probe），建立其动量分布的非可逆熵平衡（irreversible entropy balance），并将碰撞导致的熵产生与介质演化造成的驱动严格区分，在 Bjorken 膨胀的温差协议下给出强弱耦合的闭式极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重夸克常被当作 QGP 的布朗探针，但其动量分布的熵产生与介质演化带来的驱动混在一起，缺乏可区分二者并给出定量极限的框架。本文动机即为建立可分离碰撞产生（collisional production）与演化驱动（driving）的不可逆熵收支。

Method: 构建线性动力学下的不可逆熵平衡，将总产生分解为定向动量（directed momentum）与动量宽度（momentum width）两个正贡献；静态热浴满足精确 H 定理（H-theorem），冷却热浴加入显式驱动项，纵向 Bjorken 膨胀作为独立的运动学驱动处理。对 Bjorken 温度协议推导总产生的强、弱耦合闭式极限，并以具 Jüttner 稳态的相对论 Fokker-Planck 方程对粲、底夸克数值求解，用静态生成元计算线性响应系数进行验证。

Result: 静态热浴下 H 定理精确成立；冷却热浴产生显式驱动项，纵向膨胀作为独立运动学驱动而非碰撞产生。强耦合下总产生超出初始相对熵（relative entropy）一个正比于弛豫时间（relaxation time）的项，其准静态部分具热力学长度（thermodynamic-length）形式；弱耦合下则随弛豫时间反比趋于零。正强耦合斜率因此保证中间耦合处耗散最大。数值熵平衡以高精度满足，且由静态生成元得到的线性响应系数可重现两个极限。

Conclusion: 该框架将重夸克动量分布的熵产生清晰拆分为碰撞产生与运动学驱动，给出强弱耦合的闭式极限并预测中间耦合的极大耗散；同一结构适用于具 Jüttner 稳态的相对论 Fokker-Planck 方程，数值结果与解析极限一致。

Abstract: Heavy quarks are often treated as Brownian probes of the quark-gluon plasma. We formulate the irreversible entropy balance of their momentum distribution and separate the production generated by collisions from the driving caused by an evolving medium. For linear dynamics the production splits into two positive contributions, from directed momentum and from the momentum width. A static bath obeys an exact H-theorem. A cooling bath adds an explicit driving term, and longitudinal Bjorken expansion enters as a separate kinematic drive rather than as collisional production. For a Bjorken temperature protocol we derive closed-form strong- and weak-coupling limits of the total production. At strong coupling it exceeds the initial relative entropy by a term linear in the relaxation time, whose quasistatic part has the thermodynamic-length form. At weak coupling it vanishes inversely with the relaxation time. A positive strong-coupling slope therefore guarantees maximal dissipation at intermediate coupling. The same structure holds for a relativistic Fokker-Planck equation with a Jüttner stationary state, solved numerically for charm and bottom quarks under the temperature protocol. Linear-response coefficients computed from the static generator reproduce both limits, and the numerical entropy balance is satisfied to high accuracy.

</details>


### [92] [Regularization of stochastic fluid dynamics and non-perturbative renormalization of the shear viscosity of incompressible fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23442)
*Mattis Harhoff,Cedric Riedel,Johannes Roth,Sören Schlichting*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种保持全部相关对称性的随机流体力学（stochastic fluid dynamics）正则化方案，证明其紫外有限（UV finite）并可用于重整化群（renormalization group）改进的剪切黏度（shear viscosity）计算与格点数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机流体动力学中的涨落效应带来紫外发散问题，需要一种既能保持对称性又可定义连续极限（continuum limit）的正则化（regularization）方法。

Method: 针对不可压缩流体（incompressible fluid）构造保持对称性的正则化理论，显式论证其紫外有限性；并同时采用格点离散化（lattice discretization）的直接数值模拟与连续统泛函方法（functional methods），进行重整化群改进的微扰计算。

Result: 正则化理论紫外有限并存在连续极限，且既可做格点直接数值模拟，也可用连续统泛函方法处理；据此给出二维与三维不可压缩流体剪切黏度重整化的结果，并与格点模拟对比。

Conclusion: 该正则化方案在保持对称性的同时实现了紫外完备性，为随机流体力学中重整化群分析与格点模拟的相互验证提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We propose a regularization of stochastic fluid dynamics that preserves all relevant symmetries. By considering an incompressible fluid, we demonstrate explicitly that the regularized theory is UV finite, and therefore admits a continuum limit. Strikingly, the regularized theory of stochastic fluid dynamics is not only amenable to direct numerical simulations of the lattice discretized theory, but also to functional methods in the continuum. We exploit this to perform renormalization group improved perturbative calculations of the renormalization of the shear viscosity of an incompressible fluid in two and three spatial dimensions, and compare our results to direct numerical simulations of the lattice discretized theory.

</details>

### [93] [Long-Lived Charge Trap Characterization for Rare-Event Searches with Skipper-CCDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22418)
*Ansh Desai,Ana M. Botti,Brenda A. Cervantes-Vergara,Rouven Essig,Stephen E. Holland,Ian Lawson,Steffon Luoma,Santiago Perez,Javier Tiffenberg,Sho Uemura,Yikai Wu,Tien-Tien Yu*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该工作利用“像素泵浦（pixel-pumping）”原位均匀注入电荷技术，在Fermilab MINOS洞室地下的SENSEI传感器中系统刻画单电子陷阱（single-electron traps），测得1787个陷阱并评估其对单电子本底（single-electron background）的贡献，发现已刻画陷阱群体不足以显著影响单电子率，但无法排除足够多的“隐藏陷阱群体”的潜在贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Skipper型电荷耦合器件（Skipper Charge-Coupled Devices, Skipper-CCDs）具备亚电子分辨率，是稀有事件搜寻（rare-event searches）的关键探测器；但其单电子率受低能本底制约，而晶格缺陷（lattice defects）导致的单电子电荷陷阱可能是该本底的成分之一，此前缺乏对长寿命陷阱的有效探测手段。

Method: 采用名为“像素泵浦（pixel-pumping）”的新型原位（in-situ）均匀电荷产生技术，对置于Fermilab MINOS洞室地下的SENSEI传感器进行单电子陷阱研究，从而将探测灵敏度拓展至发射时间（emission times）大于10^3秒的长寿命陷阱区间，并对陷阱群体进行统计刻画与模拟外推。

Result: 共完整刻画1787个陷阱；通过模拟发现所刻画的陷阱群体对单电子率无显著影响。研究进一步给出该分析敏感的能量（energies）与截面（cross-sections）范围，并与可能影响单电子率的参数区间比较，表明本分析覆盖了该区间的较大比例。

Conclusion: 已刻画的陷阱不足以解释单电子本底，但数量足够多的“隐藏群体（hidden population）”仍可能显著贡献单电子率，需发展新的测量技术方能完全排除该可能性。

Abstract: Skipper Charge-Coupled Devices (Skipper-CCDs) are detectors with sub-electron resolution, allowing them to play a critical role in rare-event searches. A major challenge in experiments using these detectors is understanding and mitigating low-energy backgrounds which impact the single-electron rate. One potential component of the single-electron rate could arise from single-electron charge traps caused by lattice defects. In this work we use a new in-situ technique for generating uniform charge known as ``pixel-pumping'' across the CCD to study single-electron traps in a SENSEI sensor placed underground in the MINOS cavern at Fermilab, which enables sensitivity to long-lived traps with emission times $>10^3$ seconds, a regime that was previously not accessible. We fully characterize 1787 traps and use this information to estimate their contribution to the single-electron background in SENSEI. Through simulation, we find the characterized population of traps does not significantly impact the single-electron rate. We further identify the energies and cross-sections for traps to which this analysis is sensitive, and compare this with the values that could impact the single-electron rate. This analysis probes a large fraction of this region. However, we also see that a sufficiently numerous hidden population could contribute significantly to the single-electron rate, and new measurement techniques will have to be devised in order to fully rule out this population.

</details>


### [94] [Improved aluminum film transport properties and enhanced TES response in athermal phonon detectors: implications for quasiparticle dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22856)
*Mahdi Mirzakhani,Keith Hunter,Rupak Mahapatra,Mark Platt*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该文报道通过优化铝膜沉积工艺（增大晶粒尺寸与残余电阻比RRR）来提升QET传感器中准粒子扩散长度，从而增强非热声子收集效率与信号强度。对比实验显示，仅改变铝膜沉积条件即可获得更高的信号强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质与相干弹性中微子—核散射（CEνNS）探测需要更低阈值与更强信号，而QET传感器中铝膜吸收非热声子的效率受限于准粒子扩散长度（quasiparticle diffusion length），因此提升该长度是改进声子收集效率的关键方向。

Method: 采用光刻图案化的超导非热声子传感器（QET），耦合千克级锗、硅、蓝宝石吸收体；铝膜被图案化为有限长度的鳍状结构。通过改进铝膜沉积工艺获得更大晶粒尺寸与更高残余电阻比（residual-resistivity ratio, RRR），并在其他制备参数完全相同的条件下与旧探测器进行对照。

Result: 新制备的铝膜表现出系统性更大的晶粒尺寸和更高的RRR；在给定能量下，改进铝膜沉积的探测器相比仅铝膜沉积不同的对照探测器实现了更高的信号强度。

Conclusion: 改善超导铝膜的晶粒尺寸与RRR可有效提升声子收集效率与信号强度，为低阈值暗物质与CEνNS搜寻提供了工艺优化路径。

Abstract: Our detectors for Dark Matter and Coherent Elastic Neutrino--Nucleus Scattering (CE$ν$NS) use photolithographically patterned superconducting athermal phonon sensors that are coupled to kg-scale absorbers of different materials (germanium, silicon, sapphire). Following a particle interaction, athermal phonons from the bulk absorber break Cooper pairs in a superconducting aluminum film that surrounds a tungsten Transition Edge Sensor (TES) in equilibrium through electrothermal feedback. This sensor design is called Quasiparticle-trap-assisted Electro-thermal-feedback Transition-edge-sensor (QET). The QET aluminum film that absorbs the athermal phonons is patterned into fins with lengths limited by the quasiparticle diffusion length in aluminum. Improving the quasiparticle diffusion length in the superconducting aluminum is an area of vigorous research to improve phonon collection efficiency and increase the signal strength for lower-threshold searches. We report significantly improved aluminum films that show systematically larger grain sizes and residual-resistivity ratio (RRR) compared to detectors we have fabricated in the past. The improved RRR and grain sizes were expected to improve the collected signal for a given energy. We report higher signal strength achieved in a detector in comparison to another detector in which all other fabrication parameters were kept identical except for the aluminum film deposition.

</details>


### [95] [Automated end-station at the soft X-ray beamline featuring robotic air-to-ultrahigh-vacuum sample transfer, scanning probe spectroscopy, and machine learning analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22384)
*Keita Hiromori,Masashige Miyamoto,Masafumi Horio,Takeru Nakashima,Yasunobu Ando,Masato Kotsugi,Iwao Matsuda*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该工作报道了在NanoTerasu光源BL08U光束线搭建的全自动、无需现场人员值守的软X射线实验终端站：由四个腔室单元与两台机器人完成样品从大气到超高真空测量台的自动传输与回收，光谱采集后经机器学习方法自动评估，数据汇入数据库供远程用户查阅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能与机器人技术在实验科学中的融合日益加深，促使同步辐射软X射线光束线的用户实验执行方式需要重新定义；无人值守终端站可为用户提供更高效的机时，并使光束线科学家与技术人员得以从事更具人性化与创造性的工作。

Method: 系统安装于NanoTerasu的BL08U光束线，由四个腔室单元和两台机器人组成，自动将样品从环境空气传入超高真空（ultrahigh vacuum）测量台，测量后再送回原位。用户在指定样品区域编程后，扫描微聚焦光束即可自动获取软X射线吸收光谱（soft X-ray absorption spectra），随后采用主成分分析（principal-component analysis）、多维标度（multidimensional scaling）、t分布随机邻域嵌入（t-distributed stochastic neighbour embedding）和均匀流形近似与投影（uniform manifold approximation and projection）等机器学习技术进行自动评估。

Result: 实现了样品传输、光谱采集与数据评估的全流程自动化，高通量数据被系统地积累于数据库中，供远程用户检查。

Conclusion: 该无人值守终端站为用户提供了高效的机时利用方式，并使光束线科学家/技术人员能够投入更多具有人性化与创造性的工作。

Abstract: Driven by the growing integration of artificial intelligence and robotics in experimental science, we redefine the execution of user experiments at synchrotron soft X-ray beamlines by developing a highly automated end-station that can be operated without on-site staff. Installed at beamline BL08U of NanoTerasu, the system comprises four chamber units and two robots that automatically transfer samples from ambient air to an ultrahigh vacuum measurement stage for spectroscopic evaluations, before returning them to their original locations post-measurement. Soft X-ray absorption spectra are automatically acquired once programmed by scanning the micro-focused beam over the designated sample area, followed by automated evaluation using machine learning techniques such as principal-component analysis, multidimensional scaling, t-distributed stochastic neighbour embedding, and uniform manifold approximation and projection. The high-throughput data are systematically accumulated in the database for remote user examination. This unstaffed end-station provides efficient beamtime for users and allows beamline scientists/technicians to pursue more human and creative endeavors.

</details>


### [96] [Experimental Demonstration of a Balanced Travelling Wave Parametric Amplifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23810)
*Nikita Klimovich,Peter K. Day,Henry G. Leduc,Boon-Kok Tan*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证一种平衡式（balanced）架构，将三个混频（three-wave-mixing, 3WM）行波参量放大器（travelling-wave parametric amplifier, TWPA）的镜像抑制瞬时带宽（image-rejected bandwidth）提升一倍，通过空间分离放大信号与泵浦/闲频（idler）信号消除边带简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导行波参量放大器具备宽带增益、低附加噪声与大动态范围，适用于量子比特谐振腔阵列、光谱仪与辐射计等宽带或多频复用信号放大。但其可用带宽常受镜像频率处闲频（idler）产生的限制，3WM TWPA中输入信号与镜像频率信号难以区分，导致宽带或未知输入谱下出现频谱模糊（spectral ambiguity）。

Method: 采用平衡式架构：将两只放大器连接在一对180°混合耦合器（hybrid coupler）之间，信号与泵浦分别通过和端口（sum port）与差端口（difference port）注入与输出，从而在空间上分离放大信号与输出泵浦及生成的闲频信号。实验使用两只动态电感（kinetic-inductance）TWPA进行验证。

Result: 实验实现约20 dB信号增益，覆盖近一个倍频程（octave）带宽；泵浦抑制（pump rejection）低于−20 dB，工作频带内平均镜像抑制约−15 dB，并实现镜像抑制瞬时带宽的加倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明平衡式TWPA是实现宽带参量放大的可行方案，可获得更优的无污染带宽并免除复杂滤波需求。

Abstract: Superconducting travelling-wave parametric amplifiers (TWPAs) provide broadband gain, low added noise, and high dynamic range, making them attractive for applications requiring wide instantaneous bandwidth. This is crucial for amplifying multiplexed narrow band signals spread over a wide frequency range, such as qubit resonator arrays, or amplifying inherently wide band signals, such as in spectrometers or radiometers. In many cases, however, the useful bandwidth of a TWPA is limited by the production of the idler tone at an image frequency. For three-wave-mixing (3WM) TWPAs, for example, an input signal at $f_s$ is indistinguishable from one at $f_p - f_s$, which can produce spectral ambiguity when the input spectrum is broadband or not known in advance. We experimentally demonstrate a balanced architecture that doubles the instantaneous image-rejected bandwidth available to a 3WM TWPA by spatially separating the amplified signal from the output pump and generated idler, which resolves the sideband degeneracy. The architecture consists of two amplifiers connected between a pair of 180$^{\circ}$ hybrid couplers, with the signal and pump injected and output through the sum and difference ports, respectively. Using two kinetic-inductance TWPAs, we demonstrate approximately 20 dB of signal gain across nearly an octave of bandwidth, together with pump rejection below --20 dB and an average image rejection of approximately --15 dB over the operating band. These results establish balanced TWPAs as a practical approach to broadband parametric amplification, enabling improved contamination free bandwidth and eliminating the need for complex filtering.

</details>
