### [97] [Visualizing surface-reaction-driven chloride migration and cascading failure in sulfide solid electrolytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22394)
*Xiaoya He,Haoran Zhang,Xinyang Yue,Hang Liu,Zian Mao,Jialong Wang,Min Liu,Shufen Chu,Liwei Chen,Zhaoxin Yu,Xi Liu,Chuying Ouyang,Ziyang Ning,Yujun Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用低温四维扫描透射电子显微镜（cryo-4D-STEM）在纳米尺度揭示了不同氯含量硫银锗矿（argyrodite）电解质在湿气暴露下的降解差异，并结合密度泛函理论（DFT）指出氯再分布是降解扩展的关键过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硫化物固态电解质（sulfide solid electrolytes）的湿气不稳定性（moisture instability）限制了其在全固态电池（all-solid-state batteries）中的实际应用，而湿气暴露过程中的气-固反应（gas–solid reactions）尚缺乏空间分辨的表征手段。

Method: 采用低温四维扫描透射电子显微镜（cryo-4D-STEM）对低氯与高氯两种硫银锗矿电解质 Li6PS5Cl 与 Li5.5PS4.5Cl1.5 的纳米尺度降解进行空间成像，并用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算氯迁移势垒，考察氯脱除与空位形成对 PS4^3- 骨架的影响；同时评估表面处理（surface treatment）对湿气耐受性的改善。

Result: Li6PS5Cl 主要表现为表面局域化降解并逐步向内延伸；Li5.5PS4.5Cl1.5 则出现快速的内部非晶化（interior amorphization）并伴随向外的氯再分布（chloride redistribution）。DFT 表明高氯组分在建模路径上具有更低的氯迁移势垒，且氯脱除与空位形成会扭曲 PS4^3- 骨架并可能使晶格失稳。

Conclusion: 氯再分布被确定为降解传播的关键过程；表面处理可抑制可检测的大尺度氯偏析（large-scale chloride segregation）并提升湿气耐受性，0.33 C 循环 150 次后仍保持初始容量的 99.9%，为湿气耐受型固态电池提供了可能路径。

Abstract: Moisture instability of sulfide solid electrolytes limits practical use in all-solid-state batteries. Spatially resolving gas--solid reactions during moisture exposure remains challenging. Here, cryogenic four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (cryo-4D-STEM) maps nanoscale degradation in low- and high-Cl argyrodite electrolytes, Li\(_6\)PS\(_5\)Cl and Li\(_{5.5}\)PS\(_{4.5}\)Cl\(_{1.5}\). Li\(_6\)PS\(_5\)Cl undergoes predominantly surface-localized degradation that progressively extends inward, whereas Li\(_{5.5}\)PS\(_{4.5}\)Cl\(_{1.5}\) exhibits rapid interior amorphization accompanied by outward chloride redistribution. Density functional theory calculations indicate a lower chloride-migration barrier along the modeled pathway in the higher-Cl composition, while chloride removal and vacancy formation distort the PS\(_4^{3-}\) framework and may destabilize the lattice. Surface treatment suppresses detectable large-scale chloride segregation and improves moisture tolerance, retaining 99.9% of the capacity measured at the start of 0.33 C cycling after 150 cycles. These results identify chloride redistribution as a key process in degradation propagation and suggest a route toward moisture-tolerant solid-state batteries.

</details>


### [98] [Comment on "Skyrmion Hall Effect in Altermagnets"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22486)
*Nicolai Timon Bechler,Jan Masell*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文为针对 Jin 等人（PRL 133, 196701）关于 d 波交错磁体（altermagnet）中斯格明子霍尔效应（skyrmion Hall effect, SkHE）预测的评论（Comment），指出其将 SkHE 归因于无量纲四极张量（quadrupole tensor）$A_{ij}$ 的做法在数学上不成立，数值结果实为离散化假象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jin 等人提出在 d 波交错磁体中，子晶格间拓扑荷（topological charge）相互抵消，故将自旋转移矩（spin-transfer torque, STT）驱动的横向速度归因于四极张量 $A_{ij}$ 并拟合出非零 $A_{xy}$。本文旨在检验这一归因的合理性。

Method: 通过散度定理（divergence theorem）对 $A_{ij}$ 进行解析变换，将其化简为边界积分，并分析该积分在充分光滑且局域化的磁织构（magnetic texture）下的取值行为，据此复核 Jin 等人的数值结论。

Result: $A_{ij}$ 可化为边界积分，对充分光滑、局域化的织构该积分趋于零，因而无法支撑有限 $A_{xy}$ 的拟合结果。

Conclusion: 支持此前文献 [2] 的主张：Jin 等人对采用标准 Zhang-Li STT 的微磁模型（micromagnetic model）所观测到的 SkHE，仅是离散化假象（discretization artefact），而非真实的物理效应。

Abstract: In their Letter, Jin et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 133, 196701 (2024)] predict a transverse velocity $v_y$ for skyrmions driven by spin-transfer torque (STT), known as skyrmion Hall effect (SkHE), in d-wave altermagnets. Usually, the SkHE emerges from the skyrmion's topological charge which here cancels between sublattices. Instead, Jin et al. attribute the SkHE to the dimensionless quadrupole tensor $A_{ij}$. The result in their Eq. (12) is fitted to micromagnetic simulations, yielding a finite value for $A_{xy}$. However, via the divergence theorem, $A_{ij}$ simplifies to a boundary integral that vanishes for a sufficiently smooth and localized texture. In this Comment, we substantiate our claim from Ref. [2] that the numerically observed SkHE by Jin et al. for a micromagnetic model with standard Zhang-Li STT in the individual sublattices is merely a discretization artefact.

</details>


### [99] [A Comprehensive Survey of Crystal Structural Prototypes: Construction, Classification, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22600)
*Jianjun Hu,Ying Feng,Rongzhi Dong,Jeffrey Hu,Remy Rogers,Dipannoy Gupta*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统综述晶体结构原型库的构建方法与等价规则，并通过基于 GraphID 的实证分析与生成模型对比，揭示原型统计、邻域容差敏感性与模板检索的有效性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体结构原型（crystal structural prototypes）跨化学体系重复出现，但不同原型库在保留的结构信息与施加的等价规则上存在差异，缺乏统一比较框架，且原型复用对预测精度与泛化的影响尚不明确。

Method: 从晶体学整编、标准化、几何匹配、局域环境聚类与周期图（periodic-graph）分析等维度比较方法，区分晶体学结构类型、几何实现、化学匿名拓扑与化学修饰四个层次；综述 AFLOW、CSPD、LAE-ICSPD、EMFDTW、TYPIX、ICSD 结构类型等库及家族归属算法，并对 ICSD、Materials Project、OQMD、LeMat 样本进行 GraphID 分析及 12 个生成式 CSP 模型比较。

Result: 各样本中最高频的 100 个家族仅覆盖 30%–67% 的结构，而 72%–82% 的家族为单例（singleton）；邻域容差变化可使多达 76% 结构的 GraphID 改变；12 个生成式晶体结构预测（CSP）模型中模板检索（template retrieval）的 top-1 成功率最高；移除某一约简化学计量类比后，该类测试结构中 50%–78% 出现新失败，反映的是化学计量覆盖敏感性而非对未见原型的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 研究结果与结构类型、空间群、周期网络及蛋白质折叠覆盖度的既往统计相呼应；建议采用独立数据库覆盖度测量、发现率分析与家族留出（family-held-out）测试来评估原型复用的影响。

Abstract: Crystal structural prototypes describe recurring atomic arrangements across chemically distinct materials. Prototype libraries are built through crystallographic curation, standardization, geometric matching, local-environment clustering, and periodic-graph analysis, and support chemical substitution, derivative enumeration, high-throughput screening, structure solution, crystal structure prediction (CSP), and generative design. This survey compares methods by the structural information they retain and the equivalence rules they impose, distinguishing crystallographic structure types, geometric realizations, chemistry-anonymous topologies, and chemical decorations. We review curated and automatically extracted prototype libraries (AFLOW, CSPD, LAE-ICSPD, EMFDTW, TYPIX, ICSD structure types), their source databases, and algorithms for assigning family membership. A GraphID-based analysis of samples from ICSD, the Materials Project, OQMD, and LeMat shows that the 100 most frequent families in each sample cover 30 to 67 percent of analyzed structures, while 72 to 82 percent of observed families are singletons. The GraphIDs of up to 76 percent of structures change when the neighbor tolerance is varied. In our comparison of 12 generative CSP models, template retrieval achieves the highest top-1 success rate. Removing a reduced-stoichiometry class from training introduces new failures in 50 to 78 percent of the test structures of that class, which measures sensitivity to stoichiometric coverage, not generalization to unseen prototypes. We relate these findings to earlier statistics of structure types, space groups, and periodic nets, and to protein-fold coverage studies. We propose independent-database coverage measurements, discovery-rate analyses, and family-held-out tests to assess how prototype reuse affects prediction accuracy, generalization, and novelty.

</details>


### [100] [Physics-Informed Machine Learning for Response Tensors in Crystalline Magnets: Application to Kagome Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22642)
*Yu-Hsuan Lin,Joel E. Moore*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作为任意晶体结构与磁织构构建完备的对称不变量描述符基（symmetry-invariant descriptor basis），并提出层级稳定的无序参量提取框架，用于响应张量的机器学习建模与可解释分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从磁织构（magnetic texture）预测响应张量（response tensor）是非共线磁体（noncollinear magnets）研究的核心难题，而现有描述符构造方法仅限一阶多极展开（multipole expansion）或局限于简单磁结构，缺乏通用且可解释的描述符体系。

Method: 构建适用于任意晶体结构与磁织构的完备对称不变量描述符基；改造标准主成分分析（principal component analysis, PCA）框架，导出层级稳定的无序参量（order parameter）——将无序参量空间扩展至包含更高阶自旋序时，新增参量与已有参量互不相关。

Result: 在磁性 kagome 晶格的反常霍尔电导率（anomalous Hall conductivity, AHC）回归与分类任务中验证该框架；在 AHC 可由对称不变量无序参量线性回归良好描述的区间内，回归系数的平方按大小排序服从类 Zipf 幂律：$β_{r}^2 \propto r^{-α}$。

Conclusion: 该方法为构建覆盖各阶自旋序的可解释无序参量提供了系统路径，将磁晶体的对称性与响应张量关联起来，可用于响应张量的机器学习建模与物理解释。

Abstract: The prediction of response tensors from magnetic textures is a crucial challenge in the study of noncollinear magnets. Existing methods for constructing descriptors of a given response tensor are limited to first-order multipole expansions or restricted to simple magnetic structures. In this work, we construct a complete symmetry-invariant descriptor basis for arbitrary crystal structures and magnetic textures, providing interpretable features for machine learning modeling of response tensors. We modify the standard principal component analysis framework and derive uncorrelated order parameters that are hierarchically stable: extending the order parameter space to include higher-degree spin order introduces additional order parameters that are uncorrelated to the previous ones. We apply this framework to regression and classification of the anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) in a magnetic kagome lattice. In particular, in the regime where AHC is well modeled by linear regression of the symmetry-invariant order parameters, we find that the squared regression coefficients, ranked by their magnitude, obey a Zipf-like power law: $β_{r}^2 \propto r^{-α}$. Our method provides a systematic route to construct interpretable order parameters of all spin orders that connect the symmetry of a magnetic crystal to its response tensors.

</details>


### [101] [Generalized length-gauge time-dependent Liouville equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22799)
*G. P. Zhang,M. S. Si*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作指出长度规范（length gauge, LG）含时刘维尔方程（time-dependent Liouville equation）中位置算符对易子存在数十年的两种不可调和版本，证明广义方程应含两个对k的偏导而无需狄拉克δ函数，并表明此前的k对角化处理（Aversa–Sipe 形式）仅为忽略相对动量（relative momentum）Q 的极限情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长度规范含时刘维尔方程是模拟超快动力学（ultrafast dynamics）的主要工具，但其核心项——密度算符ρ̂与位置算符r的对易子——存在Kohn–Luttinger与Aversa–Sipe两套互不相容的表达（双偏导 vs. 单偏导加狄拉克δ），该差异长期未受关注。

Method: 从理论上推导广义长度规范含时刘维尔方程，考察位置算符的k非对角矩阵元（off-diagonal matrix elements）在电场驱动下产生ρ̂的k非对角分量的作用，并与假设位置矩阵对k对角化的做法对比；辅以数值测试验证。

Result: 广义方程含两个对k与k'的偏导数且不含狄拉克δ函数；Aversa–Sipe方程是其忽略两态间相对动量Q的极限情形；数值测试显示k对角化形式结果定性不同，并将能量吸收（energy absorption）与极化（polarization）低估约7倍。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了长度规范含时刘维尔方程的普遍形式，为超快时间尺度研究开辟了新途径，并提示基于仅含k对角项方程的历史结论可能需要重新审视。

Abstract: The length-gauge (LG) time-dependent Liouville equation is a major
  workhorse to simulate ultrafast dynamics. The equation features the
  commutator between the density operator $\hatρ$ and the
  position operator ${\bf r}$, $\langle n{\bf k}|[{\bf
  r},\hatρ]|m{\bf k}'\rangle$, where $|n{\bf k}\rangle$ and $|m{\bf
  k}'\rangle$ are Bloch states for bands $n$ and $m$ at crystal momenta
  ${\bf k}$ and ${\bf k}'$, respectively. This commutator has two
  irreconcilable versions: Kohn and Luttinger (Phys. Rev. ${\bf
  108}$, 590 (1954)) have two partial derivatives with respect to
  ${\bf k}$ and ${\bf k}'$, but Aversa and Sipe (AS) (Phys. Rev. B
  ${\bf 52}$, 14636 (1995)) have one partial derivative and one Dirac
  delta. For several decades, this outstanding discrepancy has
  received little attention. Here, we show that the generalized LG
  time-dependent Liouville equation has two partial derivatives, but
  no Dirac delta. Physically, the ${\bf k}$-off-diagonal matrix elements
  of the position operator provide a channel by which the electric
  field generates ${\bf k}$-off-diagonal matrix elements for
  $\hatρ$ in LG. The discrepancy emerges when the position
  matrix is assumed to be diagonal in ${\bf k}$. Consequently the
  AS' equation is a limiting case of our generalized Liouville
  equation, where the relative momentum ${\bf Q}$ between two states is
  ignored. Our numerical test reveals that the ${\bf k}$-diagonal
  formalism yields qualitatively different results and underestimates
  both energy absorption and polarization by a factor of 7. Our
  study establishes a general formalism for the length-gauge
  time-dependent Liouville equation and opens the door for future
  research on the ultrafast time scale, while prior results based on
  the ${\bf k}$-diagonal only equation may need to be revisited.

</details>


### [102] [Interplay between high-entropy effects and the superionic transition in fluorite oxides: A liquid-like perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22850)
*Fengnian Zhuang,Gaosheng Yan,Hong Chen,Yi Zhang,Wenshan Yu,Minglong Xu,Shengping Shen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用混合蒙特卡洛/分子动力学（MC/MD）方法，揭示含次锕系（MA）的MOX高熵陶瓷中Np、Am偏析形成的短程有序（SRO）导致严重晶格畸变，并在2000–2800 K区间呈现局域高熵阻塞与集体超离子动力学的竞争机制，最终由熵驱动的补偿机制引发O离子迁移率的动力学反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含次锕系（minor actinide, MA）的MOX作为高熵陶瓷（high-entropy ceramic, HEC）是先进快堆燃料的重要候选材料，但其局域化学有序如何调控极端条件下的离子输运尚不明确，需要为兼具抗辐照性与传质性能的核燃料设计提供理论依据。

Method: 采用混合蒙特卡洛/分子动力学（hybrid Monte Carlo/Molecular Dynamics, MC/MD）方法，对SRO模型与随机固溶体（random solid solution, RSS）模型进行对比，并结合吉布斯自由能最小化与缺陷分析。

Result: Np、Am偏析建立的SRO诱导严重晶格畸变；低温区局域静电与应变耦合势阱限制O离子扩散，随热激活体系动力学异质性增强；这些局域陷阱自发转变为高度互联的链状多面体拓扑网络，形成低黏度输运通道，使SRO模型的O离子迁移率超过RSS模型，发生动力学反转。

Conclusion: 研究阐明局域化学有序在极端条件下调控离子输运的机制，并借吉布斯自由能最小化与缺陷分析揭示HEC中超离子转变的结构与热力学起源，为设计兼具优异抗辐照性与优化传质能力的高性能核燃料提供理论支撑。

Abstract: Minor actinide (MA)-bearing mixed oxide (MOX), a prominent high-entropy ceramic (HEC), is a promising candidate for advanced fast reactor fuels. Using a hybrid Monte Carlo/Molecular Dynamics (MC/MD) approach, we reveal that Np and Am segregation establishes a distinct short-range order (SRO) that induces severe lattice distortion. We uncover a competitive mechanism between localized high-entropy blocking effects and collective superionic dynamics, where the anionic kinetic and structural evolution within the 2000-2800 K range is partitioned and governed by the Tammann crossover temperature and superionic transition temperature. In the low-temperature regime, severe lattice distortions induced by the SRO structures create localized electrostatic and strain-coupled potential wells, which spatially confine the O-ions and lead to their sluggish diffusion. The system exhibits increased dynamical heterogeneity as thermal activation alleviates these structural constraints. Crucially, these localized traps spontaneously transform into a highly interconnected, string-like polyhedral topological network. Driven by the maximization of configurational entropy, this structural change creates low-viscosity transport pathways that trigger confinement-enhanced diffusion. Consequently, a dramatic kinetic inversion occurs, where the O-ion mobility of SRO model surpasses that of random solid solution (RSS) model via an entropy-driven compensation mechanism. Finally, through Gibbs free energy minimization and defect analysis, structural and thermodynamic origins of the superionic transition in HEC are elucidated. This study unveils how local chemical ordering regulates ion transport under extreme conditions, providing theoretical support for the design of high-performance nuclear fuels with both superior radiation tolerance and optimized mass transfer capabilities.

</details>


### [103] [Adaptive Crack-Tip Localization of Phase-Field Fracture Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22888)
*Aarosh Dahal,Aditya Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文将相场断裂模型与自适应网格细化/粗化框架结合，通过Schur补将裂纹尖端活动区域之外的非活动域凝聚到界面并复用其分解，从而把重复求解的计算量局限在裂纹尖端附近的小区域，实现大规模相场断裂模拟的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场断裂模型（phase-field models of fracture）能统一模拟裂纹形核与扩展，但由于平衡方程与相场方程需在整个区域上反复迭代求解，计算成本很高，亟需降低重复求解的开销。

Method: 扩展已有的自适应网格细化与粗化（adaptive mesh refinement and coarsening）框架，使空间分辨率与重复求解计算量均定位到线弹性脆性材料裂纹尖端周围的小活动区域（active patch）；非活动外部区域通过Schur补（Schur complement）消去并凝聚到区域界面，只要活动区域固定即复用其分解结果，同时将外部相场冻结，方法在FEniCSx中实现。

Result: 在准静态裂纹扩展、动态裂纹分叉、三维混合模式裂纹扩展及单胞中多裂纹合并等问题上，凝聚格式复现参考解，全局量差异低于0.5%，且显著减少活动自由度数量；准静态surfing问题中，区域尺寸加倍时墙钟加速比（wall time speedup）从6.9提升至22.8。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持精度的同时大幅降低相场断裂模拟的求解规模，且计算优势随问题规模增大而增强，适用于大规模相场断裂模拟。

Abstract: Phase-field models of fracture provide a unified framework for simulating crack nucleation and propagation, but their computational cost remains high because the governing equilibrium and phase-field equations are typically iteratively solved over the entire domain at every staggered iteration. We present an extension of the recently developed adaptive mesh refinement and coarsening framework that localizes not only spatial resolution but also repeated solution effort to a small active patch around the crack tip for linear elastic brittle materials. The inactive exterior is eliminated through a Schur complement and condensed onto the patch interface, and its factorization is reused as long as the patch remains fixed. The phase field is frozen in the exterior, introducing only a small approximation in the crack wake. The method is implemented in FEniCSx and evaluated for quasi-static crack propagation, dynamic crack branching, three-dimensional mixed-mode crack growth, and coalescence of multiple cracks in a unit cell. Across these problems, the condensed formulation reproduces the reference solutions with differences in global quantities below 0.5%, while substantially reducing the number of active degrees of freedom. In the quasi-static surfing problem, wall time speedups increase from 6.9 to 22.8 as the domain size is doubled, demonstrating increasing computational advantage for large-scale phase-field fracture simulations.

</details>


### [104] [Coherent phonons in photoexcited semiconductors from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22901)
*Yuhui Qiu,Rong-Tian Pang,Meng-Xue Guan,Jin-Jian Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将第一性原理运动方程（equation-of-motion, EOM）框架扩展至光激发半导体，通过引入残余等离子体（plasma）力与等离子体诱导的电子结构重整化（renormalization），成功描述了单层MoTe₂中相干声子（coherent phonons）的载流子密度依赖行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有第一性原理EOM方法虽能描述金属与半金属中的相干声子，但推广到半导体时需处理带隙以上激发产生的长寿命电子—空穴等离子体（electron–hole plasma），现有框架未涵盖这一效应。相干声子作为相锁定（phase-locked）晶格振动，是探测非平衡动力学（nonequilibrium dynamics）的灵敏手段。

Method: 在EOM框架中加入两项：残余等离子体力（residual plasma force），用于移动晶格平衡位置；以及等离子体诱导的电子结构重整化。以单层MoTe₂为对象，将计算结果与受约束密度泛函微扰理论（constrained density-functional perturbation theory）比较验证。

Result: 计算得到的载流子密度依赖Te位移仅在纳入重整化能带结构后才与约束DFPT结果一致。提高光激发载流子密度会同时增强初始总力与A₁g模式的等离子体移动平衡位置，从而产生更大的相干晶格振荡。

Conclusion: 密度调控的响应同时体现在原子位移与相干声子诱导的能带重整化（band renormalization）中，可为超快反射率（ultrafast reflectivity）及时间分辨与角分辨光电子能谱（time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）提供互补的实验特征。

Abstract: Coherent phonons are phase-locked lattice vibrations that provide a sensitive probe of nonequilibrium dynamics in materials. Existing first-principles equation-of-motion (EOM) approaches have successfully described coherent phonons in metals and semimetals, but their extension to semiconductors requires treating the long-lived electron--hole plasma created by above-gap excitation. Here we extend the first-principles EOM framework to photoexcited semiconductors by incorporating the residual plasma force that shifts the lattice equilibrium together with the plasma-induced renormalization of the electronic structure. For monolayer MoTe$_2$, the calculated carrier-density-dependent Te displacement agrees with constrained density-functional perturbation theory only when the renormalized band structure is included. We then show that increasing the photoexcited carrier density enhances both the initial total force and the plasma-shifted equilibrium position of the $A_{1g}$ mode, producing larger coherent lattice oscillations. This density-controlled response appears in both the atomic displacement and the coherent-phonon-induced band renormalization, providing complementary signatures for ultrafast reflectivity and time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy.

</details>


### [105] [From Surface Reactions to Inhibitor Coverage: A Machine-Learning-Assisted Computational Study of Area-Selective Atomic Layer Deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22911)
*Gijin Kim,Seungwu Han,Youngho Kang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将预训练通用机器学习原子间势与离格巨正则蒙特卡洛模拟相结合，在非晶SiO2上研究抑制剂吸附与前驱体阻挡，揭示位点依赖性反应能、空间位阻和吸附片段反应活性共同决定抑制剂覆盖度与阻挡性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域选择性原子层沉积（area-selective atomic layer deposition, AS-ALD）需要预测抑制剂覆盖度以理解和优化表面阻挡；然而真实非晶表面位点异质性强，覆盖度与阻挡能力的关联尚不清楚。

Method: 构建结合预训练通用机器学习原子间势（universal machine-learning interatomic potential）与离格巨正则蒙特卡洛（off-lattice grand canonical Monte Carlo）的计算流程，并应用于非晶SiO2（amorphous SiO2）上的抑制剂吸附与前驱体阻挡。

Result: 真实表面模型显示反应能与活化势垒存在显著位点依赖差异，硅醇基（silanol groups）是氨基硅烷类抑制剂及金属前驱体的主要吸附位点；生成相同吸附片段的抑制剂因完整分子的空间位阻（steric constraints）不同而呈现不同覆盖度与阻挡能力。

Conclusion: 在真实非晶表面上需同时考虑空间位阻效应与化学反应活性；吸附抑制剂片段对前驱体的反应活性决定其稳定性与阻挡表现，该计算框架为理解抑制机理及筛选抑制剂—前驱体组合提供了依据。

Abstract: Area-selective atomic layer deposition (AS-ALD) enables selective material growth for the fabrication of nanoscale semiconductor devices. Predicting inhibitor coverage is essential for understanding and optimizing surface blocking in AS-ALD. Here, we present a computational workflow combining a pretrained universal machine-learning interatomic potential with off-lattice grand canonical Monte Carlo simulations and apply it to inhibitor adsorption and precursor blocking on amorphous SiO2. Realistic surface models reveal substantial site-dependent variations in reaction energies and activation barriers, with silanol groups serving as the primary adsorption sites for aminosilane and related inhibitors and metal precursors. Coverage simulations show that inhibitors producing identical adsorbed fragments can yield different coverages because the intact molecules impose distinct steric constraints, leading to different accessible site densities and blocking capabilities. Beyond coverage, we highlight the importance of the reactivity of adsorbed inhibitor fragments toward precursors in determining inhibitor stability and blocking performance. Together, these findings demonstrate the need to consider both steric effects and chemical reactivity on realistic amorphous surfaces. Our calculations connect local reaction chemistry with inhibitor coverage and provide a computational basis for understanding inhibition mechanisms and selecting suitable inhibitor-precursor combinations for AS-ALD.

</details>


### [106] [Light-induced local control of magnetic anisotropy and domain pattern in Co/Pt multilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23132)
*Ivan Soldatov,Aman Singh,Volker Neu,Rudolf Schäfer,Andrei Sherstobitov,Yana Vaynzof,Stanislav Bodnar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用超短激光脉冲在垂直磁化Co/Pt多层膜中实现双机制局域磁各向异性调控：低注量下可逆、依赖螺旋度的瞬态磁态累积，高注量下作为局域热退火永久增强有效垂直磁各向异性，使局域矫顽场翻倍并展宽条纹畴宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统调控磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）的手段如材料组分、热退火与离子轰击虽广泛应用，但难以实现空间分辨的连续调控，因而需要发展可局域化、可灵活图案化的各向异性构筑方法，以服务于自旋电子学（spintronics）与磁存储器件。

Method: 采用多尺度表征手段，结合克尔显微术（Kerr microscopy）、磁力显微术（magnetic force microscopy）与振动样品磁强计（vibrating sample magnetometry），研究超短激光脉冲辐照对垂直磁化（perpendicularly magnetized）Co/Pt多层膜的注量依赖效应。

Result: 低注量辐照产生可逆、依赖螺旋度（helicity-dependent）的磁相累积，且不改变本征准静态性质；高注量辐照则等效于局域热退火，通过结构弛豫显著增强有效垂直磁各向异性（effective perpendicular magnetic anisotropy），使局域矫顽场（coercive field）提高约两倍，并展宽平衡条纹畴（stripe domain）宽度。

Conclusion: 该研究同时实现了可逆自旋操控与二维磁畴形貌的确定性工程，为无需光刻（lithography-free）的柔性各向异性结构化提供了途径，可推动先进自旋电子学应用的发展。

Abstract: Control of magnetic anisotropy is essential for the fabrication of next-generation devices in spintronics and magnetic memory. While conventional approaches such as material composition, thermal annealing, and ion bombardment are widely used, achieving spatially resolved, continuous control remains a challenge. Herein, we demonstrate a dual-regime, localized control over both transient magnetic states and permanent intrinsic properties in perpendicularly magnetized Co/Pt multilayers using ultra-short laser pulses. By employing a multi-scale approach combining Kerr microscopy, magnetic force microscopy, and vibrating sample magnetometry, we reveal that low-fluence irradiation enables a reversible, helicity-dependent accumulation of magnetic phases without altering the intrinsic quasistatic properties. Conversely, high-fluence exposure acts as localized thermal annealing that permanently hardens the magnetic system. This structural relaxation leads to a substantial enhancement of the effective perpendicular magnetic anisotropy, driving a twofold increase in the local coercive field and expanding the equilibrium stripe domain width. By offering both reversible spin manipulation and deterministic engineering of 2D magnetic landscapes, this work paves the way towards flexible, lithography-free anisotropy structuring for advanced spintronic applications.

</details>


### [107] [The effect of oxygen on the surface tension of AlTi metallic alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23286)
*Yuna Kato,Jürgen Brillo,Thomas Voigtmann,Linnea Heitmeier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potential, MLIP）的分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟研究了AlTi合金熔体的表面性质及氧的影响，发现表面张力对氧的响应强烈依赖于AlTi组成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlTi合金熔体的表面性质及氧的作用尚待澄清，尤其缺乏对组成依赖性与界面元素分布的原子尺度认识；本文借助已在实验数据上验证过的MLIP模型对此进行模拟研究。

Method: 对所建立的MLIP模型（其体相（bulk）静态与动力学性质已由实验数据检验）开展AlTi金属合金熔体的MD模拟，计算表面张力响应与元素分辨（element-resolved）密度分布，并与微重力（microgravity）悬浮技术（levitation techniques）获得的实验值进行对比。

Result: 表面张力对氧的响应随AlTi组成显著变化；元素分辨密度分布表明，界面附近的氧富集（oxygen enrichment）伴随着无氧液体中所观察到的Al表面偏析（surface segregation）被抑制。

Conclusion: 氧与Al表面偏析之间存在耦合，且氧对熔体表面性质的影响具有明显组成依赖性；模拟结果与微重力悬浮实验数据可比对，为MLIP在合金熔体表面研究中的适用性提供支持。

Abstract: We perform molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of AlTi metallic alloy melts, with a specific focus on surface properties and the effect of oxygen. The simulations use a machine-learned inter- action potential (MLIP) that has recently been tested through experimental data for the statical and dynamical properties of AlTi melts in the bulk [Kato et al. (2026)]. We find that the surface- tension response to oxygen depends strongly on the AlTi composition. Element-resolved density profiles show that oxygen enrichment near the interface is accompanied by a suppression of the Al surface segregation observed in the oxygen-free liquid. The results are compared to experimental values obtained using levitation techniques in microgravity.

</details>


### [108] [Hydrogen Ion-Cut Single-Crystal Diamond Membranes for Wide and Ultrawide Bandgap Semiconductor Heterointegration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23433)
*Xuanyu Zhou,Chenyu Wang,Dmitry Shinyavskiy,Lu Chen,Luke Suter,Andreas Graff,Michel Simon-Najasek,Vincent Gambin,Katherine T. Fountaine,Zhenqiang Ma,Frank Altmann,Matthias Muehle,Jung-Hun Seo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用氢离子剥离（hydrogen ion-cut）结合两步退火、电化学刻蚀与微转印技术制备单晶金刚石膜（single-crystal diamond membranes, SCDms），并成功将其无胶转移至GaN、SiC、AlGaN与AlN衬底，形成金刚石/宽禁带（WBG）与超宽禁带（UWBG）异质结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向金刚石基异质集成（heterogeneous integration）应用，需要高质量、可转移的单晶金刚石膜，并需明确合适的离子注入物种与工艺路线。

Method: 采用氢离子注入剥离结合两步注入后退火、电化学刻蚀与微转印打印（micro-transfer printing）；通过蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）比较H、He、C、O注入的空位分布，并用拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）与光学带隙分析评估晶格损伤及恢复情况。

Result: 模拟显示在所考察注入条件下氢的空位分布剖面更窄；剥离并去除残余石墨材料后获得晶体质量改善的自支撑SCDms，并实现无粘附层转移至GaN、SiC、AlGaN和AlN衬底。

Conclusion: 氢注入被确立为制备高质量单晶金刚石膜的有效途径，所实现的金刚石/WBG与金刚石/UWBG异质结构证明其可与多种半导体平台集成的可行性，为未来金刚石基异质集成应用提供基础。

Abstract: Single-crystal diamond membranes (SCDms) were created using a hydrogen ion-cut process combined with a two-step post-implantation annealing, electrochemical etching, and micro-transfer printing. Monte Carlo simulations comparing H, He, C, and O implantation predicted a narrower vacancy-distribution profile for hydrogen under the implantation conditions examined. Raman spectroscopy was used to evaluate implantation-induced lattice damage and its recovery during annealing and membrane release, while optical bandgap analysis provided further evidence of restoration of the diamond lattice structure. After delamination and removal of residual graphitic material, freestanding SCDms with improved crystalline quality were obtained. The SCDms were subsequently transferred to GaN, SiC, AlGaN, and AlN substrates without an adhesive layer. The successful formation of diamond/WBG and diamond/UWBG heterostructures demonstrates the feasibility of integrating the SCDms with a range of semiconductor platforms. Overall, these results establish hydrogen implantation as an effective route for producing high-quality SCDms for future diamond-based heterogeneous integration applications.

</details>


### [109] [Magnetic Field-assisted Crystallographic Selection During Grain Growth in Non-Magnetic Polycrystals: A Phase-Field Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23473)
*Rakesh Maurya,Soumya Bandyopadhyay,Somnath Bhowmick,Rajdip Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究用相场模型（phase-field model）揭示磁场通过取向相关的磁自由能差驱动弱磁性Bi的晶界迁移与织构选择，并可在特定条件下形成双峰晶粒尺寸分布（bimodal grain-size distribution），且磁场历史可重定向粗化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非磁性体系中磁场可通过各向异性磁化率（anisotropic susceptibility）驱动晶界迁移，但局域取向如何影响宏观集体晶粒长大行为尚不明确。

Method: 采用相场模型（phase-field model）研究抗磁性Bi（diamagnetic Bi）在磁场辅助下的晶粒长大，结合双晶（bicrystal）模拟与多晶磁场扫描（field-sweep）模拟，并考察撤场与磁场重取向情形。

Result: 双晶模拟表明晶界迁移由不同取向晶粒间的磁自由能差控制；多晶模拟显示从毛细驱动粗化（capillary-dominated coarsening）向磁偏置织构选择的转变；当少数晶粒被强烈偏好时，驱动力局域化并出现双峰粗化，即使无晶界能各向异性、粒子钉扎（particle pinning）或非均匀性。

Conclusion: 研究为利用静态与动态磁场调控弱磁性材料的织构发展与晶粒长大动力学（grain growth kinetics）提供机制基础，并表明磁场历史可改变粗化路径。

Abstract: Magnetic fields drive grain boundary migration in non-magnetic systems through anisotropic susceptibility, yet how the local orientation can affect the resulting collective grain growth behavior remains unclear. We employ a phase-field model to investigate magnetic-field-assisted grain growth in the diamagnetic Bi. This study provides a mechanistic basis for using static and dynamically varied magnetic fields to control texture development and grain growth kinetics in weakly magnetic materials. We also identify the conditions under which magnetic-field-driven orientation selection produces a distinct bimodal grain-size distribution. The bicrystal studies establish that boundary migration is governed by the magnetic free-energy difference between differently oriented grains, while polycrystalline field-sweep simulations reveal a transition from capillary-dominated coarsening to magnetically biased texture selection. For unbiased orientation distributions, magnetic selection remains distributed; however, when a small population of grains is strongly favored, the driving force localizes and exhibits bimodal coarsening characterized by enlarged favored grains embedded within a fine-grained matrix, even without grain boundary energy anisotropy, particle pinning, or heterogeneity. The field removal and field reorientation simulations further show that magnetic field history can redirect the coarsening pathway.

</details>


### [110] [topas-editor and TOPilot: tools for AI-driven analysis of diffraction and total scattering data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23512)
*John S. O. Evans,Oliver J. Wagstaff,Ivana R. Evans,Arnt Kern,Alan A. Coelho*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文介绍 topas-editor 与 TOPilot 两个工具：前者是理解 TOPAS 语法的 VS Code 扩展，后者由 Python 例程与机器可读参考文档构成，借助可迁移的 topas-inp-writer 技能实现生成式 AI 驱动的 TOPAS 晶体学任务自动化，并以多类实例验证其能力与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TOPAS 软件功能强大但输入文件编写复杂，需为晶体学任务提供更友好的接口，并实现生成式 AI（generative AI）驱动的自动化分析。

Method: topas-editor 提供具晶体学感知的编辑功能、悬停提示与参考手册自动链接；TOPilot 通过 Python 例程、机器可访问（machine-accessible）参考文档及可迁移的 topas-inp-writer 技能构建 AI 自动化分析流程，兼容多种大语言模型（large language models, LLMs）。

Result: 在含样品与数据复杂因素的 Rietveld 精修（Rietveld refinement）、多数据集自动分析、从指标化（indexing）到结构解析（structure solution）再到精修的流程、物相自动识别、非公度结构（incommensurate structures）的单晶与粉末衍射分析、以及 PDF 数据拟合等实例中展示了该方法的效能与可靠性。

Conclusion: TOPilot 可与 topas-editor 配合使用，也可通过独立的 topilot-wizard、命令行接口（command line interfaces）或主流 AI 厂商的桌面应用调用；工具基于 Claude 模型开发测试但可配置其他云端或本地模型，且免费提供。

Abstract: This paper describes topas-editor and TOPilot, which provide a powerful interface to the TOPAS software suite and allow generative AI-driven automation of complex crystallographic tasks. topas-editor is a TOPAS-aware VS Code extension designed for the rapid creation and editing of TOPAS input files and for interacting with the software. It knows TOPAS syntax, has crystallographic-aware editing features and provides in-line user guidance via hover tips and automated links to reference manuals. TOPilot is a collection of Python routines and machine-accessible reference documents that allow AI-driven automated analysis in TOPAS. It uses a transferable topas-inp-writer skill that can be applied in all TOPAS application areas. We demonstrate the power and reliability of the approach using examples including: a complex Rietveld refinement with sample- and data-related complications; automatic analysis of multiple data sets; a pipeline from indexing through structure solution to refinement; automatic identification of the phases present in a sample; single crystal and powder diffraction analysis of incommensurate structures; and fitting PDF data. TOPilot can be used directly alongside topas-editor, via a stand-alone topilot-wizard, or via command line interfaces or desktop apps from major AI providers. TOPilot was developed and tested using Claude large language models, but can be configured to use other cloud or local models. topas-editor and TOPilot are freely available.

</details>


### [111] [pymatgen: 15 years of community growth, new functionality, and future prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23538)
*Janine George,Matthew K. Horton,Aaron D. Kaplan,Seán R. Kavanagh,Janosh Riebesell,Andrew S. Rosen,Haoyu Yang,Felix Adams,Mahdi Amachraa,Oxana Andriuc,Omar A. Ashour,Saurabh Bajaj,Ella Banyas,Luis Barroso-Luque,Karlo Berket,Noam Bernstein,Arpit Bhardwaj,Ali Hussain Umar Bhatti,Samuel M. Blau,Brandon Bocklund,Alexander Bonkowski,Danny Broberg,Guillaume Brunin,Kyle Bystrom,Charles A. Cardot,Rees Chang,Chi Chen,Wei Chen,Xin Chen,Yuan Chiang,Kamal Choudhary,Orion A. Cohen,Miguel D. Costa,Stephen T. Dacek,Maarten de Jong,Tom Demeyere,Alan K. Dozier,Alexander Dunn,Shyam Dwaraknath,Maxwell T. Dylla,Kristjan Eimre,Alexander R. Epstein,Christina Ertural,Marco Esters,Matthew L. Evans,Johannes Falke,Adam Fekete,Bernard Field,Alexandr Fonari,Nathan C. Frey,Dale W. Gaines,Alex M. Ganose,Matteo Giantomassi,Rhys E. A. Goodall,Petre-Flaviu Gostin,Aayush Gupta,Viet-Anh Ha,Geoffroy Hautier,Niclas Heinsdorf,Michael F. Herbst,Tingzheng Hou,Patrick Huck,Yuji Ikeda,Adam J. Jackson,Saivenkataraman Jayaraman,Finn R. Kearns,Sunghyun Kim,Ryan S. Kingsbury,Adam M. Krajewski,Yu Kumagai,Matthew C. Kuner,William T. Laderer,Weitang Li,Xiang-Guo Li,Yu Hsuan Liang,Hanyu Liu,Ke Liu,Miao Liu,Ahmad Lutfi,Olivier Marchand,Matthew J. McDermott,Bharat K. Medasani,Fanchen Meng,Kyle D. Miller,Henrique Miranda,Rohith Srinivaas Mohanakrishnan,Joseph H. Montoya,Guy C. Moore,Benjamin J. Morgan,Jason M. Munro,Koki Muraoka,Aakash A. Naik,Willis O'Leary,Anthony Onwuli,Jaesuk Park,Drew F.
