Richards,Gian-Marco Rignanese,Xiaoxu Ruan,Germain Salvato Vallverdu,Jonathan Schmidt,Jimmy-Xuan Shen,Ryuichi Shimogawa,Kohei Shinohara,Arunima K. Singh,Eric Sivonxay,Stef Smeets,Tess E. Smidt,Andrei Sobolev,Evan Walter Clark Spotte-Smith,Christopher Stihl,Wenhao Sun,Jack D. Sundberg,Yuta Suzuki,Leopold Talirz,Hanmei Tang,Eric Taw,Cooper Tezak,Félix Therrien,Scott D. Thiel,Steven B. Torrisi,Mark E. Turiansky,Katharina Ueltzen,Michiel J. van Setten,Josua Vieten,Sudarshan Vijay,Nicholas Wagner,Frank Wan,Amanda Wang,Jingyang Wang,Zhenbin Wang,Logan Ward,Thomas Warford,David Waroquiers,Mingjian Wen,Oskar Weser,Nicholas Winner,Donald Winston,Michael Wolloch,Brandon M. Wood,Rachel Woods-Robinson,Yihan Xiao,Yaoshu Xie,Firat Yalcin,Ruo Xi Yang,Weike Ye,Pezhman Zarabadi-Poor,Jinzhe Zeng,Hongsheng Zhao,Kaifeng Zheng,He Zhu,Zhuoying Zhu,Nils E. R. Zimmermann,Daniel Zügner,Kristin A. Persson,Anubhav Jain,Shyue Ping Ong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文是对Python材料基因组学库（pymatgen）的回顾性综述，总结其15年社区发展历程、开源协作模式与最佳实践，并展望其在计算材料科学与材料信息学（materials informatics）快速演变中的未来走向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pymatgen已成为最大的开源材料科学代码库之一，其长期社区驱动的演进经验具有超出自身范围的借鉴价值，因此有必要系统回顾其发展并提炼可推广的实践指导。

Method: 采用回顾性（retrospective）综述方式，梳理pymatgen开源基础与协作特性，说明其对高级计算材料科学工作流（computational materials science workflows）的支持方式及对需求变化的适应机制，并总结社区成长中的经验教训。

Result: 论文阐述了pymatgen在支持先进计算工作流方面的能力、其对变化需求的适应性，以及从不断壮大的社区中获得的经验，为pymatgen社区之外的读者提供指导；同时讨论了社区驱动演化所面临的管理挑战及潜在解决方案。

Conclusion: pymatgen将持续演进以回应计算材料科学与材料信息学快速变化的格局，论文并对其未来发展作出展望。

Abstract: We present a retrospective on the Python Materials Genomics (pymatgen) library, reflecting on 15 years of community growth and established best practices. The current version of pymatgen builds on its robust, open-source foundation, emphasising its collaborative nature. Over the past decade, pymatgen has thrived as one of the largest open-source materials science codebases. We detail how pymatgen supports advanced computational materials science workflows, how it adapts to changing demands, and the lessons learned from its growing community, providing guidance beyond pymatgen's open-source community. Pymatgen will continue to evolve in response to the current rapidly changing landscape of computational materials science and materials informatics. We discuss the challenges and potential solutions to managing the community-driven evolution of pymatgen and provide an outlook on its future development.

</details>


### [112] [Linear response for machine-learned interatomic potentials: phonons at arbitrary wave vectors from the primitive cell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23545)
*Thalis H. B. da Silva,Hai-Chen Wang,Miguel A. L. Marques*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出一种在原胞（primitive cell）内、任意波矢（wave vector）下精确获得消息传递型（message-passing）机器学习势动力学矩阵（dynamical matrix）的方法，避免有限位移超胞法的系统性误差，并在NequIP与Prophet上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声子（phonon）计算几乎都采用超胞（supercell）中的有限位移（finite displacements）方法，仅在公度波矢处严格成立，其误差取决于力常数（force constants）的作用范围；而在消息传递网络中该范围随层数增长，最深通用模型可达140 Å，故超胞误差可能显著且收敛缓慢。

Method: 借鉴密度泛函微扰理论（density-functional perturbation theory）思想，将位移用Bloch相位（Bloch phase）调制，取力对位移的线性响应（linear response）以在原胞中精确构造任意波矢的动力学矩阵；相位仅注入近邻原子特征聚合之处，训练模型无需修改。再作一次求导可解析得到群速度（group velocities）与模式Grüneisen参数（mode Grüneisen parameters）。

Result: 在NequIP与Prophet上复现超胞Hessian至机器精度（machine precision）。以该方法为参考，高通量计算常用超胞导致声子频率出现约0.1 THz量级误差，且随超胞尺寸增大收敛缓慢，深层模型尤为明显；对低对称硅酸盐（silicate），超胞还预测出模型本身并不存在的动力学不稳定性（dynamical instability）。依赖色散导数（dispersion derivatives）的量如群速度与低温Grüneisen参数受影响远大于频率本身。

Conclusion: 消息传递势的精确动力学矩阵可在原胞内以Bloch相位调制的线性响应方式获得，避免了超胞有限位移法的收敛问题；当两者用于获得相同力常数时，对具对称性的晶体其计算代价相当。

Abstract: Phonon calculations with machine-learned interatomic potentials are almost exclusively performed with finite displacements in supercells, an approach that is exact only at wave vectors commensurate with the supercell and whose error depends on the range of the force constants. In message-passing networks this range grows with the number of layers, and reaches 140 angstrom for the deepest universal models. Here we show that the dynamical matrix of a message-passing potential can be obtained exactly in the primitive cell, at any wave vector, as the linear response of the forces to a displacement modulated by a Bloch phase, in the spirit of density-functional perturbation theory. The phase enters only where the features of neighboring atoms are gathered, and the trained model is used without modification. One further derivative yields group velocities and mode Grüneisen parameters analytically. We apply the method to NequIP and Prophet, and reproduce the Hessians of supercells to machine precision. Using these results as a reference, we find that the supercells commonly employed in high-throughput calculations lead to errors of several tenths of a THz in the phonon frequencies, and that these errors decrease slowly with the size of the supercell, in particular for deep models. For a low-symmetry silicate, the supercell predicts a dynamical instability that the model does not have. Quantities that depend on derivatives of the dispersion, such as the group velocities and the Grüneisen parameter at low temperature, are considerably more affected than the frequencies themselves. When both approaches are used to obtain the same force constants, their computational cost is comparable for crystals with symmetry.

</details>


### [113] [Magnetic order in epitaxial (La2/3Sr1/3MnO3)3/(BaTiO3)3 trilayer heterostructures: Evidence for quasi-two-dimensional ferrimagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23556)
*E. Rebolini,H. Himanshu,K. Beauvois,S. Grenier,B. Mercey,B. Domenges,B. Ouladdiaf,C. Simon,M. B. Lepetit*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过逐层激光烧蚀制备高外延质量的(La2/3Sr1/3MnO3)3/(BaTiO3)3三明治异质结，结合电镜、磁化测量与中子衍射确认其晶格有序性，并揭示其室温铁磁性及约600 K的高临界温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋电子学（spintronics）领域仍缺乏兼具高外延质量与高临界温度的人工磁性结构，该工作旨在制备并阐明此类外延异质结的磁结构。

Method: 采用逐层激光烧蚀（layer-by-layer laser ablation）生长(La2/3Sr1/3MnO3)3/(BaTiO3)3三明治异质结，并用高分辨透射电子显微镜、磁化测量与中子衍射（neutron diffraction）进行表征。

Result: 电镜与磁化测量确认优异结晶有序且无结构缺陷；中子衍射未观察到反铁磁布拉格峰（antiferromagnetic Bragg peaks），排除多数非零波矢Q的反铁磁构型；磁滞回线显示室温铁磁有序，剩余磁矩约0.5 μB/Mn，可随外场呈面内或面外取向。

Conclusion: 中子衍射是解析复杂薄膜磁结构的有力工具；经BaTiO3的弱层间耦合（interlayer coupling）导致高临界温度（约600 K）的铁磁性。

Abstract: The realization of artificial magnetic structures with high epitaxial quality and elevated critical temperatures remains a key challenge for spintronics. Here, we report the fabrication and characterization of epitaxial (La2/3Sr1/3MnO3)3/(BaTiO3)3 trilayer heterostructures grown by layer-by-layer laser ablation. High-resolution transmission electron microscopy, magnetization measurements, and neutron diffraction confirm their exceptional crystalline order and the absence of structural defects. Neutron diffraction reveals no antiferromagnetic Bragg peaks, ruling out most antiferromagnetic configurations with a non zero wave vector Q. Magnetic hysteresis loops instead evidence a ferromagnetic order with a remanent moment of about 0.5 bohr magneton per Mn atom at room temperature, persisting either in-plane or out-of-plane depending on the applied field. Our findings highlight neutron diffraction as a powerful tool for elucidating the magnetic structure of complex thin films and suggest that weak interlayer coupling through BaTiO3 leads to ferromagnetism with a high critical temperature (about 600 K).

</details>


### [114] [Oxygen on Rh(100) revisited with ultra-high precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23585)
*Lutz Hammer,Tilman Kißlinger,Margareta Wagner,Reinhard B. Neder,Michael Schmid,Ulrike Diebold,M. Alexander Schneider*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合定量低能电子衍射（LEED）、变温扫描隧道显微镜（STM）与密度泛函理论（DFT），以皮米级精度重新确定了Rh(100)表面0.25与0.50单层氧相的原子结构，并解释了0.50 ML相真实周期性长期被掩盖的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rh(100)表面氧相在催化中具有重要意义，但0.25 ML与0.50 ML相的结构细节及0.50 ML相与早期(2×2)pg模型的长期分歧尚未澄清。

Method: 采用定量LEED结构分析（Pendry R因子低至约0.07）、变温STM和DFT计算，并辅以短程有序（short-range order）模拟。

Result: 0.25 ML的(2×2)-O相中氧占据四重空心位（fourfold hollow sites），且未重构衬底第二层存在明显褶皱（buckling）；0.50 ML相为(2√2×2√2)R45°-4O基态结构，在低于100 K时即分解为大量纳米畴（nano-domains）。

Conclusion: 纳米畴散射贡献的相干叠加导致衍射斑点虚拟消光（virtual extinction），从而长期掩盖了0.50 ML相的真实周期性与对称性，并解决了与原始(2×2)pg模型的分歧。

Abstract: We have reanalyzed the 0.25 and 0.50 monolayer (ML) phases of oxygen on the catalytically important Rh(100) surface by quantitative low-energy electron diffraction (LEED), variable-temperature scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) and density functional theory (DFT). For both phases, the crystallographic structure could be determined by LEED structure analyses with single-digit picometer precision enabled by ultra-low Pendry R factors of R$_\mathrm{P}$ $\approx$ 0.07. For the (2$\times$2)-O phase at 0.25 ML we find oxygen adsorbed at fourfold hollow sites in accordance to earlier results [Surf. Sci. 207 (1988) 55], however, with a pronounced buckling within the second layer of the otherwise unreconstructed substrate. For the 0.50 monolayer phase, we recently identified a (2$\sqrt{2}$ $\times$ 2$\sqrt{2}$)R45$^\circ$-4O ground state structure [Phys. Rev. Res. 8 (2026) 023296], which decomposes into a large number of nano-domains already at temperatures below 100 K. In this paper we provide a detailed analysis of the atomic structure and the ordering behavior of this phase and resolve long-standing discrepancies with the original (2$\times$2)pg model [Phys. Rev. Lett. 82 (1999) 4874]. We further prove by short-range order simulations that the coherent superposition of scattering contributions of the nano-domains leads to a virtual extinction of diffraction spots, which has concealed the true periodicity and symmetry of this phase for so long.

</details>


### [115] [Grain boundary strength and corrosion cracking susceptibility in TiN coatings under fission product segregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23627)
*Jia-Hong Ke,Rongjie Song*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过第一性原理计算，量化了Nd与Ce在bcc Fe和TiN的六种重合位置点阵晶界上的偏聚、解聚与键合行为。结果显示TiN虽不排斥裂变产物，但其晶界力学强度在偏聚后基本保持，而Fe的晶界强度显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核燃料与包壳化学相互作用（FCCI）中，镧系裂变产物（lanthanide fission products）迁移至包壳晶界（grain boundaries, GBs）并偏聚，可能削弱晶界强度并促进腐蚀开裂，因此需定量评估Nd、Ce对候选包壳材料晶界强度的影响。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）的偏聚能、解聚（decohesion）与键合（bonding）计算，在统一计算框架下评估Nd和Ce在bcc Fe与TiN各六种重合位置点阵（coincident-site lattice）晶界上的偏聚能、完全界面解理功（work of separation）及表征理想抗裂纹张开阻力的牵引-分离曲线（traction-separation curves）。

Result: Nd与Ce对Fe和TiN均发生有利偏聚，且TiN中偏聚能中位数更负，说明TiN并不简单排斥这些元素于内界面。Fe中Nd、Ce显著降低解理功与峰值牵引力（中位保留率分别0.72/0.65与0.47/0.43）；TiN中多数偏聚晶界基本保持洁净响应（解理功保留率1.00/0.97，峰值牵引力保留率均为0.85）。键合分析表明Fe中出现弱占据反键X-Fe替换键（antibonding X-Fe bonds），而TiN偏聚后Ti-N网络大体保持。

Conclusion: 结果从原子尺度支持采用TiN及相关氮化物涂层来降低FCCI损伤——即使裂变产物偏聚至涂层内界面，晶界强度仍可保留；该强度保留率亦可作为评估涂层对FCCI辅助腐蚀开裂与环境辅助沿晶断裂敏感性的机制性描述符（mechanistic descriptor）。

Abstract: During fuel-cladding chemical interaction (FCCI), lanthanide fission products generated in the fuel can migrate into cladding grain boundaries (GBs), where segregation may reduce GB strength and promote corrosion cracking. We use first-principles segregation, decohesion, and bonding calculations to quantify Nd and Ce effects on six coincident-site lattice GBs in bcc Fe and TiN. For each material, segregation energies, work-of-separation values for complete interfacial cleavage, and traction-separation curves measuring ideal resistance to crack opening were evaluated within a consistent computational framework. Nd and Ce segregate favorably to both materials, and the median segregation energies are more negative in TiN, showing that TiN does not simply exclude these species from internal interfaces. The mechanical responses of Fe and TiN differ sharply. In Fe, Nd and Ce consistently reduce work of separation and peak traction, with median work-of-separation retentions of 0.72 and 0.65 and median peak-traction retentions of 0.47 and 0.43. In TiN, most decorated GBs preserve much of the clean response, with median work-of-separation retentions of 1.00 and 0.97 and median peak-traction retention of 0.85 for both fission products. Bonding analysis shows weak, occupied antibonding X-Fe replacement bonds in Fe, whereas the surrounding Ti-N network remains largely preserved after Nd/Ce segregation in TiN. These results provide atomistic support for the use of TiN and related nitride coatings to reduce FCCI damage by retaining GB strength if fission products segregate to coating internal interfaces. This retention of GB strength also provides a mechanistic descriptor for assessing coating susceptibility to FCCI-assisted corrosion cracking and environmentally assisted intergranular fracture.

</details>


### [116] [Scalable long-range machine learning interatomic potentials with electrical response for atomistic material modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23643)
*Yicheng Chen,Xiaoyun Yu,Hyejeong Song,Xintian Wang,Zihui Li,Zixi Xia,Junmin Chen,Peichen Zhong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过将长程静电力与Born有效电荷（Born effective charges, BECs）解析地拆分为全局静电计算与学习潜电荷的严格局域导数，构建了DPA4C-LR框架，在保持等变机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potentials, MLIPs）空间可分解性的同时实现大规模分布式计算，并成功应用于红外光谱、弛豫铁电体、非晶离子导体与界面热输运等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料在外电场下的精确原子尺度模拟需要长程静电与电响应建模，但其尺度远超常规第一性原理方法；现有严格局域MLIPs忽略长程相互作用，而静电扩展又引入全局依赖，阻碍分布式计算，二者难以兼顾。

Method: 将长程静电力与BECs解析分解为全局静电计算加上学习潜电荷的严格局域导数，从而保留等变MLIPs的空间可分解性，并在\texttt{nvalchemi}平台上以DPA4C-LR实现，支持不同边界条件下能量、力、应力与BECs的分布式评估。

Result: 在四块GPU上扩展至520万原子，计算开销约为其短程骨干的两倍；仅用能量与力训练即可准确推断BECs并重现水的场依赖红外光谱；再现了弛豫铁电体Pb(Mg_{1/3}Nb_{2/3})O3被小尺寸有限尺寸效应掩盖的细瘦极化滞回，预测非晶Li_{0.9}NbO_{0.9}Cl_{4.1}室温场驱动离子电导率为6.8±0.7 mS/cm，并借助平板修正静电建模Si/金刚石界面热输运。

Conclusion: 该框架将长程物理、电响应推断与分布式计算相结合，把预测性原子尺度建模拓展至器件相关尺度，为大规模电场下材料模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Accurate atomistic simulations of materials under electric fields require long-range electrostatics and electrical-response modeling at length scales beyond the reach of conventional first-principles methods. Yet these capabilities remain difficult to reconcile with the scalability of machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs). Strictly local models enable efficient spatial parallelization but neglect long-range interactions, whereas existing electrostatic extensions introduce global dependencies that hinder distributed computation. Here, we overcome this limitation by analytically separating the long-range electrostatic forces and Born effective charges (BECs) into a global electrostatics calculation and strictly local derivatives of learned latent charges. This formulation preserves the spatial decomposability of equivariant MLIPs while enabling distributed evaluation of energies, forces, stresses, and BECs across different boundary conditions. Implemented as DPA4C-LR on the \texttt{nvalchemi} platform, the framework scales to 5.2 million atoms on four GPUs at approximately twice the computational cost of its short-range backbone. Trained on energies and forces, DPA4C-LR accurately infers BECs and reproduces the field-dependent infrared spectra of water. We further demonstrate its capabilities by recovering the characteristic slim polarization hysteresis of relaxor \ce{Pb(Mg_{1/3}Nb_{2/3})O3}, which is obscured by finite-size effects in smaller simulations; predicting a room-temperature field-driven ionic conductivity of $6.8 \pm 0.7$~mS/cm in amorphous \ce{Li_{0.9}NbO_{0.9}Cl_{4.1}}; and modeling interfacial thermal transport across Si/diamond interfaces with slab-corrected electrostatics. By combining long-range physics, electrical-response inference, and distributed computation, this framework extends predictive atomistic modeling toward device-relevant length scales.

</details>


### [117] [Learning the self-energy through equivariant neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23717)
*Wudong Shen,Yang Zhong,Min-Ye Zhang,Xinyang Dong,Hongjun Xiang,Xinguo Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出以E(3)等变消息传递网络从原子种类与坐标直接预测分子在虚频上的G_0W_0自能矩阵（动力学关联部分），并通过保因果、保非负谱密度的表示延拓到实轴，从而在无需显式G_0W_0计算的情况下获得谱函数与准粒子能级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理计算电子自能（electronic self-energy）代价高昂，且其作为机器学习目标具有复杂性：复数值、局域轨道基下的矩阵值、并依赖连续频率。因此需要一种高效且物理一致的替代方案。

Method: 采用E(3)等变消息传递网络（E(3)-equivariant message-passing network），由架构本身施加轨道空间矩阵的旋转协变性，静态交换部分取自密度泛函参考；频率或以显式网络输入、或以频率专用输出头引入；预测矩阵在一种按构造满足因果性（causality）与非负谱密度的表示中延拓至实轴。

Result: 频率条件模型在所研究体系虚轴上的矩阵元误差低于1 meV，且该精度在延拓后仍保持：对水团簇（包括训练集之外的四聚体）及QM9中化学多样性分子，重建谱函数逐峰吻合G_0W_0参考，前沿准粒子能级恢复到数十meV量级。

Conclusion: 在虚轴学习自能是通往实频谱的可行路径：模型训练完成后，在给定其依赖的密度泛函参考的条件下，新几何结构无需显式G_0W_0计算即可获得结果。

Abstract: The electronic self-energy determines the single-particle Green's function and, through it, quasiparticle energies and spectral functions, but computing it from first principles is expensive and it is a difficult machine-learning target: complex-valued, matrix-valued in a local-orbital basis, and dependent on a continuous frequency. We predict the dynamical correlation part of the imaginary-frequency G_0W_0 self-energy matrix of molecules from atomic species and coordinates with an E(3)-equivariant message-passing network, the rotational covariance of the orbital-space matrix being imposed by the architecture itself and the static exchange part taken from the density-functional reference. Frequency enters either as an explicit network input or through frequency-specific output heads, and the predicted matrices are continued to the real axis in a representation in which causality and a non-negative spectral density hold by construction. The frequency-conditioned model reproduces matrix elements to below 1 meV on the imaginary axis for the systems studied here, and that accuracy survives the continuation: for water clusters, including a tetramer absent from the training set, and for chemically diverse molecules from QM9, the reconstructed spectral functions follow the G_0W_0 reference peak by peak and the frontier quasiparticle levels are recovered to a few tens of meV. Learning the self-energy on the imaginary axis is therefore a practical route to real-frequency spectra: once the model is trained, and given the density-functional reference it is built on, a new geometry costs no explicit G_0W_0 calculation.

</details>


### [118] [Magnetic tunnel junctions of twisted La2/3Sr1/3MnO3 freestanding bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23720)
*Kota Kanda,Shiho Irie,Kodai Niitsu,Masayuki Ochi,Takumi Yamazaki,Kouta Kondou,Kohei Ueda,Takeshi Seki,Jobu Matsuno,Junichi Shiogai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用水溶性牺牲层的外延剥离与干法转移技术，将半金属铁磁金属La2/3Sr1/3MnO3的独立薄膜堆叠并扭转，以范德华间隙作为隧穿势垒构建磁隧道结，观测到隧穿磁电阻与隧穿各向异性磁电阻效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属氧化物（transition metal oxides, TMOs）中晶格、轨道、电荷与自旋自由度的耦合带来丰富功能，自由薄膜堆叠与扭转技术为研究此类强关联氧化物的扭角电子学提供了新手段；但迄今TMO扭转结主要局限于绝缘体，聚焦结构与介电性质，金属性铁磁体系的扭转结输运研究尚属空白。

Method: 采用水溶性牺牲层辅助的外延剥离（epitaxial lift-off）与干法转移（dry transfer）技术，制备La2/3Sr1/3MnO3扭转双层独立薄膜结，并以两层膜之间的范德华（van der Waals, vdW）间隙作为隧穿势垒，构筑磁隧道结（magnetic tunnel junction, MTJ），测量其隧穿输运行为。

Result: La2/3Sr1/3MnO3扭转结表现出依赖于上下薄膜磁化相对取向的隧穿磁电阻（tunnelling magnetoresistance, TMR）效应；高场磁电阻呈现单轴各向异性，可归因于反映完全弛豫La2/3Sr1/3MnO3菱方畸变的隧穿各向异性磁电阻（tunnelling anisotropic magnetoresistance, TAMR）效应。

Conclusion: TMR与TAMR的观测表明，3d电子的自旋与面内动量自由度在TMO扭转结间得以保持（守恒），为借助隧穿过程中的电子相干性实现强关联扭角电子学（strongly correlated twistronics）提供了新途径。

Abstract: Transition metal oxides (TMOs) host a variety of emergent spintronic or multiferroic functionalities owing to the intricate interplay between lattice, orbital, charge, and spin degrees of freedom. Recent advances in epitaxial lift-off and dry transfer techniques using water-soluble sacrificial layers enables stacking and twisting of freestanding membranes of such strongly correlated oxides, providing even richer physical properties and functionalities. To date, the twisted junctions of TMOs have been mainly investigated in insulators, focusing on their structural and dielectric properties. Here, we demonstrate that the twisted bilayer membranes of La2/3Sr1/3MnO3, a prototypical half-metallic ferromagnetic metal TMO, offer a magnetic tunnel junction (MTJ) with their van der Waals (vdW) gap acting as a tunnel barrier. The twisted junctions of La2/3Sr1/3MnO3 exhibit tunnelling magnetoresistance (TMR) effect with respect to relative orientation of magnetizations of top and bottom membranes. In addition, high-field magnetoresistance shows uniaxial anisotropy, which can be assigned to tunnelling anisotropic magnetoresistance (TAMR) effect reflecting rhombohedral distortion of fully-relaxed La2/3Sr1/3MnO3. The demonstration of TMR and TAMR effects suggests that spin and in-plane momentum degrees of freedom of 3d electrons are conserved across the twisted junctions of TMOs, opening a new avenue for realizing strongly correlated twistronics utilizing electron coherence through tunnelling processes.

</details>


### [119] [Intercalation of Ultrathin Ferromagnetic Iron Films at the Graphene/SiC Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23728)
*Niels Rösch,Rico Ehrler,Stefanie Suzanne Brinkman,Xin Liang Tan,Anders Christian Mathisen,Marc Stevens,Sascha Hermann,Olav Hellwig,Philip Schädlich,Hendrik Bentmann,Susanne Wolff,Thomas Seyller,Fabian Göhler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究探究了在4H-SiC(0001)上由PASG法生长的零层石墨烯（zero-layer graphene）中实现铁（Fe）插层（intercalation）的条件，发现Fe插层需与硅化铁及石墨碳生成相竞争，成功插层后的超薄Fe膜受石墨烯保护且保持室温铁磁性；但富硒气氛下Fe几乎被Se完全置换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在考察Fe这一特殊金属在零层石墨烯中的插层行为，并评估插层金属膜的稳定性及其对后续二维化合物构建的适用性。

Method: 采用沉积-退火（deposition-and-annealing）方法对高质量PASG生长的零层石墨烯进行Fe插层，并在高温下将其暴露于富硒（Se）气氛中以考察元素置换行为。

Result: Fe不易插层，插层过程须与界面处硅化铁（iron silicides）和石墨碳（graphitic carbon）的形成相平衡；插层成功后超薄Fe膜被石墨烯覆盖而抗氧化，并在室温下保持面内易轴（in-plane easy axis）的铁磁性；富硒高温条件下Fe几乎被Se完全置换。

Conclusion: Fe插层成功实现了受石墨烯保护的室温铁磁超薄膜，但Se对Fe的近乎完全置换对通过顺序插层（sequential intercalation）方案构建多元素二维化合物构成重要限制。

Abstract: The intercalation of high-quality PASG-grown (polymer-assisted sublimation growth) zero-layer graphene on 4H-SiC(0001) with iron is studied using a deposition-and-annealing approach. We find that unlike other metals, Fe does not easily intercalate, and the intercalation procedure has to be balanced with the formation of iron silicides and graphitic carbon at the interface. After successful intercalation, the ultrathin iron films are protected against oxidation by the covering graphene sheet, and retain ferromagnetic behaviour with an in-plane easy axis even at room temperature. When exposed to a selenium-rich atmosphere at elevated temperatures, an almost complete exchange of Fe by Se is observed, imposing a major restriction for the formation of multi-element 2D compounds via sequential intercalation schemes.

</details>


### [120] [Analysis of Transient Microscopy Data in Reciprocal Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23756)
*Thomas J. Sheehan,William A. Tisdale*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于倒空间（reciprocal space）中空间频率（spatial frequency）分布演化来分析瞬态显微（transient microscopy）数据的方法，并通过模拟数据集证明其在常规扩散输运中相比均方位移（mean squared displacement, MSD）拟合方法具有更高的准确度与精密度，同时更适用于表征反常输运（anomalous transport）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有瞬态显微数据分析通常依赖对均方位移（MSD）随时间变化进行曲线拟合（curve-fitting）来提取扩散系数（diffusivity），但在许多常见实验条件下会给出不准确或误导性的结果，且仅在单一扩散组分的严格情形下成立。

Method: 论文引入基于倒空间（reciprocal space）中分布形状演化的新分析方法，利用模拟数据集（simulated datasets）定量评估其性能，并与当前最先进（state-of-the-art）的分析技术进行对比。

Result: 在一系列贴近实际的初始条件与信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）下，基于倒空间的分析方法在常规扩散输运的准确度（accuracy）与精密度（precision）上总体表现最佳；同时揭示了仅用 MSD 表征材料中反常输运（anomalous transport）的局限，并说明空间频率分析可更全面地理解复杂激发态动力学（excited-state dynamics）及其物理机制。

Conclusion: 基于倒空间的分析方法是表征与量化多种输运行为的有力工具，可克服传统 MSD 拟合方法在多组分与反常输运情形下的不足。

Abstract: Transient microscopy has evolved to include a family of well-established techniques for studying the spatiotemporal dynamics of excited states in a variety of materials systems. In these methods, a laser pulse is focused to locally excite a material, and the subsequent evolution of the excited species is visualized to extract transport parameters such as diffusivities. Typically, transport coefficients are extracted from experimental datasets using curve-fitting methods to analyze the change in the mean squared displacement (MSD) of the spatial profiles over time. However, this approach can lead to inaccurate or misleading diffusivity values under many common experimental conditions, and is only formally accurate for the case of a single diffusing species. In this work, we introduce a new approach to the analysis of transient microscopy data based on the evolution of profile shapes in reciprocal space. We use simulated datasets to quantitatively analyze the performance of our proposed methods and compare them to current state-of-the-art analysis techniques. We demonstrate that reciprocal-space-based analysis methods have the highest overall performance in terms of accuracy and precision for normal diffusive transport over a range of realistic initial conditions and signal-to-noise ratios. Furthermore, we highlight the limitations of using MSD alone to characterize anomalous transport in materials, and we show how analysis of spatial frequencies can yield a more comprehensive understanding of complex excited-state dynamics and their underlying physical mechanisms. Our results show that reciprocal-space-based analysis methods are powerful tools for characterizing and quantifying many different types of transport behaviors.

</details>


### [121] [Temperature and Thickness Dependent Spin Current Transmission through Epitaxial Antiferromagnetic α-Fe2O3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23764)
*Koushik Prabhakara,Rajeev Nepal,Subhashree Chatterjee,Rabindra Basnet,Ezana Negusse,Ramesh C Budhani*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过自旋泵浦（spin pumping）与逆自旋霍尔效应（inverse spin Hall effect）测量Py/α-Fe2O3/Pt结构中的自旋电流透射（spin-current transmission），发现对称电压系数Ks在特征温度T*两侧呈近似线性温区，且T*与α-Fe2O3的Morin转变相关，表明自旋输运对绝缘反铁磁层磁状态敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-Fe2O3作为反铁磁绝缘体（antiferromagnetic insulator）有望支持自旋波（spin waves）长距离相干传播以实现无耗散信息传输，但其结构缺陷与随温度变化的自旋重取向（spin reorientation）会影响传播，因此需厘清反铁磁绝缘体的磁状态如何影响自旋电流透射。

Method: 制备Permalloy/α-Fe2O3/Pt三明治结构的外延α-Fe2O3薄膜（厚度0、30、40、100 nm），利用自旋泵浦与逆自旋霍尔效应在1.6–300 K范围测量Pt层上的对称直流电压，并对部分样品进行直流磁化测量。

Result: 在所有厚度与整个温区内均观测到有限的对称直流电压；对称电压系数Ks存在两个近似线性的温度区间，由厚度依赖的特征温度T*分隔，且T*与Morin转变（Morin transition）相关联；在T*以下dKs/dT的幅度显著更大。

Conclusion: 自旋电流透射随厚度与温度的变化表明其对赤铁矿绝缘层的磁状态具有敏感性，为理解反铁磁绝缘体中自旋输运机制提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic insulator α-Fe2O3 is a potential medium for coherent motion of spin waves over relatively large distances, allowing non-dissipative transfer of information between circuit elements. However, the structural defects and temperature dependent spin reorientations in this oxide impact the propagation. Understanding how the magnetic state of an antiferromagnetic insulator affects spin-current transmission is therefore important for elucidating the mechanisms governing spin transport. Here, we report spin-current transmission through epitaxial α-Fe2O3 thin films sandwiched between Permalloy and Pt. Using spin pumping and inverse spin Hall effect, we observe a finite symmetric dc voltage across the Pt layer over the temperature range 1.6 to 300 K for alpha-Fe2O3 thicknesses of 0, 30, 40, and 100 nm. The symmetric voltage coefficient Ks exhibits two approximately linear temperature regimes, separated by a characteristic temperature T* whose value depends on the thickness of the α-Fe2O3 layer. Further, the dc magnetization measurements on some of the samples suggest that the T* is correlated with the spin reorientation transition (Morin transition) in α-Fe2O3. Interestingly, a significantly larger magnitude of dKs/dT is noted below the characteristic temperature T*. The observed thickness and temperature dependent spin-current transmission indicates a sensitivity to the magnetic state of the insulating hematite layer.

</details>


### [122] [Ionic switching between supermagnetic states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23829)
*Sumaiyatul Ahsan,Yin-Ju Yen,Andrew H. Comstock,Tanya A. Balandin,Jason D. Azoulay,Jamie H. Warner,Dario A. Arena,Faisal M. Alamgir*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作首次演示了无需重新合成或持续外场、仅靠室温锂离子输运即可在超铁磁（superferromagnetic, SFM）与超自旋玻璃（superspin-glass, SSG）态之间实现可逆磁离子切换（magneto-ionic switching）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超磁态（supermagnetic states）通常在材料合成阶段被设定，改变并保持这些状态需要持续的外部激励，缺乏可逆、低能耗的调控手段。

Method: 利用NiO向纳米尺度Ni与Li2O的不完全锂化（incomplete lithiation）过程，通过室温Li离子输运在SFM与SSG两态之间切换；锂化生成碎片化纳米Ni网络，脱锂（delithiation）则部分再生NiO。

Result: 锂化形成的碎片化纳米Ni网络增强偶极相互作用（dipolar interactions），促进SFM序，表现为更强记忆效应与更高室温磁矩；脱锂驱动体系进入SSG态，具有更高低温矫顽力（coercivity）与更尖锐的温度选择性记忆。

Conclusion: SFM与SSG之间的相互转换在后续所有测量的锂化—脱锂循环中均可保持，为超磁切换提供了通用框架（general framework）。

Abstract: Supermagnetic states are typically set during synthesis. Changing and retaining these states requires sustained external stimulus. Without resynthesis or continued stimulus, we show, for the first time, a reversible magneto-ionic switching between superferromagnetic (SFM) and superspin-glass (SSG) states using room-temperature Li-ion transport. We utilize the incomplete lithiation of NiO into nanoscale Ni and Li2O to switch between the states. Lithiation generates a fragmented nano-Ni network that strengthens dipolar interactions and promotes SFM order with stronger memory and higher room-temperature moments. Delithiation partially regenerates NiO and drives the system into an SSG state with higher low-temperature coercivity and sharper temperature-selective memory. The interconversion between SFM and SSG persists at all measured subsequent lithiation-delithiation cycles, creating a general framework for supermagnetic switching.

</details>


<div id='math.DS'></div>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Finiteness of planar central configurations of six bodies for all positive masses: Part I, exceptional-mass first limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23329)
*Zhe Chen,Zhicheng Tong*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文处理平面六体中心构型在一般正质量情形下的有限性问题，将其与例外质量轨迹上的有限性区分开，并对 Chang--Chen 图枚举中的 40 个一阶极限图义务给出归约到必要质量轨迹的论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面六体中心构型（central configurations）的正质量有限性问题具有一般性，但与例外质量轨迹（exceptional mass loci）上的情形混杂，需要先分离出通用情形以简化论证。

Method: 基于 Chang--Chen 图枚举（diagram enumeration）的 117 个图，将 40 个一阶极限图义务（first-limit diagram obligations）限制到适当的必要质量轨迹；用直接函数域论证（function-field argument）建立第 66 号图在零与非零漂移下的质量条件；并借助图规则与计算证书（computational certificates）完成从无限正族到无界七乘积芽（germ with an unbounded seven-product）的代数归约。

Result: 证明第 66 号图的质量条件在零漂移与非零漂移两种情形下均成立，并将相关质量向量限制到这些轨迹的并集之中，从而把一般正质量有限性问题归约到例外正质量轨迹。

Conclusion: 例外正质量轨迹上的有限性被留待第二部分（Part II）另行讨论；本文完成了通用情形的分离与相应归约。

Abstract: We separate the generic positive-mass finiteness problem for planar six-body central configurations from finiteness on exceptional mass loci. The Chang--Chen diagram enumeration has 117 graphs. The arguments below confine 40 first-limit diagram obligations to proper necessary mass loci. In particular, a direct function-field argument establishes the No.~66 mass condition for both zero and nonzero drift. We establish the algebraic reduction from an infinite positive family to a germ with an unbounded seven-product, and use the stated diagram rules and computational certificates to confine its mass vector to the union of these loci. Finiteness on the exceptional positive mass loci, the proposed subject of Part II, will be discussed in another paper.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [EEG Lempel-Ziv Complexity vis-à-vis Autocorrelation Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23231)
*Pedro Montoya,Ignacio Cifre,José Luis Rosselló Sanz,Dante R. Chialvo*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究重新审视归一化Lempel-Ziv复杂度（normalized Lempel-Ziv complexity, LZC）与时间自相关（autocorrelation）之间的关系，表明LZC所反映的信息本质上是被传统自相关度量已捕捉的时间冗余（temporal redundancy），因此在解释基于复杂度的脑电发现时应显式考虑其与传统时间统计量的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号复杂度（signal complexity）度量在脑电（electroencephalography, EEG）中日益流行，常被解读为神经信息处理或意识状态的指标，但其生理可解释性较弱；相比之下，自相关函数（autocorrelation function, ACF）等经典时间依赖度量与已知生理机制联系更直接、更易解释。作者希望厘清二者关系，以提供更透明的机制推断框架。

Method: 首先使用具有不同时间依赖程度的随机过程（stochastic processes）生成覆盖广泛时间结构与复杂度水平的合成时间序列，计算并比较LZC与自相关度量；随后在健康成人静息态EEG（跨通道、跨被试）以及睁眼/闭眼（eyes open/close）动态过程中验证该关系。

Result: 在合成信号中，归一化LZC与一阶自相关系数（first autocorrelation coefficient）之间呈现稳健的负相关关系，即时间相关性越强的信号其复杂度估计越低；该关系在健康成人静息态EEG的跨通道、跨被试数据中成立，并在睁眼/闭眼动态过程中同样成立。

Conclusion: LZC所捕捉的信息反映了传统自相关度量已量化的时间冗余；鉴于自相关度量与振荡持续性（oscillatory persistence）、网络同步（network synchronization）和时间整合（temporal integration）等神经生理机制联系更直接，解释基于复杂度的EEG结果时应显式考虑其对传统时间统计量的依赖，从而获得更透明的机制推断框架。

Abstract: Measures of signal complexity have become increasingly popular in electroencephalography (EEG), where they are often interpreted as indicators of neural information processing, dynamical richness, or conscious state. In contrast, classical measures of temporal dependence, such as the autocorrelation function (ACF), remain more directly linked to known physiological mechanisms and are easier to interpret. The present work revisits, for the EEG practicioner, the relation between normalized Lempel-Ziv complexity (LZC) and temporal autocorrelation in both synthetic and physiological EEG signals. First, synthetic time series exhibiting a broad range of temporal structure and complexity levels were generated using stochastic processes with varying degrees of temporal dependence. LZC and autocorrelation measures were then computed and compared. Across simulated signals, a robust inverse relationship was observed between normalized LZC and the first autocorrelation coefficient, indicating that signals with stronger temporal correlations exhibit lower complexity estimates. This relationship also holds in resting-state EEG recordings from healthy adult participants across channels and subjects, as well as dynamically during eyes open/close maneuvers. Overall, these results show that the information captured by LZC reflects temporal redundancy already quantified by conventional autocorrelation measures. Because autocorrelation measures relate more directly to established neurophysiological mechanisms, including oscillatory persistence, network synchronization, and temporal integration, these results suggest that interpreting complexity-based EEG findings should explicitly account for their dependence on conventional temporal statistics, which may provide a more transparent framework for mechanistic inferences.

</details>


<div id='nucl-ex'></div>

# nucl-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Proton-Number Fluctuations in $^{238}$U+$^{238}$U Collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}} = 2.1$ GeV with the CEE Fast Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23460)
*Shuaike Lv,Fan Si,Xionghong He,Xu Sun,Yige Huang,Wanbing He,Yifei Zhang,Ming Shao,Ziyang Li*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 基于 JAM2 模型与 CEE 快速模拟，评估了 $^{238}$U+$^{238}$U 碰撞中质子数高阶累积量的测量可行性与统计需求；经逐径迹效率修正与方位角接受度补偿后，修正累积量在误差范围内与生成器真值一致，但接受度补偿带来显著统计代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CEE 实验为高重子密度区 QCD 相图（QCD phase diagram）研究提供独特条件，而逐事件质子数分布的高阶累积量（higher-order cumulants）被视为核物质相结构的敏感探针，因此需评估其分析可行性与统计要求。

Method: 用 JAM2 模型生成 $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=2.1$ GeV 的 U+U 碰撞，经含探测器响应的 CEE 快速模拟（fast simulation）处理，并实施中心度判定（centrality determination）与粒子鉴别（particle identification）等实验分析流程；计算至四阶的质子数累积量及其比值，测试 TPC 接受度内的逐径迹效率修正（track-by-track efficiency correction），并引入附加因子补偿双 TPC 间方位角间隙导致的接受度损失。

Result: 在当前数据样本的不确定度范围内，修正后的累积量及累积量比值与生成器层面（generator-level）数值一致；进一步研究了累积量比值不确定度对事件样本的依赖，并据此估算未来测量所需统计量，发现接受度补偿（acceptance compensation）伴随可观的统计成本。

Conclusion: 结果为 CEE 上质子数涨落（proton-number fluctuation）测量的分析可行性与统计需求提供了基于模拟的评估。

Abstract: The Cooling Storage Ring External-target Experiment (CEE) at the Heavy Ion Research Facility in Lanzhou (HIRFL) offers a unique opportunity to map the QCD phase diagram in the high-baryon-density region. In particular, higher-order cumulants ($C_r$) of event-by-event proton number distributions have been proposed as sensitive probes of the nuclear-matter phase structure. In this work, $^{\text{238}}$U+$^{\text{238}}$U collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}} = 2.1$ GeV are generated using the JAM2 model and then processed through the CEE fast simulation with detector responses. Experimental analysis procedures, including centrality determination and particle identification, are also implemented. Proton-number cumulants up to fourth order and their ratios are calculated and compared with the true values. A track-by-track efficiency correction method is tested by introducing reconstruction efficiency within the TPC acceptance. An additional factor is also included to account for acceptance losses caused by the azimuthal gap between the two TPCs. Within the uncertainties of the current data sample, the corrected cumulants and cumulant ratios are consistent with their generator-level values. The event-sample dependence of the cumulant-ratio uncertainties is further investigated to estimate the statistics required for future measurements, revealing a substantial statistical cost associated with the acceptance compensation. These results provide a simulation-based assessment of the analysis feasibility and statistical requirements for proton-number fluctuation measurements at CEE.

</details>


### [126] [Unexpectedly large violation of isospin symmetry in nucleus-nucleus collisions at CERN SPS energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23498)
*Andrzej Rybicki for the NA61/SHINE Collaboration*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: NA61/SHINE在CERN SPS的0-10%中心Ar+Sc碰撞中观测到带电与中性K介子产额比显著超出理论预期，表现为异常大的同位旋对称性破缺，且在2.6-200 GeV能区与其他实验数据在较大不确定度内一致，目前缺乏理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论模型中的已知机制无法解释中心Ar+Sc碰撞中带电K介子相对中性K介子的过量产生，该现象涉及对粒子产生机制与同位旋对称性（isospin symmetry）的基本理解，因而需要实验上系统确认其普遍性。

Method: 利用CERN SPS上的NA61/SHINE实验，测量0-10%中心Ar+Sc碰撞在√s_NN=8.8与11.9 GeV下的带电与中性K介子包容产生（inclusive production），并与其它实验数据及理论模型预测进行对比；同时补充最小偏差（minimum bias）π^-+C反应在√s_NN=17.3与25.7 GeV下的测量。

Result: 在2.6<√s_NN<200 GeV能区，多个实验数据在较大不确定度内显示出一致的同位旋对称性破缺导致的带电K介子过量；LHC能区（0.9<√s_NN<13 TeV）带电/中性K介子比与1相容但不确定度仍较大；π^-+C反应中也观测到类似过量，且与模型预测不符。

Conclusion: 迄今尚无明确的理论解释；异常大的同位旋对称性破缺可能对粒子产生（particle production）的理解产生重要影响，需实验与理论的集中协同研究。

Abstract: The NA61/SHINE experiment at the CERN SPS reports an unexpectedly large excess in the inclusive production of charged over neutral kaons in 0-10% central Ar+Sc collisions at $\sqrt{s_\mathrm{NN}}=8.8$ and 11.9 GeV. This effect corresponds to an unexpectedly large violation of isospin symmetry, that cannot be explained by known mechanisms contained in the present theoretical models. The comparison of the NA61/SHINE results to data from other experiments reveals, within sizeable uncertainties, a consistent isospin symmetry-violating excess of charged kaon production in the energy regime $2.6<\sqrt{s_\mathrm{NN}}<200$ GeV. At LHC energies ($0.9<\sqrt{s_\mathrm{NN}}<13$ TeV) the charged-over-neutral kaon ratio appears consistent with unity, but the uncertainties remain sizeable. A similar excess of charged over neutral kaon yields is observed by NA61/SHINE in minimum bias $π^-$+C reactions at $\sqrt{s_\mathrm{NN}}=17.3$ and 25.7 GeV, also in disagreement with model predictions. No clear theoretical explanation of these phenomena has been provided so far. An unexpectedly large violation of isospin symmetry brings potential heavy consequences for our understanding of particle production, and calls for a concentrated experimental and theoretical effort.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [General Relativity in Three Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22396)
*Pawel Nurowski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明复广义相对论（complex general relativity）可直接用每时空点三个仿射直线（affine lines）表述：直线由Plücker双矢量表示，经Urbantke构造重构共形度规，并由chiral Plebański作用量经$3+1$分解得出一阶约束理论，其Legendre映射在零演化轨迹处奇异但可构造光滑的二阶延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是建立一种以直线（而非度规或联络）为基本变量的引力表述，为演化方向变为零（null）的情形（如近视界几何）提供正则框架。

Method: 以Plücker双矢量（Plücker bivectors）表示三个仿射直线，借助Urbantke构造由相应二形式（two-forms）重构共形时空度规；从chiral Plebański作用量出发，经单纯性约束（simplicity constraints）将三个二形式化为简单双矢量的零内基，再做$3+1$分解得到以加权三直线为位形变量、以线动量为共轭变量的一阶正则理论。

Result: 约束系统含三个内Gauss约束、三个矢量约束与一个Hamiltonian约束；对线动量变分所得方程在分部积分后逐点代数化，可在Legendre映射可逆处显式消去动量，得到仅以加权三直线为动力学变量的二阶作用量。Legendre映射在度规仍非退化的零演化轨迹（null-evolution locus）上奇异，秩由九降为六，给出三个代数相容性条件，并可构造有限不变的部分Legendre变换与光滑约束二阶延拓。

Conclusion: 该直线表述为演化方向变为零的情形提供了正则框架，并可能有助于近视界几何及相关引力现象的研究。

Abstract: We show that complex general relativity can be formulated directly in terms of three affine lines assigned to each spacetime point. The lines are represented by Plücker bivectors, and Urbantke's construction applied to the associated two-forms reconstructs the conformal spacetime metric. Starting from the chiral Plebański action, the simplicity constraints turn the three two-forms into a null internal basis of simple bivectors and thereby into three projective lines. A $3+1$ decomposition then yields a first-order canonical theory whose configuration variable is the weighted triple of lines and whose conjugate variable is a line momentum. The resulting constraint system consists of three internal Gauss constraints, three vector constraints, and one Hamiltonian constraint.
  A notable feature of the line formulation is that the equation obtained by varying the line momentum is pointwise algebraic after the spatial integrations by parts. Consequently the momentum can be eliminated explicitly wherever the corresponding Legendre map is invertible, giving a second-order action whose only dynamical configuration variable is the weighted triple of lines. The line curvature separates naturally into a cosmological trace and the Weyl matrix of the selected chiral sector. The Legendre map becomes singular on a distinguished null-evolution locus where the spacetime metric remains nondegenerate. There its rank drops from nine to six, giving three algebraic compatibility conditions. We construct a finite invariant partial Legendre transform and a smooth constrained second-order continuation through this locus. This analysis provides a regular framework for regimes in which the evolution direction becomes null and may be useful in the study of near-horizon geometries and related gravitational phenomena.

</details>


### [128] [Asymptotic evaluation of multidimensional oscillatory integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22767)
*Samuel Crew,Job Feldbrugge*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一种代数方法评估振荡积分（oscillatory integrals）的渐近展开，通过构造有限维高斯-曼宁系统（Gauss-Manin system）及其博雷尔变换（Borel transform）确定斯托克斯常数（Stokes constants）与鞍点邻接关系，无需计算高维梯度流即可实现超渐近与超超渐近近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡积分广泛出现在物理各领域，其渐近求值需判断哪些实与复鞍点（saddle points）有贡献及斯托克斯现象（Stokes phenomena）如何连接它们；多维情形传统上需在高维复空间计算最速下降流形（steepest descent manifolds），代价高昂。

Method: 针对多项式与有理指数积分，利用分部积分（integration by parts）与 Brieskorn 约化（Brieskorn reduction）构造生成所有鞍点渐近展开的有限维 Gauss-Manin 系统；其 Borel 变换给出 Fuchsian 系统，由单值性（monodromy）确定 Stokes 常数与鞍点邻接，再结合 South-East rule 识别贡献鞍点。

Result: 以抛物脐点衍射积分（parabolic umbilic diffraction integral）与洛伦兹透镜（Lorentzian lens）为例验证，结果与机器精度一致，实现了超渐近（superasymptotic）与超超渐近（hyperasymptotic）近似。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了无需计算梯度流的代数替代方案，并可复现；相关方法实现于开源 Julia 包 Resurgence.jl。

Abstract: Oscillatory integrals appear throughout physics, from diffraction and gravitational lensing to real-time path integrals. Their asymptotic evaluation is complicated by the need to determine which real and complex saddle points contribute, and how they are connected by Stokes phenomena. In multiple dimensions, this traditionally requires computing steepest descent manifolds in a high-dimensional complex space. Here, we develop an algebraic alternative for integrals with polynomial and rational exponents. Using integration by parts and Brieskorn reduction, we construct a finite-dimensional Gauss-Manin system that generates the asymptotic expansion of all saddle points. Its Borel transform yields a Fuchsian system, whose mondromy determines the Stokes constants and hence the adjacency of the saddle points. Combined with the recent South-East rule, this identifies the contributing saddles and enables superasymptotic and hyperasymptotic approximations without computing gradient flows. We demonstate the method for the parabolic umbilic diffraction integral and a Lorentzian lens as examples, obtaining agreement to machine precision. The methods are implemented in an accompanying open-source Julia package Resurgence.jl

</details>


### [129] [Folding to the Bessel-Clifford system, and Equivariant Topological Recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23320)
*Mohamad Alameddine,Alexander Hock*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究亚纯联络（meromorphic connections）的对称性约化、折叠（folding）与拓扑递归（topological recursion）之间的相互作用，构造了克莱因四元群（Klein four-group）对称谱曲线上的等变拓扑递归，并证明其关联子可通过折叠化为商谱曲线上的普通拓扑递归关联子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在建立可积系统（integrable systems）的对称性约化与拓扑递归中的折叠操作之间的联系，并以 Bessel-Clifford 系统作为动机性示例说明该联系的具体实现。

Method: 将 Bessel-Clifford 系统实现为 Painlevé IV 层级第 0 个成员（0-member）的对称不变子空间的折叠；引入带 Z₂×Z₂ 对称性的谱曲线上的等变拓扑递归（equivariant topological recursion），并论证其等变关联子（equivariant correlators）可折叠为商谱曲线上的普通关联子，同时验证该折叠与波函数（wave functions）和量子曲线（quantum curves）的相容性。

Result: 证明了等变关联子经折叠得到商谱曲线上的普通拓扑递归关联子，且该折叠与波函数、量子曲线相容；在 Bessel-Clifford 例子中给出了等变波函数与折叠波函数之间的显式关系，并恢复了相应的量子曲线约化。

Conclusion: 研究结果为将可积系统的对称性约化与拓扑递归中的折叠操作联系起来提供了一个框架。

Abstract: We study the interplay between symmetry reductions of meromorphic connections, folding, and topological recursion. As a motivating example, we realize the Bessel-Clifford system as a folding of a symmetry-invariant subspace of the $0-$member of the Painlevé IV hierarchy. We introduce equivariant topological recursion for spectral curves with a Klein four-group $\mathbb Z_2\times\mathbb Z_2$ symmetry and prove that the corresponding equivariant correlators can be folded to the ordinary topological recursion correlators on the quotient spectral curve. We further establish the compatibility of this folding with the associated wave functions and quantum curves. In the Bessel-Clifford example, this yields an explicit relation between the equivariant and folded wave functions and recovers the corresponding quantum curve reduction. These results provide a framework for relating symmetry reductions of integrable systems to folding operations in topological recursion.

</details>


### [130] [Randomness from initial conditions: The challenge of incompleteness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23355)
*Klaas Landsman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文分析以算法随机性（algorithmic randomness）形式化玻姆力学（Bohmian mechanics）与动理论（kinetic theory）中"随机"初条件之设想的可行性。结论是：柴廷（Chaitin）不完备定理限制了动力学理论对随机序列的有限位数处理能力，使此类理论的预言力仅止于典型（typical）层面，其决定论主张被推至形而上学高位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 某些表面上决定论的理论（如玻姆力学、动理论）需借助"随机"初条件方能实现其解释目标。近期有提议以算法随机性来落实这一想法，并将其视为对原有典型性论证（typicality arguments）的数学化精炼，本文旨在审视该提议的合理性与其代价。

Method: 概念与逻辑分析：一方面批判原有典型性论证从而为该精炼方案作辩护；另一方面援引柴廷关于随机序列的不完备定理，论证动力学理论在处理随机初条件时所面临的位数精度与随机性保持之间的两难（实为同一问题的两面）。

Result: 柴廷不完备定理使得动力学理论无法在超过有限（但非任意）位数上处理给定随机初条件，或无法以任意精度陈述该条件而不损害其随机性。因此此类理论除"通常意义上的典型"预言（如宏观陈述）外，难以给出其他预言。

Conclusion: 算法随机性对典型性论证的精炼虽可辩护，但其代价是削弱相关理论的决定论地位：所谓决定论被提升至形而上学阶梯的高处，理论的实质预言力局限于典型性陈述。

Abstract: Certain prima facie deterministic theories, notably Bohmian mechanics and kinetic theory, require ``random'' initial conditions in order to achieve their goals. I analyze recent proposals that this idea should be implemented using algorithmic randomness, seen as a mathematical sharpening of the typicality arguments originally used to understand these theories. On the one hand my analysis includes a critique of these arguments and hence a defense of this sharpening. On the other hand, I argue that Chaitin's incompleteness theorems for random sequences make it impossible for a dynamical theory to either process given random initial conditions beyond some finite (but not arbitrary) number of their digits, or else to state such conditions with arbitrary precision without compromising their randomness (these are two sides of the same coin). This challenges the ability of such theories to make any other predictions than those that hold typically in the usual sense of the word (like macroscopic statements), and moves their alleged determinism high up the metaphysical ladder.

</details>

### [131] [Magnetic flux ring splitting near a Navier-Stokes singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22510)
*Antti J. Niemi*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该工作以电阻磁流体力学（resistive magnetohydrodynamics, MHD）模拟受驱动磁环，检验有限时间奇异性（finite-time singularity）流体行为在磁场结构上的物理后果。结果显示，在奇异性时刻前后，磁重联（magnetic reconnection）通过极向通量函数（poloidal flux function）的分岔将磁环分裂为多个子环，并伴随合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenAI 报告了在有限时间内变为奇异的受迫纳维-斯托克斯流（Navier-Stokes flow），其物理后果尚不清楚；磁重联（magnetic reconnection）提供了通过磁通结构分裂与合并进行检验的途径。

Method: 采用电阻磁流体力学（resistive magnetohydrodynamics）模拟一个磁环，由施加的、经过正则化（regularized）的流驱动，以再现坍缩核（collapsing core）的几何与标度；在名义奇异时间撤去驱动使系统自由演化，并进行三维模拟对照。

Result: 释放前后的重联通过极向通量函数（poloidal flux function）的分岔将磁环分裂为两个或更多子环，洛伦兹反馈（Lorentz feedback）选择分岔类型；中面上下旋流（swirl）绕数不等产生非对称环向磁应力，使运动学的类叉式（pitchfork-like）转变展开为折转（fold）。对单岛分裂，尖点正规形（cusp normal form）无需可调参数即可预测子环的非对称性；三维模拟保留分裂与合并。

Conclusion: 趋近流体爆炸（hydrodynamic blow-up）的过程可触发磁结构重组（magnetic restructuring）。

Abstract: OpenAI reports a forced Navier-Stokes flow that becomes singular in finite time, raising the question of its physical consequences. Magnetic reconnection offers a test through the splitting and merging of flux structures. We use resistive magnetohydrodynamics to simulate a magnetic ring driven by an imposed, regularized flow that reproduces the geometry and scaling of the collapsing core. At the nominal singular time, we remove the imposed drive and allow the system to evolve freely. Reconnections before and after release split the ring into two or more daughter rings through bifurcations of the poloidal flux function, with Lorentz feedback selecting the bifurcation type. Unequal winding by the swirl above and below the midplane produces asymmetric toroidal magnetic stresses, unfolding the kinematic pitchfork-like transition into a fold. For single-island splits, the cusp normal form predicts the asymmetry of the daughter rings without adjustable parameters. Three-dimensional simulations retain the splits and merger, confirming that approach to hydrodynamic blow-up can trigger magnetic restructuring.

</details>


### [132] [Stability and Nonlinear Dynamics of Field-Driven Extended Domain Walls with Interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23413)
*Enzo Fabrizio Pusiol,Alberto Rosso,Alejandro B. Kolton*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 论文研究无缺陷、无热涨落的理想体系中，含Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）的扩展畴壁（domain wall, DW）在外磁场驱动下的稳定性与非线性动力学；发现中等DMI强度与中间磁场区间存在时空混沌（spatio-temporally chaotic）区域，并用Floquet理论给出相图，经微磁学模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究常以刚性Walker模型描述畴壁运动，忽略弯曲自由度（flexural degrees of freedom）。本文旨在检验含DMI的扩展畴壁在宽场强与DMI参数范围内是否保持刚性运动，并厘清其失稳与非线性动力学行为。

Method: 在清洁极限（clean limit，无无序与热涨落）下建立扩展畴壁模型；对低场与高场区用刚性Walker模型分析；对中间场区采用基于Floquet理论的线性稳定性分析（linear stability analysis），显式纳入DMI与内部相位动力学，计算磁场-DMI相空间（phase space）中的相边界；并以求解完整Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert（LLG）方程的微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations）进行数值验证。

Result: 中等DMI强度下，低场与高场区动力学可由忽略弯曲自由度的刚性Walker模型准确描述；中间场区系统转变为非平凡的时空混沌态，畴壁出现动态粗糙化（dynamically roughened），呈现单值或多值剖面（single- or multi-valued profiles）。理论分析与预测相图得到完整LLG微磁学模拟的充分支持。

Conclusion: 含DMI的扩展畴壁并非在所有参数区都保持刚性运动，其中间场区存在由弯曲与内部相位自由度激发的时空混沌相；基于Floquet理论的稳定性分析与相图可作为判别该混沌区边界、并替代刚性描述的可靠理论工具。

Abstract: We analyze the stability and non-linear dynamics of extended domain walls (DWs) with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI) driven by an external magnetic field in the clean limit (i.e., in the absence of disorder and thermal fluctuations). We show that for moderate DMI strengths, the dynamics in both the low- and high-field regimes are accurately described by a rigid-wall Walker model that neglects flexural degrees of freedom. At intermediate fields, however, the system undergoes a transition into a non-trivial spatio-temporally chaotic regime characterized by dynamically roughened domain walls displaying single- or multi-valued profiles. The phase boundaries delimiting this regime in the magnetic field-DMI phase space are computed using a linear stability analysis based on Floquet theory applied to the extended domain wall, explicitly accounting for DMI and internal phase dynamics. Our theoretical analysis and predicted phase diagram are fully supported by numerical micromagnetic simulations solving the full Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation.

</details>


### [133] [Topological Magnetism in 2D and 3D: Skyrmions and Hopfions. Classical and quantum approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23490)
*ANtonio S T Pires*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该综述以连续场论（continuum field theory）、磁动力学、拓扑学（topology）与量子力学为框架，系统梳理铁磁与反铁磁体系中斯格明子（skyrmion）、半子（meron）、涡旋（vortex）及三维霍普夫子（hopfion）等拓扑磁结构的形成、稳定性、动力学、相互作用与量子性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑磁结构（topological magnetic textures）的研究分散于多种理论工具与磁体系之中，缺乏统一的理论联系；本文旨在弥合连续场论、磁动力学、拓扑与量子力学之间的隔阂，为理解不同维度与磁性系统中的拓扑磁结构提供连贯的框架。

Method: 综述性（review）方法：以 Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya 相互作用（Dzyaloshinskii Moriya interaction）、非线性 sigma 模型（nonlinear sigma model）、阻挫（frustration）与偶极相互作用（dipole dipole interactions）为切入点讨论铁磁斯格明子，并借助 Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert、Thiele 与 Landau–Lifshitz–Bloch 方程描述磁织构动力学；随后延伸至反铁磁系统、三维拓扑结构及量子斯格明子。附录补充解析解与拟设（ansatz）、自旋相干态与路径积分（spin coherent states and path integrals）、自旋力矩、纤维丛（fiber-bundle）与 Hopf 映射、三维 Euler–Lagrange 形式等内容。

Result: 综述重点论述了斯格明子之间的相互作用及磁织构的动力学方程；在三维情形下强调 Hopfion 及其拓扑表征（topological characterization），并讨论了超越半经典描述（semiclassical description）的量子斯格明子研究路径及量子 Hopfion 的出现。

Conclusion: 通过连接连续场论、磁动力学、拓扑与量子力学，该综述为跨维度、跨磁性体系中拓扑磁织构的形成、稳定性、动力学、相互作用与量子性质提供了连贯的理论框架。

Abstract: We discuss the properties of ferromagnetic skyrmions in relation to the Dzyaloshinskii Moriya interaction, nonlinear sigma models, merons, vortices, frustration, and dipole dipole interactions. Particular attention is given to skyrmion skyrmion interactions and to the dynamical equations governing magnetic textures, including the Landau Lifshitz Gilbert, Thiele, and Landau Lifshitz Bloch equations. The review subsequently extends the discussion to antiferromagnetic systems and to topological structures in three dimensions, with emphasis on Hopfions and their topological characterization. We then examine quantum skyrmions, including approaches that go beyond the semiclassical description, and discuss the emergence of quantum Hopfions. The theoretical framework is complemented by appendices presenting analytical solutions and ansatzes for skyrmions, spin coherent states and path integrals, quantum antiferromagnets, spin torques, fiber-bundle concepts and the Hopf map, and the Euler Lagrange formalism in three dimensions. By connecting continuum field theory, magnetic dynamics, topology, and quantum mechanics, this review aims to provide a coherent framework for understanding the formation, stability, dynamics, interactions, and quantum properties of topological magnetic textures across different dimensions and magnetic systems.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Gauge-Covariant Wavelets for Quark-Propagator Sparsening and Distillation in Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23207)
*Debsubhra Chakraborty,Nilmani Mathur,Mrinmoy Basak*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该文建立一个规范协变的小波框架（gauge-covariant wavelet framework），用于格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）中强子关联函数（hadronic correlation functions）的计算，将空间局域化与分辨率尺度分离联系到传播子稀疏化（propagator sparsening）与蒸馏（distillation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于降低多算符强子与核系统计算（尤其在较大格点体积下）的计算成本，同时控制早期激发态污染（excited-state contamination）与收缩（contraction）代价。

Method: 方法包含两条互补路径：基于紧支撑双正交滤波器（compactly supported biorthogonal filters）的规范协变快速小波变换（fast wavelet transform）用于粗粒化夸克传播子；以及对局域源施加空间协变拉普拉斯算符（spatial covariant Laplacian）的预解滤波（resolvent filter），无需计算拉普拉斯本征对即可生成蒸馏子空间（distillation subspace）。空间局域化还支持对强子收缩做无偏的 Hansen-Hurwitz 采样。

Result: 在单强子、I = 2 双π系统、氘核道（deuteron channel）与 boosted 核子上的数值基准显示，提取的能级在统计误差内与完全或常规蒸馏计算吻合良好；并给出按需基构造与收缩的成本量化及在相当统计精度下的收缩加速。文中还讨论了固定粗网格引起的动量混叠（momentum aliasing）及用随机网格平移消除期望中非期望贡献。

Conclusion: 研究为基于小波的传播子压缩与算符构造奠定了实用基础，适用于强子与核系统的多算符计算，尤其在大格点体积场景下。

Abstract: We develop a novel gauge-covariant wavelet framework for computing hadronic correlation functions in lattice QCD. Two complementary approaches connect spatial localization and resolution-scale separation to propagator sparsening and distillation. A gauge-covariant fast wavelet transform, based on compactly supported biorthogonal filters, coarse-grains quark propagators and reduces early-time excited-state contamination relative to direct decimation at equal spatial reduction. A second construction applies a resolvent filter of the spatial covariant Laplacian to localized sources, generating a distillation subspace without computing Laplacian eigenpairs. We detail its gauge covariance, smearing profiles, and implementation in exact and stochastic distillation. Spatial localization enables unbiased Hansen-Hurwitz sampling of hadronic contractions, reducing their computational cost substantially. We also examine momentum aliasing induced by fixed coarse grids and the use of random grid shifts to remove unwanted contributions in expectation. Numerical benchmarks of the proposed methods are performed with single hadrons, the I = 2 two-pion system, the deuteron channel, and boosted nucleons. The extracted energy levels agree very well with full or conventional distillation calculations within statistical uncertainties. We quantify the costs of on-demand basis construction and contractions, demonstrate contraction speedups at comparable statistical precision. These studies establish a practical foundation of wavelet-based propagator compression and operator construction for multi-operator calculations of hadronic and nuclear systems, particularly at larger lattice volumes.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [A Physics-based and Geometry-aware Reduced-order Framework for Accelerated Part-Scale Thermomechanical Simulation of Additive Friction Stir Deposition Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22375)
*Apurba Sarker,Sourav Saha*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于物理且几何感知的降阶（reduced order）框架，用于部件尺度增材摩擦搅拌沉积（additive friction stir deposition, AFSD）温度场与残余应力场的快速预测，相比有限元（finite element）方法实现约12–16倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFSD 适合海军与航空航天领域大型金属构件的高速率固态近净成形与修复，但移动热源、逐层沉积与几何演化使全部件尺度热力耦合仿真计算代价高昂，亟需快速预测手段。

Method: 瞬态温度场在固定笛卡尔背景网格上以张量积二次 B 样条（B spline）基表示，任意几何通过符号距离函数（signed distance function, SDF）与光滑材料指示函数嵌入；残余应力由固有应变（inherent strain）公式恢复，其标定特征应变场来自离线热弹塑性代表体积单元（representative volume element, RVE）模拟，并按部件尺度峰值温度历史进行空间缩放。

Result: 热学模型与已发表的 AFSD 数值及实验数据对比验证。四层薄壁构件热解约 22 s（有限元 806 s），完整热力框架约 86.4 s（有限元 2,406 s）；20 层中空圆柱热解为 0.148–0.152 h（有限元 1.8 h），加速约 11.8–12.2 倍，完整框架 0.346 h（有限元 5.4 h），总体加速约 15.6 倍。

Conclusion: 该降阶框架在保持物理一致性的前提下显著降低部件尺度 AFSD 热力仿真成本，具备用于大型构件成形与修复快速评估的潜力。

Abstract: Additive friction stir deposition (AFSD) is attractive for naval and aerospace sustainment because it enables high rate, solid state near net shape fabrication and repair of large metallic components. However, full part scale thermomechanical simulations remain computationally demanding because of the moving heat source, progressive material deposition, and evolving geometry. This work presents a physics-based and geometry-aware reduced order framework for rapid prediction of temperature and residual stress fields in part scale AFSD builds. The transient temperature field is represented using a tensor product quadratic B spline basis on a fixed Cartesian background grid, while arbitrary part geometries are embedded using a signed distance function (SDF) and a smooth material indicator. Residual stresses are recovered through an inherent strain formulation in which an offline thermo elasto plastic representative volume element (RVE) simulation supplies a calibrated eigenstrain field that is spatially scaled using the part scale peak temperature history. The thermal formulation is evaluated against published numerical and experimental AFSD data and two sample multilayer builds are simulated as demonstrations. For a four layer thin-wall build, the thermal solution requires approximately 22 s compared to 806 s for the finite element model, while the complete thermo-mechanical framework requires approximately 86.4 s compared to 2,406 s. For a larger 20 layer hollow cylindrical build, the reduced order thermal simulations requires 0.148 to 0.152 h compared with 1.8 h for the finite element model, corresponding to thermal speed-ups of approximately 11.8 to 12.2 times. The complete thermomechanical framework requires 0.346 h compared to 5.4 h for the full finite element analysis, providing an overall speed-up of approximately 15.6 times.

</details>


### [136] [Active Learning for Interatomic Potentials: A Review of Uncertainty-Driven Data Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23776)
*Ilya V. Chepkasov,Alexander V. Shapeev,Alexander G. Kvashnin*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该综述梳理了七十余项研究（重点为2020—2026年），展示主动学习（active learning, AL）如何从基于查询委员会与高斯过程力场的即时训练（on-the-fly training）技术，演变为面向机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potential, MLIP）的多样化数据高效策略族，并指出不确定性估计存在系统性隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建仅对特定应用条件准确、而非仅对开发者预设构型准确的机器学习原子间势（MLIP）具有显著挑战性；主动学习通过闭合候选生成、数据选择、参考计算与模型更新之间的回路来应对这一问题，且相关文献已快速增长，亟需系统性梳理。

Method: 采用文献综述方式，调查逾七十项研究（侧重2020—2026年），归纳主动学习的多种范式，包括即时分子动力学（on-the-fly molecular dynamics）、面向预训练模型微调（fine-tuning）的主动数据选择、基于冻结预训练模型的核方法与表示法采集（kernel- and representation-based acquisition），以及针对远离平衡化学体系的反应感知采样（reaction-aware sampling）；并考察其在多个材料与化学领域的应用。

Result: 主动学习已广泛应用于元素与合金力学、氢与离子扩散、铁电体（ferroelectrics）及功能材料有效哈密顿量、反应与光化学动力学、高熵与成分无序材料，以及生成式与自主材料发现流程；同时，基于方法学自省的批判性文献显示，不确定性估计（uncertainty estimates）在不同模型架构间不可互换，且往往恰在最关键之处最为不可靠。

Conclusion: 主动学习虽已取得显著进展，但伴随系统性注意事项；作者展望在基础模型（foundation-model）时代发展标准化、不确定性校准（uncertainty-calibrated）且面向可观测目标（observable-targeted）的主动学习工作流。

Abstract: Developing a machine learning interatomic potential (MLIP) that is accurate for the specific conditions under which it will be applied, rather than simply for the predefined configurations chosen by the model developer, is a significant challenge in atomistic simulation. Active learning (AL) addresses this challenge by closing the loop between candidate generation, data selection, reference calculation, and model updating. Here, we survey more than seventy studies, with an emphasis on work from 2020 to 2026, to understand how active learning has evolved from a niche, on-the-fly training technique for query-by-committee and Gaussian process force fields into a diverse family of data-efficient strategies. he reviewed examples include on-the-fly molecular dynamics, active data selection for fine-tuning pretrained models, kernel- and representation-based acquisition using frozen pretrained models, and reaction-aware sampling for chemical systems far from equilibrium. We explore the use of active learning in elemental and alloy mechanics, hydrogen and ion diffusion, ferroelectrics and effective Hamiltonians of functional materials, reactive and photochemical dynamics, high-entropy and compositionally disordered materials, as well as generative and autonomous material discovery pipelines. Drawing on a growing body of critical, methodologically self-aware literature, we also show that this achievement carries systematic caveats. Uncertainty estimates are not interchangeable across model architectures and are frequently the least reliable exactly where they matter most. We close by discussing the outlook for standardized, uncertainty-calibrated, and observable-targeted active-learning workflows in the foundation-model era.

</details>
