### [137] [Multi-Time States in Quantum and Operational Probabilistic Theories: An Axiomatic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22489)
*Kaumudibikash Goswami,Archana Barvar,Manabendra Nath Bera*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一个适用于一类操作概率理论（operational probabilistic theories, OPT）的多时间态（multi-time states）公理化框架，涵盖马尔可夫与非马尔可夫动力学，并在boxworld与量子动力学中给出具体应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间量子态的公理化刻画仅限于马尔可夫动力学（Markovian dynamics），缺乏容纳非马尔可夫动力学（non-Markovian dynamics）的通用框架，且将其推广到量子力学之外的一般物理理论仍属空白。

Method: 基于复合性（compositionality）、可重构性（reconstructibility）与时空边缘一致性（spatio-temporal marginal consistency）三条假设，在包含量子力学为特例的一类操作概率理论（OPTs）中构建多时间态的公理化框架。

Result: 框架可同时容纳马尔可夫与非马尔可夫动力学，并给出判定信号传递（signaling）与时间记忆（temporal memory）的充分条件；在boxworld中完整刻画了唯一的多时间态并给出物理变换的贝叶斯回溯（Bayesian retrodiction）方法；在非马尔可夫量子动力学中建立多时间态与过程矩阵（process-matrix）框架的对应，并据此提出用半定规划（semidefinite program）推断因果序的原理性方案。

Conclusion: 该工作为在OPTs内统一研究时空态、因果关联与贝叶斯推理提供了统一框架，与量子理论一致并复现若干已有结果，对基础与应用研究均具意义。

Abstract: Quantum states over time were introduced, in part, to extend Bayesian probability to quantum theory and have since found applications across diverse areas, ranging from quantum causality and open-system dynamics to tests of macroscopic realism. While axiomatic characterizations of these states exist for Markovian dynamics, a general framework accommodating non-Markovian dynamics is still lacking. Moreover, extending this framework beyond quantum mechanics to general physical theories also remains elusive. In this work, we develop an axiomatic framework for multi-time states within a class of operational probabilistic theories (OPTs) that includes quantum mechanics as a special case. Based on three assumptions---compositionality, reconstructibility, and spatio-temporal marginal consistency---the framework accommodates both Markovian and non-Markovian dynamics, and provides sufficient conditions to certify signaling and temporal memory within OPT. As a concrete application of our OPT framework, we fully characterize the unique multi-time state in boxworld and develop a method for Bayesian retrodiction of physical transformations there. Our general framework is consistent with quantum theory, reproducing several existing results. In the non-Markovian quantum dynamics, we establish a correspondence between multi-time states and the process-matrix framework, and fully characterize the multi-time states compatible with general dynamics. This correspondence also lets us infer quantum causal models from multi-time states, and as a proof of principle, we propose a semidefinite program to infer causal order from quantum multi-time states. These results provide a unified framework for studying spatio-temporal states, causal relations, and Bayesian reasoning within OPTs, with implications for both fundamental and applied research.

</details>


### [138] [A thermally and perturbatively stable classical spin model in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22548)
*Shankar Balasubramanian,Margarita Davydova,Ting-Chun Lin,Ryan Thorngren*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明在非零温度下，arXiv:2605.10943 构造的二维经典自旋模型（2D classical spin model）拥有大量对任意哈密顿量微扰（Hamiltonian perturbations）稳定的亚稳态（metastable states），为首次给出此类例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于寻找并构造在非零温度下存在大量亚稳态、且这些态对任意哈密顿量微扰仍保持稳定的自旋模型实例，此类例子此前尚未出现。

Method: 证明方法基于团簇展开（cluster expansion）的递归应用（recursive application）。

Result: 结果表明，所考察的二维经典自旋模型在非零温度下确有许多亚稳态，且这些亚稳态对任意哈密顿量微扰稳定，构成首个此类例子。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了首个具有大量微扰稳定亚稳态的非零温二维经典自旋模型实例，其论证核心为团簇展开的递归使用。

Abstract: We show that the 2D classical spin model constructed in arXiv:2605.10943 hosts many metastable states at non-zero temperature which are stable to arbitrary Hamiltonian perturbations, providing the first such example. Our proof relies on a recursive application of the cluster expansion.

</details>


### [139] [On the relationship between the POVM framework and the no-signaling hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22823)
*Will Cavendish*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文精确形式化"无信号假说（no-signaling hypothesis）"，证明在标准非相对论量子力学（NRQM）复合实验表征与两条边际概率教科书论断下，该假说与POVM框架的普适性等价，并据此刻画NRQM概率生成部分各公设间的逻辑关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有大量"无超光速信号"论证实际依赖Born规则或POVM框架，其所确立的更强主张是"互孤立系统间任何速度的信号均被禁止"，作者希望检视这一更强假说本身能推出什么。

Method: 形式化"无信号假说"，并以标准NRQM的复合实验表征加上两条关于边际概率（marginal probabilities）的教科书式断言为附加假设，推导其与POVM框架普适性之间的逻辑关系。

Result: 所需附加假设出奇地少：在上述条件下，无信号假说与POVM框架的普适性等价；更广泛地，该假说可推出NRQM概率生成片段的大部分（但非全部）内容。

Conclusion: 所得蕴含关系图（map of implications）澄清了NRQM哪些常见公设（postulates）在逻辑上相互关联、哪些可被独立修改。

Abstract: The quantum foundations literature contains dozens of "no-signaling" arguments that rely on the Born rule or, more generally, the positive operator-valued measure (POVM) framework to show that the resources of non-relativistic quantum mechanics (NRQM) cannot be used to send superluminal signals. What these arguments typically establish, however, is not a prohibition on superluminal signaling per se, but the seemingly stronger claim that signaling at any speed between mutually isolated systems is forbidden. This paper gives a precise formulation of this claim, the no-signaling hypothesis, and investigates the following converse question: under what additional assumptions does it imply that all experimental outcome probabilities are described by POVMs? Surprisingly few are needed: given the standard NRQM representation of composite experiments and two textbook claims about marginal probabilities, the no-signaling hypothesis is equivalent to the universality of the POVM framework. More broadly, by formalizing the relevant concepts and deriving the logical relationships between them, the paper shows that the no-signaling hypothesis can be used to derive most, though not all, of the probability-generating fragment of NRQM. The resulting map of implications clarifies which common postulates of NRQM are logically interrelated and which can be modified independently.

</details>


### [140] [Anticommuting Displacement Operators, Exact Pauli Algebras,and the Singlet State from the Oscillator Vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23264)
*S. P. Sorella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文用谐振子（harmonic oscillator）上的有界泛函演算（bounded functional calculus）构造反交换的Weyl位移算符，得到Pauli代数（Pauli algebra）的精确二值表示；对双振子真空施加单态扇区投影（singlet-sector projector）后，态具有精确单态关联并达到Tsirelson值（Tsirelson value）$2\sqrt2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量（continuous-variable）Bell检验中，Pauli代数通常只能以无界算符或伪自旋（pseudospin）方式近似实现，缺乏严格的有界表述；本文旨在给出无需无界算符的精确构造，并与旧的伪自旋方法作对照。

Method: 明确构造一对反交换的Weyl位移算符，借助有界泛函演算得到Pauli代数的二值代表元；由两个独立振子分别给出Alice与Bob的相互交换的Pauli代数，并将完全由这些有界振子可观测量（observable）表达的标准单态扇区投影作用于双振子真空。

Result: 投影后的真空具有非零范数（norm），故其归一化良定义；该态展现精确的单态关联，并在标准Bell–CHSH设置下达到Tsirelson值$2\sqrt2$。文中同时指出该构造一般实现的是带重数（multiplicity）的单态扇区，而非在完整振子Hilbert空间中选取唯一向量。

Conclusion: 该工作为连续变量Bell测试提供了严格的有界算符框架，澄清了单态扇区的重数性质，并将之与较早的伪自旋（pseudospin）方法进行比较。

Abstract: We give an explicit construction of a pair of anticommuting Weyl displacement operators for a harmonic oscillator and use bounded functional calculus to obtain exact dichotomic representatives of the Pauli algebra. Two independent oscillators provide commuting Pauli algebras for Alice and Bob. The standard singlet-sector projector, written entirely in terms of these bounded oscillator observables, is then applied to the two-oscillator vacuum. We show explicitly that the projected vacuum has nonzero norm, so its normalization is well defined, has the exact singlet correlations, and reaches the Tsirelson value $2\sqrt2$ for the usual Bell--CHSH settings. We also clarify that the construction generally realizes a singlet sector with multiplicity, rather than selecting a unique vector in the full oscillator Hilbert space, and we compare the construction with the older pseudospin approach to continuous-variable Bell tests.

</details>


### [141] [Partially isophase electro-magnetic configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23645)
*Andrey M. Pupasov-Maksimov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出“部分同相symmetry（partial isophase symmetry）”概念：当薛定谔方程（Schrödinger equation）可分离变量时，存在无穷维族的物理上不同但在某一由守恒量子数标记的选定扇区（sector）内动力学不可区分的场构型。作者以轴对称磁场背景与笛卡尔平板几何为例加以说明，并讨论其对逆谱问题（inverse spectral problem）及半经典对应（quasiclassical counterpart）的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可分离变量系统（separation of variables）中常存在谱结构上的简并或不可区分现象，作者希望刻画一类仅在单一守恒量子数扇区内动力学等价、而在其他扇区彼此不同的场构型族，从而给出这一对称性的明确定义与物理实例。

Method: 以薛定谔方程可分离变量为前提，形式化定义部分同相symmetry；考察两类主要例子：轴对称磁场背景（包含Fock–Darwin振子（Fock–Darwin oscillator）的刚性谱流（rigid spectral flow）、纯磁同相族及双通道扩展），以及笛卡尔平板几何（二维电子气（two-dimensional electron gases）中的磁势垒，置于附录处理）。

Result: 识别出无穷维的物理上不同但在一选定扇区内动力学不可区分的场构型族；在轴对称磁场情形下给出刚性谱流、纯磁同相族与双通道扩展等具体结构，并在平板几何中对应磁势垒问题。

Conclusion: 部分同相symmetry构成了可分离变量薛定谔系统中一类新的谱—动力学等价结构，对逆谱问题具有直接含义，并存在相应的半经典（quasiclassical）对应形式。

Abstract: Separation of variables in Schrödinger equation allows an infinite-dimensional family of physically distinct field configurations that are dynamically indistinguishable in one selected sector, labeled by a conserved quantum number, while differing in all others. We call such a symmetry a partial isophase symmetry. Two main examples are considered: axially symmetric magnetic backgrounds (a rigid spectral flow of the Fock--Darwin oscillator, purely magnetic isophase families, and a two-channel extension) and a Cartesian slab geometry (magnetic barriers in two-dimensional electron gases, treated in the Appendices). We further discuss the consequences of this symmetry for the inverse spectral problem and consider its quasiclassical counterpart.

</details>


### [142] [Recursive Operator Localization and Spectral Gap Descent via the Geometry of Local Spectral Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23695)
*Ryu Hayakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出递归算子局部化（Recursive Operator Localization, ROL）框架，通过希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）之间的线性映射构造相互关联的局部能量问题层级，并用于谱隙（spectral gap）分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对谱隙问题（包括量子系统（quantum systems）中出现的情形），需要一种能把局部谱隙信息系统地传播到全局算子的统一框架。

Method: 以加权单纯复形（weighted simplicial complex）索引的嵌套子空间实现 ROL；利用链环（links）上辅助经典随机游走（classical random walks）的谱隙向上传播几何估计，并将底层局部谱隙转化为全局算子的谱隙界。

Result: 对分面（facets）上的加权标量函数，ROL 分解即全方差定律（law of total variance），可恢复由局部链环扩张得到的经典谱隙界（Alev and Lau, STOC 2020）；对正二元自旋分布（positive binary spin distributions），条件影响（conditional influence）刻画子空间重叠，导出由谱独立性（spectral independence）给出的已知热浴谱隙界（Anari, Liu, and Oveis Gharan, FOCS 2020）。

Conclusion: ROL 为量子吉布斯采样（quantum Gibbs sampling）提供了可能路径，但其应用需要针对具体模型的几何估计与能量比较。

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Operator Localization (ROL), a framework for constructing hierarchies of local energy problems linked by linear maps between Hilbert spaces. To address spectral-gap problems, including those arising in quantum systems, we study a realization of ROL by nested subspaces indexed by a weighted simplicial complex. Using spectral gaps of auxiliary classical random walks on links, we propagate geometric estimates upward and use them to turn local spectral gaps at the bottom of the hierarchy into a spectral-gap bound for the global operator. For weighted scalar-valued functions on facets, the ROL decomposition becomes the law of total variance, recovering classical spectral-gap bounds from local link expansion (Alev and Lau, STOC 2020). Moreover, for positive binary spin distributions, conditional influence describes the overlap of the corresponding subspaces, yielding known heat-bath gap bounds from spectral independence (Anari, Liu, and Oveis Gharan, FOCS 2020). The framework also offers a route toward quantum Gibbs sampling, with applications requiring model-specific geometric estimates and energy comparisons.

</details>


### [143] [Metric and Dispersive Dynamics of Phase Perturbations in Bohmian Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22737)
*Felipe S. Escórcio,Hermano Velten*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过线性化Bohmian动力学中Madelung变换对应的流体力学方程，推导背景态附近相位扰动的有效度规（effective metric），并将此前结果推广为保留量子势（quantum potential）完整泛函变分的更一般结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前得到的度规仅为特例，需将其纳入更一般的框架，以涵盖量子势对密度及其空间导数的完整依赖，并纳入外部经典势。

Method: 对Bohmian动力学（Bohmian dynamics）中Madelung变换（Madelung transformation）关联的流体力学方程进行线性化，保留量子势的完整泛函变分，并引入微分算符（differential operators）分离局域有效几何部分与修正项。

Result: 推广后的结构自发包含原有度规为特例，额外项引入色散修正（dispersive corrections）并作为有效Klein-Gordon方程（effective Klein-Gordon equation）中的源项；同时给出了这些附加项可忽略的条件。

Conclusion: 在这些条件下可恢复均匀传播（homogeneous propagation），并重现自由粒子高斯波包（Gaussian wave packet）的有效几何。

Abstract: By linearizing the hydrodynamic equations associated with the Madelung transformation in the context of Bohmian dynamics, we derive an effective metric for the phase perturbation around a background state. We show that the metric previously obtained previously by the same authors arises naturally as a particular case of a more general structure, in which the complete functional variation of the quantum potential is retained and external classical potentials can be incorporated. This generalization preserves the dependence of the quantum potential on the density and its spatial derivatives, generating additional terms that introduce dispersive corrections and act as sources in the effective Klein-Gordon equation. To organize these contributions, we introduce differential operators that separate the local sector responsible for the effective geometry from the corrections associated with the complete functional structure of the quantum potential. We identify the conditions under which these additional terms become negligible, recovering homogeneous propagation and the effective geometry for the Gaussian wave packet of a free particle.

</details>


### [144] [Stability of Quantum LDPC States under Local Pauli Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22357)
*Tai-Hsuan Yang,Andrew D. Kim,Akash Vijay,Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：在局域 Pauli 噪声下，Z_d qLDPC 稳定子码的码空间归一化投影态的 CMI 随缓冲宽度指数衰减，并由此得到带恒深层 quasi-local Petz 恢复通道与 Levin–Wen 拓扑熵的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC 码（quantum low-density parity-check codes）的码空间归一化投影态位于量子纠错与量子多体物理（quantum many-body physics）的交界，但其信息论结构在局域噪声下的稳定性尚不清楚。已有研究将条件互信息（conditional mutual information, CMI）的空间衰减作为混合态物相稳定性的关键判据，本文旨在为该判据建立严格结论。

Method: 针对 Z_d qLDPC 稳定子码在局域 Pauli 噪声下的归一化码投影态，证明跨缓冲区 B 的 CMI I(A:C|B) 随 B 宽度指数衰减，分弱噪声（全系统三划分，关联需误差团簇穿越缓冲区）与强噪声（缓冲区不含逻辑算符）两个互补噪声区制；并在有限维晶格上由弱噪声界构造 quasi-local Petz 恢复通道（recovery channel）。

Result: 两类 CMI 界均与系统尺寸无关；在附加空间均匀性（space-uniformity）假设下得到 Levin–Wen 拓扑熵（topological entropy）的稳健性。在尺寸为 n 的有限维晶格上，弱噪声界还导出恒深层、作用范围 O(poly(log n)/ε) 的 quasi-local Petz 恢复通道，其以迹距离（trace distance）误差 ε 恢复无噪声态。

Conclusion: CMI 界适用于一般 qLDPC 相互作用图（interaction graphs），包括不存在此类恒深 quasi-local 恢复的几何结构。因此，噪声诱导 CMI 的指数衰减可作为混合态物相稳定性的一般判据。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes have a canonical mixed state---the normalized projector onto the code space---that lies at the intersection of quantum error correction and quantum many-body physics, yet the stability of its information-theoretic structure under local noise remains poorly understood. Recent progress on mixed-state phases of matter has identified the spatial decay of conditional mutual information (CMI) $I(A:C|B)$ as a key diagnostic of such stability, analogous to the role of the spectral gap for quantum ground states. We prove that, for the normalized code-projector state of a $\mathbb{Z}_d$ qLDPC stabilizer code under local Pauli noise, the CMI across a buffer $B$ separating $A$ from $C$ decays exponentially with the width of $B$ in two complementary noise regimes independent of system size. At weak noise, this holds for full-system tripartitions, where correlations between the two sides require an error cluster to cross the buffer, an exponentially unlikely event. At strong noise, exponential decay holds whenever the buffer supports no logical operator, since correlations can persist only along rare chains of buffer qudits that remain informative. Under an additional space-uniformity assumption, we establish the robustness of Levin--Wen topological entropy. On finite-dimensional lattices of size $n$, the weak-noise bound further implies a constant-layer quasi-local Petz recovery channel of range $O(\poly(\log n)/\varepsilon)$ that recovers the noiseless state within trace-distance error $\varepsilon$. Both CMI bounds apply to general qLDPC interaction graphs, including geometries where such constant-depth quasi-local recovery need not exist. Thus, the exponential decay of the noise-induced CMI provides a general criterion for mixed-state phase stability.

</details>


### [145] [Exploring Quantum Phase Transitions Beyond Landau Theory on a Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22482)
*Ruizhe Shen,Yin Zhong,Ching Hua Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用超导量子处理器在一维阻挫自旋链中观测到被朗道理论禁止的铁磁-价键固体（FM-VBS）连续量子相变，并结合非平衡动力学研究竞争序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同对称性破缺相之间的直接连续量子相变挑战传统朗道-金兹堡-威尔逊（Landau-Ginzburg-Wilson）范式，并为解禁闭量子临界性（deconfined quantum criticality）提供研究框架；本文旨在用可编程量子硬件探究此类朗道禁戒（Landau-forbidden）相变。

Method: 在阻挫一维自旋链上，结合矩阵乘积态辅助的线路制备（matrix-product-state-assisted circuit preparation）与量子硬件测量，进行距离分辨的关联测量和有限尺寸分析，并进一步直接制备二聚化（dimerized）态、追踪局部幺正演化下VBS序与联合FM-VBS涨落的非平衡动力学。

Result: 实验分辨出长程铁磁关联的抑制及竞争序涨落在相变处的重新分布，距离分辨关联与有限尺寸分析显示与临界行为一致的信号。

Conclusion: 结果建立了一个基于量子硬件的通用框架，可用于探测超越传统朗道理论的竞争序、临界关联与多体动力学。

Abstract: Direct continuous quantum phase transitions between phases that break distinct symmetries challenge the conventional Landau-Ginzburg-Wilson paradigm and provide a framework for deconfined quantum criticality. Here, we investigate a Landau-forbidden ferromagnetic-to-valence-bond-solid (FM-VBS) transition in a frustrated one-dimensional spin chain using superconducting quantum processors. Combining matrix-product-state-assisted circuit preparation with quantum-hardware measurements, we resolve the suppression of long-range ferromagnetic correlations and the redistribution of competing-order fluctuations across the transition. Distance-resolved correlations and finite-size analyses reveal signatures consistent with critical behavior. We further extend our investigation to nonequilibrium dynamics, directly preparing dimerized states and tracking the evolution of VBS order and joint FM-VBS fluctuations under local unitary interactions. Our results establish a versatile quantum-hardware framework for probing competing orders, critical correlations, and many-body dynamics beyond conventional Landau theory.

</details>


### [146] [Discrete time crystals and classical logic gates in coupled open Dicke models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23043)
*Emmanuel D. G. U,Roy D. Jara,Jayson G. Cosme*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究耗散下耦合Dicke模型（Dicke dimer）中耦合离散时间晶体（discrete time crystals, DTCs）的动力学，揭示耦合导致振幅衰减、强耦合下系统失稳并趋于正常相（normal phase, NP），并据此提出三DTC实现NAND与NOR逻辑门的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解耦合离散时间晶体在耗散环境中的动力学行为及其相变特征，并探索其在量子逻辑运算中的应用潜力。

Method: 采用平均场动力学数值模拟刻画耦合DTC的振荡与失稳行为，并使用截断Wigner近似（truncated Wigner approximation, TWA）引入量子涨落，进一步在噪声条件下检验NP向DTC同步/反同步的种子化现象。

Result: 耦合两个DTC使其振荡振幅在瞬态弛豫后下降；耦合足够强时耦合DTC失稳并弛豫至正常相，且弛豫时间在临界耦合强度附近发散，提示连续相变。TWA结果显示量子涨落对动力学的影响；NP短暂耦合于DTC可被种子化为同步或反同步DTC，具有一定抗噪鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于上述结果，提出通过依次耦合输入与输出位点、利用三个DTC执行NAND和NOR操作的方案，并确定逻辑门架构有效的参数区间，同时分析量子涨落对该协议的影响。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of coupled discrete time crystals (DTCs) in the coupled Dicke model or Dicke dimer (DD) with dissipation. By numerically simulating the mean-field dynamics of the system, we show that coupling two DTCs decreases their oscillation amplitude following a transient relaxation period. For sufficiently strong coupling, the coupled DTCs become unstable, resulting in the system relaxing towards the normal phase (NP). We demonstrate that the relaxation time diverges near the critical coupling strength, suggesting a continuous phase transition. Using the truncated Wigner approximation (TWA), we explore how the dynamics are affected by the introduction of quantum fluctuations. We also consider a scenario in which an NP is briefly coupled to a DTC, seeding the NP to transform into a DTC that is either synchronized or anti-synchronized relative to the DTC. We investigate the robustness of this phenomenon in the presence of noise. Using these results, we propose a scheme to perform NAND and NOR operations with three DTCs by sequentially coupling the input and output sites. We identify the parameter regimes in which the logic gate architecture remains valid, and investigate how the protocol is affected by the presence of quantum fluctuations.

</details>


<div id='nlin.PS'></div>

### [147] [Resonance interaction of vector breathers and exotic dynamics in the coupled Hirota equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23723)
*Li-Juan Bo,Xiu-Bin Wang*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 基于穿衣法（dressing method）系统研究耦合 Hirota 方程（coupled Hirota equation）中的矢量呼吸子（vector breathers）及其非线性相互作用，给出三类基本呼吸子、共振条件与非弹性/弹性相互作用场景，并经数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合 Hirota 方程（coupled Hirota equation）描述光纤中超短光脉冲的同时传播，并含刻画三阶色散、自陡峭等高阶线性与非线性效应强度的关键参数；现有工作对其中矢量呼吸子的分类与相互作用规律尚缺乏系统理论刻画。

Method: 聚焦聚焦-聚焦（focusing-focusing）情形，在非零平面波背景上，采用穿衣法（dressing method）推导多呼吸子解的显式表达式；通过选取特殊常向量将单呼吸子解分为 Type I、Type II、Type III 三类，并分析其空间局域性与群速度等特征参数及 Kuznetsov-Ma 呼吸子、Peregrine 孤子、Akhmediev 型呼吸子等特例。

Result: 发现高阶效应参数仅改变呼吸子的传播速度与振荡周期，而共振条件（波数与频率匹配）始终满足；Type I 与 Type II 间可发生非弹性共振相互作用，表现为聚变过程（I + II → II）或反向衰变过程（III → I + II）；双呼吸子解呈现呼吸子共振与弹性碰撞等丰富场景，共振特例对应特征值合并的极限情形，数值模拟验证了解的准确性。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了耦合 Hirota 方程中矢量呼吸子的系统分类与相互作用图像，阐明高阶效应参数的作用机制及共振与非弹性相互作用的对应关系，为光纤超短脉冲传播的理论描述提供支撑。

Abstract: This work presents a systematic theoretical investigation of vector breathers and their nonlinear interactions in the coupled Hirota equation. This model describes the simultaneous propagation of ultrashort optical pulses in optical fibers and contains a key parameter that characterizes the strength of higher-order linear and nonlinear effects, such as third-order dispersion and self-steepening. For the model, we focus on the focusing-focusing case. On a nonzero plane-wave background, we derive explicit expressions for multi-breather solutions of the coupled Hirota equation by the dressing method. For the single-breather solution, by choosing special constant vectors, the vector breathers can be classified into three fundamental types: Type I, Type II, and Type III. We then analyze their characteristic parameters, including spatial localization and group velocity, as well as important special cases such as the Kuznetsov-Ma breathers, Peregrine soliton, and Akhmediev type breathers etc. Through the above analysis, we find that the higher-order effect parameter only modifies the propagation velocity and oscillation period of the breathers, while the resonance conditions remain satisfied: when the wave numbers and frequencies match, inelastic resonant interactions can occur between Type I and Type II breathers, manifesting either as a fusion process (I + II \rightarrow II) or the reverse decay process (III \rightarrow I + II). Furthermore, we present the explicit expression for the two breather solutions of the equation and analyze its rich interaction scenarios, including two breather resonances and elastic collisions, and we point out that the special case of resonance corresponds to the limiting case of merging eigenvalues. Finally, we carry out numerical simulations to verify the accuracy of the obtained solutions.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Spectral networks, finite webs, and the $T_3$ cubic differential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23752)
*Omar Kidwai,Guillaume Tahar*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文研究 $\mathbf{P}^1$ 上具有三个二阶极点的三次微分（cubic differentials）的谱网络（spectral networks），基于 Hollands–Neitzke 的观察给出有限网（finite webs）的精确定义并证明其中心荷非零，进而在具体例子中证明有理角处网络完全退化、其余角处网络稠密且不含有限网。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三次微分情形下的谱网络与有限网尚缺乏精确定义与基本性质，且其物理对应物（$\mathrm{SU}(3)$ 规范群的四维 $\mathcal{N}=2$ 量子场论）需要具体的构造性例子。

Method: 沿袭 Hollands–Neitzke 的思路，在三次微分背景中给出有限网（对应物理中的 BPS 态）的精确数学定义，建立其基本性质（包括中心荷（central charge）非零性）；随后针对 $\mathbf{P}^1$ 上三个二阶极点的具体构型，按角度分类分析网络的退化性与稠密性，并结合算术判别准则显式计算墙（walls）与 Stokes 区域的数量。

Result: 在有理角（rational angles）处谱网络完全退化（totally degenerate），墙数与 Stokes 区域数可显式算出且取决于某一算术判别准则；在所有其他角度，谱网络在曲面上稠密（dense）且不含有限网。

Conclusion: 该例在物理上构成一大类规范群为 $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ 的四维 $\mathcal{N}=2$ 量子场论的基本构件（building block），为三次微分谱网络提供了可计算的原型范例。

Abstract: We describe spectral networks for cubic differentials on $\mathbf{P}^1$ with exactly three poles all of order two, following observations of Hollands-Neitzke. To do so, we give a precise definition and establish some basic properties of a class of finite webs (corresponding to BPS states in physics) in the cubic differential setting, including their non-vanishing central charge. Turning to the example, we prove that at rational angles the network is totally degenerate; the number of walls and Stokes regions is explicitly computed and depends on an arithmetic criterion. At all other angles, the spectral network is dense in the surface and contains no finite webs, as expected. Physically, this example corresponds to a fundamental building block for a large class of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ QFTs with gauge group $\mathrm{SU}(3)$.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Towards optimal selection schedules for online active learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23477)
*Hugo Cui,Francesca Mignacco,Francesco Mori*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究高维高斯设计下可实现（realizable）在线线性分类（online linear classification）中的最优查询策略（query policy），即何时对无标注样本请求标注用于训练、何时丢弃；以泛化误差（generalization error）预算约束与含查询成本Δ的累积代价（cumulative cost）为目标，刻画并比较了短视策略（myopic policies）与所提出的简单查询计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注成本高昂而无标注样本流可持续获得，因此需要明确“是否标注、何时标注、是否丢弃”的查询策略；既有启发式缺乏在预算或累积代价目标下的最优性刻画，故论文在高维比例增长框架下系统研究该问题。

Method: 设定高维区域（数据维度与无标注样本数成比例增长）与高斯设计（Gaussian design）的可实现情形，分析两类目标：预算约束下的泛化误差，以及误分类单位成本加查询成本Δ的累积代价；先推导短视策略等价于小间隔或中间间隔策略（边距区间随时间收缩），再数值确定最优阈值调度，并据累积代价的短视策略设计简单查询调度，在合成数据与真实医学影像数据上与其他启发式比较；最后讨论两层神经网络（two-layer neural networks）中的短视策略。

Result: 理论分析表明，最小化目标即时增量的贪心短视策略对应于小间隔（small-margin）或中间间隔（intermediate-margin）策略，即选取边距（margin）落入随时间收缩区间的样本；在该类策略内可数值获得使最终目标最优的选择阈值调度。所设计的简单查询调度在合成数据与真实医学影像数据集上与其它启发式进行了对比评估。

Conclusion: 短视策略在高维可实现线性分类中具有可刻画的结构（边距区间选择且区间随时间收缩），据此可构造简洁有效的查询调度；对于两层神经网络，直接基于边距的朴素策略可能不再最优，提示边距型策略的最优性依赖于模型与设定。

Abstract: We consider the problem of online linear classification, where a stream of unlabeled samples is available, and annotation is costly. We investigate the optimal query policy, that prescribes whether and when a sample should be labeled and used for a training step, or instead discarded, so as to minimize a given objective. We focus on the realizable case, under Gaussian design, in the high-dimensional regime where data dimension and number of unlabeled samples grow proportionally. We consider two objectives: 1) the generalization error under a labeling budget, and 2) a cumulative cost function where mistakes have unit cost and queries cost $Δ$. We first show that the myopic policies that greedily minimize at all times the increment in the objective correspond to either small or intermediate-margin policies, that prescribe the selection of samples whose margins fall within certain intervals that shrink with time. Within this class, we numerically ascertain the optimal schedules for the selection thresholds that minimize the final values of the objectives. Taking inspiration from the myopic policy for the cumulative cost, we design a very simple query policy schedule that we compare to other proposed heuristics, on synthetic data and real medical image datasets. Finally, we discuss myopic policies for two-layer neural networks, and exemplify how naive margin-based policies can cease to be optimal.

</details>


### [150] [Mesoscopic learning dynamics of extensive-width neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23779)
*Shu Zhou,Jean Barbier,Sebastian Goldt*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文对宽两层网络学习同类架构目标函数时的随机梯度下降（stochastic gradient descent）表征学习给出介观（mesoscopic）描述：用重叠（overlap）统计量的分布替代发散的少数序参量刻画，揭示学习先由集体变量主导、再转入特征专门化（specialization）的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在宽度与输入维度成比例增长的宽网络（wide neural networks）中，标准学习动力学描述需追踪数量发散的 overlap（学习特征与目标特征的相关性），难以用少数序参量（order parameters）刻画，因此需要新的描述框架来理解内部表征如何被学习。

Method: 研究两层网络学习由相同架构网络表示、非线性（nonlinearity）任意的目标函数，在宽度与输入维度按比例增长的极限下，通过刻画 overlap 的分布建立介观学习理论；在学习早期采用 overlap 统计量的高斯描述（Gaussian description），将其约化为少数集体变量（collective variables），并估计专门化时间尺度。

Result: 早期学习可由追踪目标函数线性与二次成分（linear and quadratic components）的少数集体变量刻画；当高斯闭合（Gaussian closure）失效、单个学习特征开始与特定目标特征强相关时进入专门化阶段。在 extensive-rank 网络中，专门化因二次屏蔽机制（quadratic shielding mechanism）而加速——学习线性与二次成分会过滤梯度涨落（gradient fluctuations）。此外，激活函数（activation function）决定专门化模式及后续特征精细化（refinement）的方式。

Conclusion: 结果阐明了集体学习（collective learning）如何让位于个体特征发现，并明确了大型神经网络中学习何时可用简单统计描述来刻画。

Abstract: Understanding how stochastic gradient descent learns internal representations in wide neural networks trained on rich target functions is a central problem in machine learning. We study this question in two-layer neural networks learning target functions represented by networks of the same architecture, with arbitrary nonlinearity, and with network width and input dimension growing proportionally. In this regime, standard approaches for describing the learning dynamics require tracking a diverging number of overlaps, which measure correlations between learned and target features, leaving no obvious description in terms of a few order parameters. Here, we develop a mesoscopic description of learning by characterizing the distribution of overlaps. In the early stages of learning, we find that a Gaussian description of overlap statistics reduces the learning dynamics to a few collective variables that track learning of the target function's linear and quadratic components. We then estimate the timescale of specialization, when Gaussian closure breaks down and individual learned features begin to strongly correlate with specific target features. We find that specialization in extensive-rank networks is accelerated via a quadratic shielding mechanism, whereby learning the linear and quadratic components of the target function filters gradient fluctuations. We finally describe how the activation function determines the pattern of specialization and characterize the subsequent refinement of learned features. Together, these results show how collective learning gives way to individual feature discovery and clarify when learning in large neural networks admits a simple statistical description.

</details>

TL;DR: LHCb 实验首次观测到稀有衰变 Ξ_b^-→Ξ^-μ^+μ^-，测得积分分支比 (118±24±7±38)×10^-8，并给出 Ξ_b^-→Ξ^-ψ(2S) 与 Ξ_b^-→Ξ^-J/ψ 分支比之比为 0.491±0.041±0.019。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该稀有衰变此前未被观测，其测量可为重味重子（heavy-flavour baryon）的弱衰变与味改变中性流（flavour-changing neutral current）过程提供新的实验约束。

Method: 利用 LHCb 在质心能量（centre-of-mass energy）7、8、13 TeV 下采集的质子-质子对撞数据，对应积分亮度（integrated luminosity）分别为 1、2、6 fb^-1；按双缪子不变质量平方（dimuon invariant mass squared）分区间报告分支比（branching fraction）。

Result: 测得积分分支比 B(Ξ_b^-→Ξ^-μ^+μ^-)=(118±24±7±38)×10^-8，其中统计、系统及归一化分支比 B(Ξ_b^-→Ξ^-J/ψ) 的不确定度分别给出，后者主要由 SU(3) 破坏效应（SU(3)-breaking effects）主导；同时测得分支比之比 0.491±0.041±0.019。

Conclusion: 该衰变被首次观测到，总不确定度主要由归一化分支比贡献，提示提升 B(Ξ_b^-→Ξ^-J/ψ) 精度对后续测量具有重要意义。

Abstract: The rare decay ${Ξ_b^-\toΞ^-μ^+μ^-}$ is observed for the first time using proton-proton collision data collected by the LHCb experiment at centre-of-mass energies of $7$, $8$ and~$13~\text{TeV}$, corresponding to integrated luminosities of $1$, $2$ and $6~\text{fb}^{-1}$, respectively. Its branching fraction is reported in intervals of the dimuon invariant mass squared. The integrated value of ${\cal{B}}(Ξ_b^-\toΞ^-μ^+μ^-)$ is measured to be ${ (118 \pm 24 \pm 7 \pm 38 ) \times 10^{-8}}$, where the first uncertainty is statistical, the second is systematic and the third uncertainty arises from the normalisation branching fraction ${\cal{B}}(Ξ_b^-\toΞ^-J/ψ)$, whose uncertainty is dominated by SU(3)-breaking effects. In addition, the ratio of the branching fractions of the ${Ξ_b^-\toΞ^-ψ(2S)}$ and the ${Ξ_b^-\toΞ^-J/ψ}$ decays is measured to be ${ 0.491 \pm 0.041 \pm 0.019 }$.

</details>


### [152] [A comparative study of variational quantum classifiers and classical machine learning for $tW-t\bar{t}$ discrimination at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22504)
*Rohan Hindocha,Priyanka*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作利用变分量子分类器（Variational Quantum Classifier, VQC）在CMS开放数据模拟的$tW$单顶夸克产生事件中区分$t\bar{t}$本底，并与提升决策树（Boosted Decision Trees, BDTs）和多层感知机（Multi-Layer Perceptron, MLP）进行系统对比，给出VQC在高能物理实际分类任务中的基准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量伴随$tW$道的单顶夸克产生需要高效甄别占主导的$t\bar{t}$本底；同时量子机器学习（quantum machine learning）在真实高能物理分类任务中的适用性尚缺乏系统检验，故本文考察VQC在该问题上的可行性。

Method: 采用VQC算法，基于CMS Open Data中Run-2对应的模拟质子-质子碰撞事件进行训练与评估；以机器学习指标及刻画信号显著度（Signal Significance）随信号效率（Signal Efficiency）变化的物理导向指标，分别在双轻子$e-\mu$道以及分辨（resolved）与增强（boosted）强子拓扑中，与BDTs、MLP两种经典方法比较；并进一步考察信号富集与本底富集相空间区域。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果，仅表明通过上述多维度的对比分析，识别出量子启发式模型相对成熟经典技术在判别能力上具备竞争力的分析区间（analysis regimes）。

Conclusion: 该研究为VQC在真实高能物理分类任务中的应用提供了系统性基准，并界定了量子启发模型可与经典方法相竞争的具体分析情形。

Abstract: Precise measurements of single top-quark production in the associated $tW$-channel require efficient discrimination against the dominant $t\bar{t}$ background. In this work, we investigate the applicability of quantum machine learning to this classification problem using the Variational Quantum Classifier (VQC) algorithm. Simulated proton-proton collision events from CMS Open Data corresponding to Run-2 are employed to train and evaluate the VQC model. Its performance is compared with two established classical approaches, Boosted Decision Trees (BDTs) and Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) using both machine learning metrics and physics-motivated metrics that describe the Signal Significance as a function of Signal Efficiency. The comparison is performed separately for the dileptonic $e-μ$ channel and for the resolved and boosted hadronic topologies. In addition, signal-enriched and background-enriched phase-space regions are studied to assess the dependence of classifier performance on event topology. Our results provide a systematic benchmark for the application of VQC for a realistic High Energy Physics classification task and identify the analysis regimes wherein quantum-inspired models offer competitive discrimination with respect to established classical techniques.

</details>


### [153] [Laboratory Characterization of a Ge-CCD Prototype for Future Near-Infrared Astronomical Instrumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22785)
*Emi Tamura,Juan Cruz Estrada Vigil,Prashansa Mukim,Giovanni Pinaroli,Christopher Leitz,Ilya Prigozhin,Kevan Donlon,Santiago Ezequiel Perez,Sven C. Herrmann*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文报道了在布鲁克海文国家实验室（Brookhaven National Laboratory, BNL）对MIT林肯实验室（MIT Lincoln Laboratory）制备的锗电荷耦合器件（germanium charge-coupled device, Ge-CCD）原型进行的低温实验室表征，给出了暗电流、读出噪声与X射线响应等基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ge-CCD可将CCD成像波段延伸至硅截止波长之外，是未来天文仪器近红外通道（如下一代宽视场巡天光谱仪）的候选探测器，因此需要对其原型器件进行实验室基准表征。

Method: 搭建并描述低温测试系统与读出方案，测量输出波形、暗电流、读出噪声与X射线响应；在无片上合并（on-chip binning）条件下用$^{55}$Fe辐照，并在高能下采用$2\times2$片上合并（使用$^{133}$Ba与$^{241}$Am源）。

Result: 94.69 K下暗电流为$26.12 \pm 0.08$ e$^-$ pixel$^{-1}$ s$^{-1}$；$^{55}$Fe辐照下X射线电荷集中于$2\times2$像元内，单像元事件的Mn K$\alpha$线半高全宽（full width at half maximum, FWHM）为$8.5 \pm 0.1$%；$2\times2$合并下30.9 keV处FWHM为$8.4\pm0.4$%，59.5 keV处为$7.45\pm0.04$%。

Conclusion: 上述结果建立了该器件与BNL测试系统的性能基线；温度依赖、光学响应与电荷转移性能的后续测量将把该基线同近红外天文仪器的需求相衔接。

Abstract: Germanium charge-coupled devices (Ge-CCDs) extend CCD imaging beyond the silicon cutoff and are candidate detectors for the near-infrared channel of future astronomical instruments, in particular the spectrographs of next-generation wide-field surveys. We report the laboratory characterization at Brookhaven National Laboratory (BNL) of a prototype fabricated at MIT Lincoln Laboratory. We describe the cryogenic test system and readout, and present measurements of the output waveform, dark current, readout noise, and X-ray response. The dark current at 94.69 K is $26.12 \pm 0.08$ e$^-$ pixel$^{-1}$ s$^{-1}$. Under $^{55}$Fe irradiation without on-chip binning, the charge generated by an X-ray is confined within $2\times2$ pixels, and the Mn K$α$ line has a full width at half maximum (FWHM) of $8.5 \pm 0.1$% for single-pixel events. Since the charge generated by X-rays above about 30 keV spreads over $3\times3$ or more pixels, the CCD was operated with $2\times2$ on-chip binning at higher energies. This yielded FWHMs of $8.4\pm0.4$% at 30.9 keV ($^{133}$Ba, ASCA grades 0, 2, 3, 4, and 6) and $7.45\pm0.04$% at 59.5 keV ($^{241}$Am, all grades). These results establish a baseline for the device and for the BNL test system. Measurements of the temperature dependence, optical response, and charge-transfer performance will connect this baseline to the requirements of near-infrared astronomical instruments.

</details>


### [154] [A REST-based Monte Carlo Simulation and Data Analysis Framework for the N$ν$DEx Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23112)
*Siyuan Huang,Tao Li,Hao Qiu,Shaobo Wang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: NνDEx（无中微子双贝塔衰变实验）基于REST框架构建了完整的仿真与重建链，模拟波形拟合重建得到的能量分辨率（FWHM）优于1%，满足实验设计要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NνDEx计划在锦屏地下实验室（CJPL）利用高压气态TPC探测⁸²Se的无中微子双贝塔衰变（0νββ），需要配套的仿真与重建软件来评估探测器响应、本底抑制与设计优化，而现有框架缺乏针对该探测器的定制描述。

Method: 基于REST（Rare Event Searches Toolkit）软件框架开发完整仿真与重建框架，定制实现探测器几何、电子漂移与扩散、Topmetal像素读出平面、信号形成及电子学响应等过程，实现从能量沉积到数字化波形的全链条描述，并采用基于波形拟合的重建方法提取沉积电荷与事例能量。

Result: 对模拟信号事例，重建能量分辨率（半高全宽，FWHM）达到小于1%，满足实验设计要求。

Conclusion: 该仿真与重建链为后续探测器响应研究、本底排除、事例分类以及NνDEx探测器设计优化提供了基础。

Abstract: The N$ν$DEx experiment is designed to search for neutrinoless double-beta decay ($0νββ$) of $\mathrm{^{82}Se}$ and is planned to be deployed at the China Jinping Underground Laboratory (CJPL). It employs a high-pressure time projection chamber (TPC) filled with $\mathrm{^{82}SeF_6}$ gas and a Topmetal pixelated charge readout system. The high $Q_{ββ}$ value of $\mathrm{^{82}Se}$, together with the track reconstruction capability of a gaseous TPC, provides strong potential for background suppression. In this work, we develop a full simulation and reconstruction framework for the N$ν$DEx experiment based on the REST (Rare Event Searches Toolkit) software framework. To meet the detector-specific requirements of N$ν$DEx, customized simulation processes are implemented to describe the detector geometry, electron drift and diffusion, the Topmetal readout plane, signal formation, and electronics response. These developments enable a complete software description of the detector response from energy deposition to digitized waveforms. A waveform-fitting-based reconstruction method is further applied to the simulated signals to extract the deposited charge and reconstruct the event energy. For simulated signal events, the reconstructed energy resolution reaches a full width at half maximum (FWHM) of less than 1\%, satisfying the design requirement of the experiment. The resulting simulation and reconstruction chain provides a basis for future studies of detector response, background rejection, event classification, and optimization of the N$ν$DEx detector design.

</details>


### [155] [Reactor Antineutrino Detection with JUNO-TAO: Commissioning Performance and First Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23331)
*JUNO Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 台山反中微子观测站（TAO）已完成探测器安装并于2026年2月开始物理取数，基于调试与首批数据验证其具备国际领先的能级性能，可实现高精度反应堆反中微子能谱测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAO作为江门中微子实验（JUNO）的卫星实验，旨在以高精度测量反应堆反中微子能谱（reactor antineutrino energy spectrum），为JUNO能谱相关分析提供关键支撑与参考。

Method: 采用2.8吨、直径1.8 m的球形液闪探测器，距堆芯43.2 m，在-50℃低温下运行，配备4,024个硅光电倍增器（SiPM）单元；利用逆β衰变（IBD）中氢中子俘获进行刻度，并结合暗噪声与光串扰修正评估光产额与能量分辨率。

Result: 光产额约4000光电子/MeV；能量分辨率在中心处对⁶⁸Ge约2.8%，对¹³⁷Cs（R=193 mm）约3.7%；有效体积内顶点重建偏差在±30 mm内、分辨率约70 mm；缪子标记效率约97%；反应堆开/关数据中IBD候选事例率分别为2900±10与419±4事例/天，呈现显著超出。

Conclusion: TAO实现了稳定的低温运行与优异的能量分辨率（属反应堆反中微子探测器已报道的最佳水平之一），验证了其高精度测量反应堆反中微子能谱的能力。

Abstract: The Taishan Antineutrino Observatory (TAO) is a satellite experiment of JUNO, designed to measure the reactor antineutrino energy spectrum with high precision at a baseline of 43.2~m from a reactor core using a 2.8-ton spherical liquid-scintillator detector, 1.8~m in diameter, operated at -50 degree and instrumented with 4,024 silicon photomultiplier tiles. The TAO detector installation was completed in 2025, and physics data taking began on 10 February 2026. This paper reports the detector design, calibration strategy, and performance based on commissioning data and the first physics runs. Stable cryogenic operation has been achieved, and about 90\% of the readout channels are retained for physics analysis after channel-quality selections. The detector light yield is measured to be about 4000~photoelectrons/MeV after correction for dark noise and optical crosstalk, using neutron capture on hydrogen in inverse beta decay (IBD) events. The energy resolution is about 2.8\% for \(^{68}\)Ge at the detector center and about 3.7\% for \(^{137}\)Cs at \(R=193\)~mm. This energy-resolution performance is among the best reported for reactor-antineutrino detectors and enables TAO to measure the reactor antineutrino spectrum with high precision. Within the fiducial volume of \(R < 800\)~mm, the vertex reconstruction bias remains within \(\pm 30~\mathrm{mm}\), and the resolution is approximately \(70~\mathrm{mm}\) over most of the fiducial volume. The combined muon-tagging efficiency of the veto system is about 97\%. Using 31.3 days of reactor-on data and 29.4 days of reactor-off data, a significant excess of IBD candidates is observed in the reactor-on sample relative to the reactor-off sample, with candidate rates of 2900$\pm$10 events/day and 419$\pm$4 events/day, respectively. The results demonstrate that TAO has reached a high performance level in reactor antineutrino measurement.

</details>
