### [156] [Dilaton-Axion Wormhole Partition Function Away from the BPS Endpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22429)
*Soo-Jong Rey*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文计算带电轴子虫洞（charged axion wormhole）在BPS端点附近双边界响应的首个有限半径修正，给出边界本征值、Hessian非对角元与跨边界协方差的标度行为，并在手征高斯系综上求逆响应的平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是刻画带电轴子虫洞（charged axion wormhole）在BPS端点附近双边界响应（two-boundary response）的有限半径修正（finite-radius correction），以补足该极限下响应结构的定量描述。

Method: 令 δ=a_*^2，在轴子–伸缩子/轴子–标量（axion–dilaton/saxion）乘积系统中做精确径向匹配（radial matching）；再对描述BPS端点的手征高斯系综（chiral Gaussian ensemble）取逆响应的平均，并以匹配的BPS扇区权重进行边界约化（boundary reduction）。

Result: 边界本征值以 O(δ) 消失，Hessian（Hessian）非对角元为 O(δ^2)，二者组合给出线性于 δ 的跨边界协方差（cross-boundary covariance），且所研究分支上首个次领头修正为负；同时确定二次近似可控的半经典区间及首个有限半径角共振 δ^2 log(δ/δ_0)。在所有活跃电荷一致重标度下，线性响应系数按电荷标度的平方反比缩放。

Conclusion: 边界约化保持硬边指数（hard-edge index）；将归一化响应乘以匹配的BPS扇区权重，即得首阶有限半径双源系数（two-source coefficient）。

Abstract: I determine the leading finite-radius correction to the two-boundary response of a charged axion wormhole near its BPS endpoint. With $δ=a_*^2$, exact radial matching in a product axion--dilaton/saxion system gives a boundary eigenvalue that vanishes as $O(δ)$ and an off-diagonal Hessian entry of order $O(δ^2)$. Their combination produces a cross-boundary covariance linear in $δ$, with a negative first sub-leading correction on the branch studied here. I determine the semiclassical range in which the quadratic approximation remains controlled and identify the first finite-radius angular resonance, $δ^2\log(δ/δ_0)$. Under a uniform rescaling of all active charges, the coefficient of the linear cross-boundary response scales as the inverse square of the charge scale. I then average the inverse response over the chiral Gaussian ensemble describing the BPS endpoint. Boundary reduction preserves the hard-edge index. Multiplication by the matched BPS sector weight converts the normalized response into the leading finite-radius two-source coefficient.

</details>


### [157] [A non-prompt null ray cures its own singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22478)
*Gauri Batra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在AdS/CFT框架下，体锥奇点（bulk cone singularity）源于边界上类时分离的两点可经体内类光测地线相连。论文考虑该测地线自引力反作用（backreaction）对关联函数的修正，发现其导致能量依赖的时间延迟与引力辐射等非弹性效应，从而消解奇点并在关联函数实部与虚部中产生振荡与零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有AdS/CFT关联函数计算多在G_N→0极限下进行，而体锥奇点在该极限下是发散的。作者关注有限耦合（非零G_N）修正，特别是类光测地线自引力反作用如何正则化该奇点。

Method: 指出该测地线非即时（not prompt），其上存在类时分离点，故可对自身产生引力影响；据此分析自引力导致的能量依赖时间延迟，并结合引力辐射引起的非弹性效应，在2+1维通过研究边界引力子模（boundary graviton mode）显式计算该时间延迟。

Result: 自引力效应导致沿测地线的能量依赖时间延迟（energy-dependent delay）；该效应与引力辐射等非弹性效应共同构成消解奇点的主要机制。被消解的奇点伴随振荡，表现为关联函数实部与虚部的零点。

Conclusion: 体锥奇点由测地线自引力时间延迟与非弹性效应所解析化；附加非弹性效应起阻尼振荡的作用。2+1维边界引力子模为显式计算能量依赖时间延迟提供了具体范例。

Abstract: In AdS/CFT correlators, bulk cone singularities arise when two points that are timelike separated on the AdS boundary may be connected by a null geodesic through the bulk. We consider corrections to these correlators away from the $G_N\to 0$ limit arising from the backreaction of this null geodesic. A peculiar property of this geodesic is that it is not prompt, meaning that there exist points on it that are timelike separated. This implies that it may gravitationally affect itself. We show that this leads to an energy-dependent delay in the time elapsed along this geodesic. This coupled with possible inelastic effects due to gravitational radiation are the leading mechanisms that resolve the singularity. The resolved singularity is accompanied by oscillations and hence zeroes in the real and imaginary parts of the correlator. We comment on the role that additional inelastic effects play in damping these oscillations. Finally, we provide a concrete example in $2+1$D where we can compute the energy-dependent time delay effect explicitly by studying the boundary graviton mode.

</details>


### [158] [Topological Aspects of the $c=2$ Model in the Continuum and on the Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22485)
*Yichul Choi,Nathan Seiberg,Sahand Seifnashri,Wucheng Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究靶空间为双环面（two-torus）的连续二维共形场论（two-dimensional conformal field theory），揭示其零形式内部对称性与由θ参数（背景B场）导致的−1形式对称性构成带反常的高群（higher group），并阐明空间缠绕B场如何破坏平移对称但留下可解性所需的高群或非可逆对称（non-invertible symmetry）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于澄清双环面靶空间共形场论中θ参数（背景B场）所携带的−1形式对称性与常规内部、空间对称性之间的关系，并理解在空间依赖B场破坏平移对称的情况下模型仍可通过傅里叶分解求解的原因。

Method: 方法为在连续二维共形场论框架下分析零形式内部对称性与−1形式对称性组合成带反常的高群；考察空间缠绕及线性变化B场对平移对称与内部动量对称的影响；随后采用修改的Villain表述（modified Villain formulation）构造该系统的格点版本进行对照。

Result: 结果表明：空间缠绕B场通常完全破坏空间平移对称，并将连续靶空间（内部）动量对称约化为投影实现的离散子群；当B场线性变化时，一个离散平移子群存活并与内部对称性组合成非阿贝尔群（non-Abelian group），其余平移以非可逆对称形式存留。非可逆平移对称解释了傅里叶分解求解的可行性；并且在有限格点上上述所有对称性微妙之处依然存在。

Conclusion: 结论指出，连续模型中出现的这些对称性结构（含高群、反常与非可逆对称）在有限格点版本中同样成立，且通过多种推广表明所揭示的现象具有更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We discuss the continuum two-dimensional conformal field theory, whose target space is a two-torus. In addition to its standard zero-form internal symmetries, the model also has a $-1$-form symmetry associated with its $θ$-parameter, which is also known as a background $B$-field. These symmetries combine into a higher group with anomalies. A spatially winding $B$-field generically breaks the spatial translation symmetry completely, and reduces the continuous target-space (internal) momentum symmetry to a (projectively realized) discrete subgroup. When $B$ varies linearly, a discrete translation subgroup survives, and it combines with the internal symmetry into a non-Abelian group, while the other translations survive as non-invertible symmetries. Even though the model lacks the standard spatial translation symmetry, the non-invertible translation symmetry explains why it is solvable via a Fourier decomposition. We then consider a lattice version of this system, using its modified Villain formulation. Surprisingly, all these subtleties in the symmetries of the continuum model exist on a finite lattice. Finally, we discuss various generalizations of this model, demonstrating that the phenomena we uncovered are more broadly valid.

</details>


### [159] [No cosmic horizons from a strengthened higher-dimensional strong energy condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22506)
*Flavio Tonioni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明，满足强化强能量条件（strengthened strong energy condition）的理论无法紧致化（compactification）为能够支持未来宇宙视界（future cosmic horizon）的低维理论；在10维与11维超引力（supergravity）约化到至少四维大时空的情形下，该条件由已满足强能量条件的源自动满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时超引力紧致化（time-dependent supergravity compactifications）超出Gibbons/Maldacena-Nuñez禁de Sitter定理的适用范围，甚至可支持永恒宇宙加速，因此有必要追问其能否同时产生未来宇宙视界；而对一般高维物质，仅靠强能量条件（strong energy condition）不足以排除此类视界。

Method: 在假定紧致化于光滑、紧致且无边界（smooth compact and boundaryless）空间上进行的前提下，作者采用强化版强能量条件作为判据，对一般高维物质及10维、11维超引力约化情形进行分析论证。

Result: 满足强化强能量条件的理论不能被紧致化为支持未来宇宙视界的低维理论；对约化到至少四个大时空维度的10维与11维超引力，该能量条件由高维中已满足强能量条件的源所满足。

Conclusion: 因此，在光滑紧致无边界约化的框架下，强化强能量条件构成对未来宇宙视界存在的排除性约束，为含时超引力紧致化中的视界问题提供了明确的判定准则。

Abstract: Time-dependent supergravity compactifications lie beyond the setting of the Gibbons/Maldacena-Nuñez no-de Sitter theorem and can even support eternal cosmic acceleration, raising the question of whether they can also produce future cosmic horizons. For general higher-dimensional matter, the strong energy condition alone does not exclude such horizons. Here, we demonstrate that a theory fulfilling a strengthened version of the strong energy condition cannot be compactified down to a lower-dimensional theory able to support a future cosmic horizon. For 10d and 11d supergravities reduced to at least four large spacetime dimensions, this energy condition is fulfilled by the sources that already fulfill the strong energy condition in the higher-dimensional theory. Our assumption on the dimensional reduction is that it is performed on a smooth compact and boundaryless space.

</details>


### [160] [Superstring theory in $AdS_5\times S^5$ background: half-BPS vertex operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22502)
*Thiago Fleury,Lucas N. S. Martins,Dennis Zavaleta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过全局 PSU(2,2|4) 变换，构造了 AdS5×S5 背景下与引力超多重态（gravity supermultiplet）相关的全部顶点算子，并在纯旋量（pure spinor）与 B-RNS-GSS formalism 中给出相应的积分顶点算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界面散射振幅（worldsheet scattering amplitude）计算需要引力超多重态对应的顶点算子；此前仅用特殊超空间参数化在纯旋量形式中构造出其中一个算子，且因玻色与费米坐标混合，实现完整全局对称性颇为困难。

Method: 引入一组经过审慎设计的重参数化（reparametrizations）序列，将混合的玻色与费米坐标解耦，并借助全局 PSU(2,2|4) 变换构造全部顶点算子，进而构造相应的积分顶点算子（integrated vertex operators）。

Result: 解决了全局对称性实现中的坐标混合难题，构造出引力超多重态的全部顶点算子，并在 pure spinor 与 B-RNS-GSS 两种形式中给出了对应的积分顶点算子。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了 AdS5×S5 背景下超弦理论（superstring theory）的表述，为世界面散射振幅计算提供了完整的顶点算子集合。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct all vertex operators associated with the gravity supermultiplet in the $AdS_5 \times S^5$ background, relevant for worldsheet scattering amplitude computations, via global $PSU(2,2|4)$ transformations. Although one such operator was previously constructed in the pure spinor formalism using a special superspace parametrization, implementing the full global symmetries proved challenging due to the mixing of bosonic and fermionic coordinates. We resolve this issue by introducing a judicious sequence of reparametrizations that disentangle these coordinates. We then advance the formulation of superstring theory in this background by constructing the corresponding integrated vertex operators in both the pure spinor and the B-RNS-GSS formalisms.

</details>


### [161] [Perturbative Defect CFT Data in Scalar Models and Consistency Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22591)
*Ziwen Kong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在 d=4-ε 维度下计算了 O(N)、超立方（hypercubic）、超四面体（hypertetrahedral）、O(m)×O(n) 双锥（biconical）及 MN 模型中标量线缺陷（scalar line defect）的缺陷共形场论（defect CFT）数据至 O(ε) 阶，并提出结合交叉对称（crossing symmetry）与 OPE 重构的微扰数据验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 除作为基准（benchmark）的 O(N) 理论外，其余四个模型的缺陷 CFT 数据集此前缺失，需要系统计算并建立可自洽检验的微扰数据体系。

Method: 在指定微扰基（perturbative basis）下确定谱（spectra）、算符混合（operator mixing）、两点与三点系数，以及 ε=0 时整数维 n≤2 的相同或成对外部算符的四点函数；并以交叉对称、OPE 重构（OPE reconstruction）及破缺对称积分恒等式（broken-symmetry integral identities）构成验证框架。

Result: 给出了四个新模型的缺陷 CFT 数据，并识别出一个新的普适缺陷方向扇区（universal defect-direction sector），其一圈（one-loop）数据可由缺陷不动点方程（defect fixed-point equation）导出；在所用微扰阶数下所有适用条件均被满足。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可验证的微扰缺陷 CFT 数据集，数据汇总于补充笔记本（supplemental notebook）defect_cft_data.nb 中，为后续缺陷 CFT 研究提供参照与检验工具。

Abstract: We compute defect CFT data through $O(ε)$ for scalar line defects in the $O(N)$, hypercubic, hypertetrahedral, $O(m)\times O(n)$ biconical, and $MN$ models in $d=4-ε$. The $O(N)$ theory provides a benchmark; the datasets for the other four models are new. For the branches considered, we determine spectra, operator mixing, two- and three-point coefficients in a specified perturbative basis, and four-point functions with identical or paired external operators of integer dimension $n\leq2$ at $ε=0$. We propose a framework for verifying perturbative defect CFT data by combining crossing symmetry, OPE reconstruction, and the applicable broken-symmetry integral identities. We also identify a new universal defect-direction sector whose one-loop data follow from the defect fixed-point equation. All applicable conditions are satisfied at the perturbative orders considered. The model data are collected in the supplemental notebook, defect_cft_data.nb.

</details>


### [162] [Maximizing the spectral gap for free bosonic CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22633)
*Hyeongjoe Chu,Yuchan Jung*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将 Narain CFT 的谱隙 $\Delta_1$ 视为 Narain 模空间上的函数加以研究，证明 $c=2,3$ 时的尖锐上界 $2/3$、$3/4$ 并刻画等号情形，同时通过格论与计算机辅助分类方法分析 $4\le c\le 12$ 的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Narain CFT 的谱隙（spectral gap）刻画非真空原初态的最小标度维数，其随 Narain 模空间（Narain moduli space）变化的整体行为尚需刻画；同时，最密欧氏格（densest Euclidean lattices）能否承载 Narain 结构是自然的问题。

Method: 利用 $SO^+(2,2)$ 与 $SO^+(3,3)$ 的例外同构（exceptional isomorphisms）在 $c=2,3$ 情形下严格求解；对 $4\le c\le 12$，将 Narain 模空间与已知最密欧氏格比较，采用算术障碍（arithmetic obstruction）、计算机辅助分类（computer-assisted classification）与概念性论证三类手段。

Result: 证明 $c=2$ 时 $\Delta_1\le 2/3$、$c=3$ 时 $\Delta_1\le 3/4$ 为尖锐界，等号仅在 $SU(3)_1$ 与 $SU(4)_1$ WZW 模型（至多相差 $O(c)\times O(c)$）处成立，并描述 $\Delta_1$ 的局部极大值；在 $4\le c\le 12$ 中，算术障碍排除五个候选格，分类结果显示 $E_8$、$K_{12}$、$BW_{16}$ 各自至多相差格自同构（lattice automorphisms）唯一地容许 Narain 结构，Leech 格则被概念性论证排除。

Conclusion: Narain CFT 的谱隙在低维模空间上具有可精确求解的尖锐上界，其饱和点由特定 WZW 模型实现；高维情形下，最密格中仅少数可兼容 Narain 结构，Leech 格被排除。

Abstract: We study the spectral gap $Δ_1$ of Narain CFTs, the smallest scaling dimension of a nonvacuum $U(1)^c_L\times U(1)^c_R$ primary, as a function on the Narain moduli space. Using the exceptional isomorphisms for $SO^+(2,2)$ and $SO^+(3,3)$, we prove the sharp bounds $Δ_1\le 2/3$ at $c=2$ and $Δ_1\le 3/4$ at $c=3$, with equality exactly at the $SU(3)_1$ and $SU(4)_1$ WZW models up to $O(c)\times O(c)$, and we describe local maxima of $Δ_1$. For $4\le c\le 12$, we compare the Narain moduli space with the densest known Euclidean lattices: an arithmetic obstruction excludes five candidates, a computer-assisted classification shows that $E_8$, $K_{12}$, and $BW_{16}$ each admit a unique Narain structure up to lattice automorphisms, and a conceptual argument excludes the Leech lattice.

</details>


### [163] [Hidden Supersymmetry in AdS/QCD models for Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22806)
*Rafael A. Costa-Silva,Henrique Boschi-Filho*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在形变AdS/QCD模型中，由Dirac方程分离出的手征模式满足类薛定谔方程，其手征有效势为SUSY伙伴势，因而这些模式是等谱的；据此可构造保持等谱性的势截断，并证明形变AdS模型的二次近似与费米子dressed soft wall模型谱可重合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究形变AdS/QCD模型中费米子手征模式谱结构的性质，并厘清不同全息费米子模型之间谱的对应关系。

Method: 将Dirac方程分离出的手征模式化为类薛定谔方程，利用手征有效势互为超对称（SUSY）伙伴势的性质论证等谱性；提出保持等谱性的势截断方案，并据此比较形变AdS模型的二次近似与费米子dressed soft wall模型。

Result: 手征模式被证明是等谱（isospectral）的，且其等谱性在特定截断下得以保持；形变AdS模型的二次近似与dressed soft wall模型的费米子谱可以重合。

Conclusion: 超对称伙伴势结构为理解手征模式的等谱性及不同全息费米子模型间的谱等价提供了统一框架，并给出可操作的截断准则。

Abstract: We show that the chiral modes splitted from the Dirac equation satisfying Schrodinger-like equations in the context of deformed AdS/QCD model are isospectral, since the chiral effective potentials are SUSY partners. In particular, we show how to perform truncations of these potentials that preserve isospectrality of the chiral modes. Following this prescription, we compare a quadratic approximation of the deformed AdS model with the dressed soft wall model for fermions and show how the spectra of both models can coincide.

</details>


### [164] [Asymmetric multiscalar fixed points from equiangular tight frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22923)
*Christopher Beem*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在 22001 个标量场理论中构造出大量仅保留普适 Z₂ 场反演对称性的一圈重整化群（renormalization group）实不动点，正面回答了 Rychkov 与 Stergiou 提出的存在性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rychkov 与 Stergiou 在研究多标量重整化群流（multiscalar RG flows）时提问：在 d=4−ε 的标量场理论中，是否存在除普适 Z₂ 场反演（field inversion）外不具备任何额外对称性的一圈实不动点（real fixed point）。该问题此前未获解答。

Method: 借助等角紧框架（equiangular tight frame）的几何结构，在不假设任何额外对称性的前提下，将（至两圈的）不动点方程约化到二维耦合空间；核心技术难点在于证明存在对应直线不保持任何对称性的此类框架，作者采用 Fickus、Mixon 与 Tremain 的 Steiner 框架构造（Steiner frame construction）实现。

Result: 在 22001 个标量场的理论中成功构造出大量满足条件的不动点，从而解决了上述存在性问题；这些不动点除普适 Z₂ 场反演外不尊重任何额外对称性。

Conclusion: 结论表明，无额外对称性的一圈实不动点在多标量理论中确实存在，等角紧框架的几何方法为约化不动点方程提供了有效途径。

Abstract: In their work on multiscalar RG flows, Rychkov and Stergiou posed the question of whether there exist real fixed points of the one-loop renormalization group for scalar field theories in $d=4-ε$ which respect no extra symmetry beyond the universal $\mathbb{Z}_2$ field inversion. In this note, we report on a construction of a large number of such fixed points in the theory of $22001$ scalar fields, thus resolving the question. The construction uses the geometry of an equiangular tight frame in order to reduce the (up to two-loop) fixed point equation to a two-dimensional space of couplings without assuming any extra symmetry. The main technical hurdle is then to establish the existence of such a frame whose corresponding lines preserve no symmetry. This can be achieved using the Steiner frame construction of Fickus, Mixon, and Tremain.

</details>


### [165] [Optical conductivity of holographic striped superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23184)
*Fang-jing Cheng,Kai Li,Yi Ling,Meng-He Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 基于呈现Mott-like绝缘行为的离子晶格全息模型，构建了电荷密度波（CDW）与超导（SC）共存的全息条纹超导体（SSC），给出e-T相图并首次在全息框架下通过光学电导率验证其超导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Mott-like绝缘背景的离子晶格全息模型中研究电荷密度波与超导的相互作用，刻画二者协作与竞争关系，并检验条纹超导相的真实超导本质。

Method: 构建全息条纹超导体模型，计算e-T相图，并沿CDW→SSC与SC→SSC两条路径计算光学电导率（optical conductivity），另在p-T相图中考察公度锁定（commensurate lock-in）行为。

Result: SSC相仅在中间e窗口出现：小e时随降温经历金属→CDW→SSC，大e时经历金属→SC→SSC。两路径下电导率虚部在临界温度T_SSC以下均发散；CDW→SSC路径继承赝能隙（pseudogap）与钉扎效应（pinning effect），SC→SSC路径中自发条纹强烈抑制相干正常流体通道并增强超流极点（superfluid pole）。

Conclusion: CDW序与SC序之间同时存在协作与竞争关系；电导率虚部的发散首次在全息框架下确证SSC相的超导性，并揭示条纹超导体的公度锁定特性。

Abstract: Based on a holographic model exhibiting Mott-like insulating behavior on an ionic lattice, we construct a holographic striped superconductor (SSC), which is characterized by the interplay between the charge density wave (CDW) and superconductivity (SC). We present the phase diagram on the $e$-$T$ plane, where the SSC phase emerges within an intermediate window of $e$, revealing both cooperative and competitive relations between the CDW order and SC order. For small charge $e$, the system undergoes the phase transition from metallic phase to CDW phase, and then to SSC phase upon cooling; while for large $e$, it transitions from metallic phase to SC phase directly, and then to SSC phase. We compute the optical conductivity of the system for these two cases and demonstrate its distinct behavior. It turns out that the imaginary part of the conductivity exhibits a divergent behavior below the critical temperature $T_{\mathrm{SSC}}$ along both pathways, so that the superconductivity of SSC phase is justified for the first time in the holographic framework. Along the CDW-to-SSC pathway the pseudogap and the pinning effect are inherited from the CDW phase, whereas along the SC-to-SSC pathway the spontaneous stripe strongly suppresses the coherent normal-fluid channel while the superfluid pole is enhanced. In addition, we demonstrate the commensurate lock-in behavior of the striped superconductor in the $p$-$T$ diagram.

</details>


### [166] [Exact $T\bar{T}$-Like Flows in Nonlinear Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23281)
*Zahra Pezhman,H. Babaei-Aghbolagh,Komeil Babaei Velni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为一类具有Courant–Hilbert表示的一尺度、自对偶非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）理论推导出精确的非微扰 $T\bar T$ 型流（$T\bar T$-like flow），并证明在物理分支上该流可化为由两个二次应力张量不变量（quadratic stress-tensor invariants）自主决定的函数形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将 $T\bar T$ 型形变推广到更广泛的自对偶非线性电动力学理论，以构造精确的、非微扰的自主无关流（autonomous irrelevant flow），弥补此前仅针对特定模型（如Born–Infeld）结果的局限。

Method: 基于Courant–Hilbert表示给出流的通用参数形式，并要求相关不变量映射（invariant map）可逆以获得自主性；以 $\partial_\lambda\mathcal L=X\,\mathcal F(Y/X)$ 的形式表达流，并进一步论证其与Russo–Townsend辅助场（auxiliary-field）表述的等价性。

Result: Born–Infeld理论给出二次应力张量流（quadratic stress-tensor flow）；因果的Russo–Townsend对数模型给出含Lambert $W$ 函数的精确非线性表达式，其弱场展开（weak-field expansion）可重现已知微扰结果。

Conclusion: 结果为一大类明确指定的自对偶非线性电动力学理论提供了自主无关流的精确构造，其中 $\mathcal F$ 的具体形式依赖于模型，而全局自主性依赖不变量映射的一一对应假设。

Abstract: We derive an exact nonperturbative $T\bar T$-like flow for the class of one-scale, self-dual nonlinear electrodynamics theories admitting a Courant--Hilbert representation. The flow has a universal parametric form in terms of the defining Courant--Hilbert function and, on any physical branch where the relevant invariant map is invertible, becomes an autonomous function of the two quadratic stress-tensor invariants, $\partial_λ\mathcal L=X\,\mathcal F(Y/X)$. The function $\mathcal F$ is model dependent, and global autonomy is assumed when the invariant map is one-to-one. Born--Infeld theory yields a quadratic stress-tensor flow, whereas the causal Russo--Townsend logarithmic model gives an exact nonlinear expression involving the Lambert $W$ function whose weak-field expansion reproduces the known perturbative result. We also establish the equivalence with the Russo--Townsend auxiliary-field formulation. These results provide an exact construction of autonomous irrelevant flows for a broad, explicitly specified class of self-dual nonlinear electrodynamics theories.

</details>


### [167] [Scalar Casimir Effect from Bilocal Correlations in a Wormhole Foam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23369)
*Celio R. Muniz,M. C. Araújo,Edson Otoniel,Jonathan A. Rebouças*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在稀疏虚拟欧氏虫洞（Euclidean wormhole）系综下，计算了其对平行Dirichlet板间标量Casimir相互作用的空间双局域修正，给出无对数镜像结构的分离核并进行压力阈值分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确定由稀疏虚拟欧氏虫洞诱导的空间双局域关联（spatially bilocal correlations）如何改变平行Dirichlet板间的标量Casimir相互作用，并检验标量腔是否可作为空间双局域性的探针。

Method: 从领头单极作用（monopole action）中分离等欧氏时间扇区，构建固定泡沫统计（foam statistics）的点喉一阶匹配旁观者模型（point-throat, first-order matched spectator model）；对所有口对中心位置作全局双板容斥（inclusion-exclusion）求和得到连续核。

Result: 所得核在重合处呈二次抑制，在约0.695板间距处变号，并在大间距趋于正平台；对截断高斯、对数正态与Gamma分布平均给出相近但存在有限形状依赖的能量曲线；大间距压力阈值约0.05，截断高斯扫描中振幅0.06时在间距比约3.17处出现交叉，0.02与0.04保持吸引，0.05为临界。

Conclusion: 标量腔构成对空间双局域性的尺度选择性探针，其领头边界响应在所述模型内可发生符号改变。

Abstract: We determine how spatially bilocal correlations induced by a dilute ensemble of virtual Euclidean wormholes modify the scalar Casimir interaction between parallel Dirichlet plates. From the leading monopole action, we isolate an equal-Euclidean-time sector and construct a point-throat, first-order matched spectator model with fixed foam statistics. Global two-plate inclusion-exclusion over all mouth-pair center positions yields a continuous fixed-separation kernel free of the logarithmic image structures produced by a restricted translation sector. The kernel is quadratically suppressed at coincidence, changes sign near 0.695 plate spacings, and tends to a positive large-separation plateau. Averaging over truncated-Gaussian, log-normal, and Gamma distributions with a common relative width yields closely spaced energy curves but a finite shape dependence. Differentiating the dimensional energy at fixed ensemble parameters gives a leading-order pressure threshold of 0.05 in the large-separation limit. In the truncated-Gaussian scan extending to twelve plate spacings, an amplitude of 0.06 yields a crossover near a separation ratio of 3.17, whereas amplitudes of 0.02 and 0.04 remain attractive and 0.05 is critical. The scalar cavity therefore provides a scale-selective probe of spatial bilocality whose leading boundary response can change sign within the declared model.

</details>


### [168] [Spinors, Doppelgängers and the Non-Perturbative Classical Double Copy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23384)
*Kymani Armstrong-Williams*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在非微扰层面以几何与旋量方法建立复化 SU(2) Yang–Mills 的 CFTW 扇区与四维共形平坦引力之间的精确对应：将规范势等同于共形平坦度量的正手征旋量联络，并给出场强与黎曼张量手征投影的精确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有双拷贝（double copy）对偶多停留在微扰层面，且其在四维中与引力的几何来源尚需澄清；本文试图以“doppelgänger”视角说明规范场与引力场可视为对同一联络数据的不同解读，并推广对应到含恒常能量—动量迹的物质情形。

Method: 采用几何与旋量表述：把规范势识别为共形平坦度量的正手征旋量联络（positive-chiral spin connection），将伴随色指标表示为旋量指标的对称对，从而把规范场强旋量精确映射至标量曲率与无迹 Ricci 分量；并分析无源 Yang–Mills 方程对该扇区的约束。

Result: 得到 Yang–Mills 场强与黎曼张量手征投影间的精确关系；在该扇区内无源 Yang–Mills 方程要求标量曲率恒常，从而解释二者共享的非线性标量方程，并将引力对应从无迹物质扩展至具有恒常能量—动量迹的物质。尽管 Weyl 张量为零，所得映射仍保留非平凡曲率信息。

Conclusion: 以反德西特（anti-de Sitter）、德西特（de Sitter）时空及由标量场支撑的 AdS₃×ℝ 为例，验证了构造的有效性，并演示向非零恒常迹物质的推广。

Abstract: We develop a geometric and spinorial formulation of the non-perturbative classical double copy between the Corrigan--Fairlie--'t Hooft--Wilczek sector of complexified SU(2) Yang--Mills theory and four-dimensional conformally flat gravity. We identify the gauge potential with the positive-chiral spin connection of the conformally flat metric, establishing an exact relation between the Yang--Mills field strength and the chiral projection of the Riemann tensor. This provides a four-dimensional realisation of the doppelgänger viewpoint, in which gauge and gravitational fields arise from different interpretations of common connection data. Within this sector, the sourceless Yang--Mills equations require constant scalar curvature, explaining the shared nonlinear scalar equation and extending the gravitational correspondence from traceless matter to matter with constant energy--momentum trace. By representing adjoint colour indices as symmetric pairs of spinor indices, we derive exact relations mapping the gauge field-strength spinors to the scalar-curvature and trace-free Ricci components of the gravitational curvature. These relations retain nontrivial curvature information despite the vanishing Weyl tensor. We illustrate the construction using anti-de Sitter and de Sitter spacetimes, together with $\mathrm{AdS}_{3}\times\mathbb{R}$ supported by a scalar field, demonstrating the extension to matter with nonzero constant trace

</details>


### [169] [Compensator-free $\mathcal N=2$ extension of ModMax and scalar propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23447)
*Ariunzul Davgadorj*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在普通平坦超空间（flat superspace）中构造了 ModMax 电动力学的一个无补偿场（compensator-free）、具有 N=2 超共形与电磁对偶不变性的扩展，并给出其在恒定非零电磁背景上全阶的二次作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ModMax 电动力学已有的超对称扩展多为 N=1 形式；本文旨在建立不引入补偿场的 N=2 超共形、且电磁对偶（electric–magnetic duality）不变的扩展，并研究其标量传播的因果性。

Method: 作用量完全由 Abel 矢量多重态（Abelian vector multiplet）通过场强的导数复合构造，并以耦合的形式幂级数（formal series）全阶求解自对偶方程（self-duality equation）；通过局部矢量预势重定义（vector-prepotential redefinition）与 N=1 ModMax 作用量对应。

Result: 加入一个不改变约化的超共形相互作用以保证标量因果传播；在标量期望值为零的恒定非零电磁背景上，由标量二次作用量满足应力张量平方根流（stress-tensor square-root flow）确定高阶标量系数，并给出全阶二次作用量：解析延拓至有限 γ≥0 时横向速度平方为 e^{-γ}、纵向速度为单位值，胶微子（gauginos）类光且动能系数为正，辅助场方程代数。

Conclusion: 该 N=2 扩展在约化到 N=1 时与已有 ModMax 超对称作用量离壳（off shell）等价，并在上述背景下给出因果的标量传播与 ModMax 光子锥，表明该构造在保持超共形与对偶不变性的同时可实现因果一致的传播结构。

Abstract: We construct a compensator-free $\mathcal N=2$ superconformal and electric--magnetic duality-invariant extension of ModMax electrodynamics in ordinary flat superspace. The action is built entirely from the Abelian vector multiplet, using a derivative composite of its field strength, and the self-duality equation is solved to all orders as a formal series in the coupling. Upon setting the additional $\mathcal N=1$ chiral multiplet to zero, the action is equivalent off shell to the $\mathcal N=1$ ModMax action of Bandos, Lechner, Sorokin and Townsend through a local vector-prepotential redefinition.
  Since the complex scalar of the $\mathcal N=2$ multiplet is set to zero in this reduction, we add one superconformal interaction that leaves the reduction unchanged and is chosen to ensure causal scalar propagation. On constant non-null electromagnetic backgrounds with vanishing scalar expectation value, the higher-order scalar coefficients are fixed by requiring the scalar quadratic action to satisfy the stress-tensor square-root flow, modulo equations of motion and a total derivative. We determine the quadratic action of the full multiplet on these backgrounds to all orders. Its analytic continuation to finite $γ\geq0$ gives causal scalar propagation, with transverse speed squared $e^{-γ}$ and unit longitudinal speed, the ModMax photon cones, luminal gauginos with positive kinetic coefficients, and auxiliary fields with algebraic equations of motion.

</details>


### [170] [Renormalization Group Domain Walls from Phantom Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23529)
*Jin Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统构造了二维有理共形场论（rational conformal field theory, RCFT）之间的重整化群畴壁（RG domain walls），借助幻影流（phantom currents）与拓扑界面（topological interface）将构造问题转化为辅助理论中自同构扭曲的 Cardy 态（automorphism-twisted Cardy states）的识别问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 RG 畴壁构造（如 Gaiotto 的构造）依赖交换相同流代数因子的外自同构（outer automorphisms），缺乏统一框架来涵盖更一般的自同构类型，本文旨在给出更系统的构造方案。

Method: 引入幻影流构造一个辅助手征扩展（auxiliary chiral extension），通过拓扑界面将其与折叠的紫外—红外（UV–IR）理论联系；将 RG 畴壁构造归结为在辅助理论中寻找合适的自同构扭曲 Cardy 态，并分析外自同构与由自旋一幻影流零模生成的连续内自同构（inner automorphisms）两类情形。

Result: 发现内自同构情形下 RG 畴壁一般具有连续缺陷共形流形（defect conformal manifold）；当一条 RG 流可被 orbifold 化为另一条时，orbifold 可提升由内自同构构造的 RG brane 的缺陷共形模，且两类 RG brane 可通过 orbifold 定义的拓扑界面相互映射。以非幺正极小模型（non-unitary minimal models）、parafermions 到极小模型以及广义 parafermions 之间的多类流为示例进行了演示，并非微扰计算了边界熵（boundary entropies）、谱及 UV–IR 算符混合等物理观测量。

Conclusion: 该构造统一并推广了 RG 畴壁的既有结果，揭示缺陷共形模空间（defect conformal moduli space）中存在非平凡边界单值性（boundary monodromy）：模空间中的闭合回路可将一个 RG 畴壁变为其他不同的非平凡共形界面。

Abstract: We develop a systematic construction of renormalization group (RG) domain walls between two-dimensional rational conformal field theories. Using phantom currents, we consider an auxiliary chiral extension linked to the folded ultraviolet--infrared theory by a topological interface. The construction of RG walls then reduces to identifying suitable automorphism-twisted Cardy states in the auxiliary theory. We find two such types of automorphisms: outer automorphisms exchanging identical current algebra factors, as in Gaiotto's construction, and continuous inner automorphisms generated by the zero modes of spin-one phantom currents. In the latter case, the RG domain walls generically admit a continuous defect conformal manifold. We further show that, when an RG flow can be orbifolded to another one, the orbifold may lift defect conformal moduli of the RG brane constructed using inner automorphisms. The two types of RG branes can then be mapped to each other via a topological interface defined by the orbifold. We demonstrate the two types of RG brane construction via various examples of flows between non-unitary minimal models, from parafermions to minimal models, and between generalized parafermions. Many physical observables of the RG walls are computed non-perturbatively, including boundary entropies and spectra, and UV--IR operator mixings. We also find interesting nontrivial boundary monodromy: a closed cycle in the defect conformal moduli space can carry an RG domain wall into other distinct non-trivial conformal interfaces.

</details>


### [171] [When Conformal Interfaces Become Logarithmic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23509)
*Jin Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在二维有理共形场论（rational CFT）之间构造了具有缺陷共形流形（defect conformal manifold）的共形界面（conformal interfaces），其中至少一方为非幺正（non-unitary）；并揭示沿缺陷模参数轨迹出现异常点（exceptional point）导致谱碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索非幺正二维有理共形场论之间共形界面的缺陷模空间结构，特别是实谱与复谱界面能否通过边缘形变（marginal deformation）相互联系，以及在此过程中谱性质如何演化。

Method: 构造至少一方非幺正的二维有理CFT之间的共形界面，使其带有缺陷共形流形；利用缺陷模参数（defect moduli parameters）对界面作边缘形变，并借助自环面配分函数（self-annulus partition function）与存在界面时的体两点关联函数（bulk two point correlators）独立重建整条谱碰撞轨迹。

Result: 在连续族中，具有实谱与复谱的界面可通过缺陷模参数相互边缘形变；沿共形流形上的轨迹出现异常点，在该点两个具有复共轭权重的界面算符（interface operators）合并为对数配对（logarithmic pair）。该谱碰撞轨迹可由自环面配分函数和体两点关联函数两种途径独立重建。

Conclusion: 结果表明非幺正有理CFT界面可展现由缺陷模参数驱动的谱碰撞与异常点结构，且此类新型谱现象可通过配分函数与关联函数两类独立方法一致地刻画。

Abstract: We construct conformal interfaces having defect conformal manifolds between two-dimensional rational CFTs, at least one of which is non-unitary. In a continuous family, interfaces with real and complex spectra can be marginally deformed to each other via their defect moduli parameters. An exceptional point then arises along the trajectory in the conformal manifold, where two interface operators with complex conjugate weights coalesce into a logarithmic pair. We reconstruct the entire trajectory of this novel spectral collision, independently, from self-annulus partition functions and bulk two point correlators in the presence of the interfaces.

</details>


### [172] [Timelike entanglement entropy with gravitational anomaly from complexified geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23514)
*Minwoo Lim,Himanshu Parihar,Junggi Yoon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在全息框架下研究含引力反常（gravitational anomaly，$c_L\neq c_R$）的二维共形场论（conformal field theory）中的类时纠缠熵（timelike entanglement entropy, TEE），其对偶为 AdS$_3$ 中的拓扑大质量引力（topologically massive gravity）。计算表明 TEE 由端点锚定于类时间隔的复测地线上大质量自旋粒子的在壳作用量（on-shell action）决定，全息结果与对偶场论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有纠缠熵研究多集中于类空区间，而含引力反常的手征不等（$c_L\neq c_R$）情形下类时间隔的纠缠结构尚缺乏系统刻画。本文旨在检验 TEE 的全息表述，并厘清其在有反常背景下的实部与虚部行为。

Method: 采用全息方法，将 TEE 映射为单条复测地线上大质量自旋粒子的在壳作用量，该作用量由复测地线长度与平行移动法标架（normal frame）的扭转（twist）构成。在 Poincaré AdS$_3$、非极端与极端旋转 BTZ 背景中完成计算，并给出等价的 Chern-Simons 推导：体联络保持实值，仅 Wilson 线探针的鞍点变为复值。

Result: 各情形下全息结果与对偶场论的 TEE 相符。TEE 的实部被唯一确定，虚部则依赖于边界 $i\varepsilon$ 处方与区间取向；体侧该取向依赖对应于标架选择（framing）的不确定性。沿复测地线残余标架群约化为 $SO(2,\mathbb C)\simeq\mathbb C^*$，端点条件仅将标架扭转确定到 $2\pi i$ 的整数倍，两种取向之差 $\frac{c_L-c_R}{6}i\pi$ 完全由引力反常决定，并在 Rényi 伪熵（Rényi pseudo-entropies）中持续存在。

Conclusion: TEE 的全息表述在含引力反常的二维共形场论中成立，其虚部受标架/取向选择影响而非普适，而取向差的虚部项为引力反常的清晰印记并延伸至 Rényi 伪熵。

Abstract: We investigate the timelike entanglement entropy (TEE) in a two-dimensional conformal field theory with a gravitational anomaly, $c_L\neq c_R$, dual to topologically massive gravity in AdS$_3$. Holographically, the TEE is determined by the on-shell action of a massive spinning particle on a single complex geodesic anchored at the endpoints of the timelike interval. This action consists of the complex geodesic length and the twist of a parallel-transported normal frame. We carry out the computation in Poincaré AdS$_3$, non-extremal and extremal rotating BTZ backgrounds, and provide an equivalent Chern-Simons derivation in which the bulk connection stays real while only the saddle of the Wilson-line probe becomes complex. In each case, the holographic result matches the corresponding TEE in dual field theory. The real part of the TEE is fixed unambiguously whereas its imaginary part depends on the boundary $iε$ prescription and the orientation of the interval. In the bulk, the dependence on orientation reflects an ambiguity in the choice of framing. Along the complex geodesic, the residual frame group becomes $SO(2,\mathbb C)\simeq\mathbb C^*$, so the endpoint conditions determine the frame twist only up to integer multiples of $2πi$. The difference between the two orientations, $\frac{c_L-c_R}{6}\,iπ$, is fixed entirely by the gravitational anomaly and persists for the Rényi pseudo-entropies.

</details>


### [173] [Holographic two-point functions across a PT symmetry-breaking transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23580)
*Zuo Wang,Liang He*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在全息偶极模型（dipole model）中偏离例外点（exceptional point），导出重整化两点函数的闭式表达，揭示其从双幂律叠加经对数形式到对数周期振荡的连续过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数共形场论（logarithmic conformal field theory, LCFT）的全息模型恰好构建于例外点处，但例外点附近标度维数（scaling dimensions）劈裂时的关联函数形式尚未被系统刻画。

Method: 对全息偶极模型进行形变，使两个合并的标度维数劈裂为两个实维数或一对复共轭维数，并以共形Ward恒等式（conformal Ward identities）与全息字典（holographic dictionary）确定两点函数形式。

Result: 重整化两点函数呈闭式；对实混合的标量主算子（scalar primary operators），其形式由Ward恒等式固定至两个归一化常数，并由全息字典确定。有限温BTZ黑洞中，两标度维数对应的准正规模（quasinormal frequencies）之差由纯虚变为纯实，线性响应由过阻尼转为欠阻尼，例外点处为临界阻尼。

Conclusion: 该形变将LCFT例外点置于PT对称（parity-time symmetry）框架中：实与复共轭标度维数分别对应PT未破缺与PT破缺相，例外点对应阻尼性质的转变临界点。

Abstract: In parity-time (PT)-symmetric non-Hermitian systems, an exceptional point, where eigenvalues and eigenvectors coalesce, typically separates a real spectrum from complex-conjugate eigenvalues. In logarithmic conformal field theories (LCFTs), such an exceptional point appears in the spectrum of scaling dimensions. Holographic models of LCFTs, such as the dipole model, are built exactly at this point. Here, we deform the holographic dipole model so that its two coalescing scaling dimensions split into either two real ones or a complex-conjugate pair. We obtain the renormalized two-point functions in closed form. They change continuously from a linear combination of two power laws, through the logarithmic form of the LCFT at the exceptional point, to log-periodic oscillations. For any pair of scalar primary operators with real mixing under dilatations, the conformal Ward identities fix this form up to two normalization constants, which the holographic dictionary determines. In PT-symmetric theories with such a dilatation operator, real and complex-conjugate scaling dimensions correspond to the PT-unbroken and PT-broken phases. In the BTZ black hole at finite temperature, the difference between the quasinormal frequencies associated with the two scaling dimensions changes from purely imaginary to purely real across the real-to-complex spectral transition. Correspondingly, the linear response changes from overdamped to underdamped, with critical damping at the exceptional point.

</details>


### [174] [Runaways in the no-boundary wavefunction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23743)
*Jordan Cotler,Prateksh Dhivakar,William Harvey,Victor Ivo,Kristan Jensen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究了正宇宙学常数下爱因斯坦引力的无边界波函数（no-boundary wavefunction），发现了一类弱弯曲体积鞍点（bulk saddles），其权重随晚期边界几何参数指数增长，对无边界态的可归一化性（normalizability）构成威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无边界态在引力有效场论（gravitational effective field theory）中是否可归一化，取决于哪些体积鞍点对波函数有贡献；本文旨在识别可能破坏归一化的“失控”鞍点族。

Method: 在正宇宙学常数下构造并分析无边界波函数的弱弯曲体积鞍点，包括各向异性晚期宇宙 $\mathbb{S}^p\times\mathbb{S}^q$ 的填充；借助 Kontsevich-Segal-Witten 判据检验鞍点，并对 $\mathbb{S}^1\times\mathbb{S}^q$（$q\equiv 0 \pmod 4$）构造围道积分模型（contour integral model）。

Result: 所识别的失控鞍点族权重随边界几何参数指数增长；其中许多违反 Kontsevich-Segal-Witten 判据，但若干属于负渐近双曲几何（minus asymptotically hyperbolic geometries），其单圈行列式（one-loop determinants）在纯引力下看似良定义。对 $q\equiv 0\pmod 4$ 的 $\mathbb{S}^1\times\mathbb{S}^q$ 宇宙，所构造的围道积分模型可在大的 $\mathbb{S}^q$ 情形下排除失控鞍点。

Conclusion: 这些失控鞍点是否实际贡献于无边界态仍为开放问题（open question）；结果表明引力有效场论内无边界态的可归一化性存在潜在挑战，而围道积分方法对特定拓扑情形提供了排除失控的途径。

Abstract: We study the no-boundary wavefunction of Einstein gravity with positive cosmological constant. We identify families of weakly curved bulk saddles, including fillings of anisotropic late-time universes $\mathbb{S}^p\times\mathbb{S}^q$, whose weights grow exponentially with geometric parameters of the late-time boundary. These runaways threaten the normalizability of the no-boundary state within gravitational effective field theory. Whether these saddles contribute remains open. Many of the runaway families we study violate the Kontsevich-Segal-Witten criterion, although several are minus asymptotically hyperbolic geometries whose one-loop determinants appear well-defined for pure gravity. For $\mathbb{S}^1\times\mathbb{S}^q$ universes with $q\equiv 0\pmod4$, we construct a contour integral model that excludes the runaway at large $\mathbb{S}^q$.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Poisson-Dirichlet statistics for the log-modulus of the Riemann zeta function on expanding intervals near the critical line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22821)
*Frédéric Ouimet*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在临界线附近研究黎曼zeta函数模值的Gibbs测度，证明在临界逆温度β_c(θ)=2√(1+θ)处发生相变，并刻画了重叠（overlap）的极限分布及低温区的Poisson-Dirichlet结构，且所有结论不依赖黎曼假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着β增大，Gibbs测度（Gibbs measure）越来越倾向于|ζ|取值较大的位置，从而为研究zeta函数极值（extreme values）的空间组织提供工具；目标是在临界线附近建立类似自旋玻璃（spin glass）的重叠相变图像。

Method: 取τ在[T,2T]上均匀分布，σ=σ(T)为从右侧趋近1/2的确定性实部；在区间I_θ=[-(log T)^θ,(log T)^θ]上构造密度正比于|ζ(σ+iτ+ih)|^β的Gibbs测度，对任意θ>0、β>0进行研究，并在σ趋近临界线的显式速率条件下分析其极限。

Result: 证明在临界逆温度β_c(θ)=2√(1+θ)处发生相变：当β≤β_c(θ)时，两位置场值间相关（重叠）的极限分布集中于0；当β>β_c(θ)时，在0与1上分别赋予质量β_c(θ)/β和1-β_c(θ)/β。此外还建立重叠的联合收敛，低温区极限阵列由Poisson-Dirichlet权重描述，并在极限中证明了Ghirlanda-Guerra恒等式。

Conclusion: zeta函数模值诱导的Gibbs测度呈现出与自旋玻璃一致的重叠相变与Poisson-Dirichlet结构，且Ghirlanda-Guerra恒等式对有界确定性可观测量一致成立；全部结果均在独立于黎曼假设（Riemann hypothesis）的前提下得到。

Abstract: For $T > 0$ large, let $τ$ be uniformly distributed on $[T,2T]$ and let $σ= σ(T)$ be a deterministic real part approaching $1/2$ from the right. For the Riemann zeta function $ζ$, we study the Gibbs measure with density proportional to $|ζ(σ+ \mathrm{i}τ+ \mathrm{i} h)|^β$ on $I_θ := [ - (\log{T})^θ,(\log{T})^θ]$, for every $θ> 0$ and $β> 0$. As $β$ increases, the Gibbs measure increasingly favors positions where $|ζ|$ is large, providing a way to study the spatial organization of extreme values. Under explicit conditions on the rate at which $σ$ approaches the critical line, a phase transition is established at the critical inverse temperature $β_{c}(θ) := 2\sqrt{1 + θ}$. The limiting distribution of the correlation (or overlap) between the field values at two positions sampled from the Gibbs measure is concentrated at $0$ when $β\leq β_{c}(θ)$, and assigns masses $β_{c}(θ)/β$ and $1 - β_{c}(θ)/β$ to $0$ and $1$, respectively, when $β> β_{c}(θ)$. Joint convergence of the overlaps is also established, with the limiting arrays described by Poisson-Dirichlet weights in the low-temperature regime. The Ghirlanda-Guerra identities, which provide recursive relations for the joint overlap distributions, are established in the limit, uniformly for bounded deterministic observables of the sampled positions. All our results are established independently of the Riemann hypothesis.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Universal link between structural relaxation and self-diffusion in simple supercooled liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22385)
*Mingyu Zhu,Xinyu Chang,Licun Fu,Yiming Zheng,Lijin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作通过引入在恒定动力学异质性条件下显现的特征尺度，数值上建立了过冷液体自扩散系数与结构弛豫时间之间的普适关系，并发现分数Stokes-Einstein指数在不同脆性液体中保持不变，从而挑战其与脆性相关的既有观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维体系中玻璃转变（glass transition）动力学的完整理解尚未建立；过冷液体接近玻璃转变时经典Stokes-Einstein（SE）关系失效，表现为自扩散系数与结构弛豫时间的解耦，通常用分数SE关系描述，而分数SE指数是否及如何变化仍存争议。

Method: 引入在恒定动力学异质性（dynamical heterogeneity）条件下显现的特征尺度，通过数值模拟建立自扩散系数与结构弛豫时间之间的普适关系，并将该关系用于检验文献中过冷水和二氧化硅数据。

Result: 所建立的关系可统一描述研究体系，并能捕捉过冷水和二氧化硅的文献数据；分数SE指数在所研究、覆盖宽脆性（fragility）范围的多种过冷液体中保持不变。

Conclusion: 分数SE指数与脆性相关的流行观点受到挑战；研究表明考察动力学异质性对理解玻璃态动力学具有超出预期的价值。

Abstract: Despite a vigorous effort, a comprehensive and satisfactory understanding of the dynamics underlying the glass transition in finite-dimensional systems has not yet emerged. One key hallmark of supercooled liquids approaching the glass transition is the breakdown of the classical Stokes-Einstein (SE) relation, manifested as the decoupling between the self-diffusion coefficient and structural relaxation time, commonly described by a fractional SE relation. Whether and how the fractional SE exponents vary remains an ongoing debate, even for the simplest supercooled liquids. In this work, we numerically establish a universal relation between the self-diffusion coefficient and structural relaxation time by introducing characteristic scales that are revealed under the condition of constant dynamical heterogeneity. Moreover, this universal relation could also capture literature data for supercooled water and silica. Remarkably, we find that the fractional SE exponent is invariant across various supercooled liquids studied spanning a wide range of fragility, thereby challenging the prevailing view that the fractional SE exponent is correlated with fragility. Our study demonstrates that investigating dynamical heterogeneity yields more valuable insights into understanding glassy dynamics than expected.

</details>


### [177] [Synergistic Self-Driven Nanoconfinement and Dynamical Heterogeneity in Deeply Supercooled "No Man's Land" Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22402)
*Péter Süle*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究利用 TIP4P/2005 分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟，在常压下于深过冷水的"无主之地"（no man's land, NML，150–235 K）发现一种由纳米团簇自组织"软骨架"（soft skeleton）支撑的结构性停滞态，提出 NML 中的纳米限域（nanoconfinement）可由体系自身驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深过冷水 150–235 K 区间因快速结晶（crystallization）难以实验观测，关于液-液相变（liquid-liquid phase transition）的假设长期存在争议，缺乏对该区间的结构与热力学刻画。

Method: 采用 TIP4P/2005 水模型进行分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟，在常压下考察该温区的结构、动力学与热力学行为，分析密度与压缩性（compressibility）的温度依赖性。

Result: 在常压下识别出低密度态（ρ≈0.925 g/cm³）的结构与动力学非均匀态；该态是由纳米团簇自发生成的"软骨架"网络驱动的稳定且结构停滞（structurally arrested）相，形成本征纳米多孔（nanoporous）结构并对剩余流体施加纳米限域；密度与压缩性呈温度依赖的非线性行为并表现出结构驱动的异常。

Conclusion: 该低密度态被视为区别于玻璃态（glassy state）与旋节态（spinodal state）的独特过冷液体；结果表明 NML 中的限域可完全由体系自驱动，并为理解深过冷水的异常热力学与 NML 中水的反常性质提供结构性框架。

Abstract: The 150--235 K ``no man's land'' (NML) of deeply supercooled water is a highly debated regime where rapid crystallization hinders experimental observation of the hypothesized liquid-liquid phase transition. Using TIP4P/2005 molecular dynamics simulations, we uncover a structurally and dynamically heterogeneous state at ambient pressure that exhibits novel physical features. Our results demonstrate that this low-density state ($ρ\approx 0.925$ g/cm$^3$) is a unique supercooled liquid, distinct from both glassy and spinodal states. Specifically, it emerges as a stable, structurally arrested phase driven by a self-generated ``soft skeleton'' of nanoclusters. This network forms an intrinsic nanoporous structure that imposes nanoconfinement on the remaining fluid, proving that NML confinement can be an entirely self-driven phenomenon. Finally, we resolve the temperature-dependent, nonlinear behavior of density and compressibility, highlighting a structurally driven anomaly that clarifies the thermodynamics of deep supercooling. Collectively, this work provides a transformative structural framework for understanding the anomalous nature of water within the NML.

</details>


### [178] [Plastic Flow Rule Based on Dislocation Link Velocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23674)
*Hanfeng Zhai,Sh. Akhondzadeh,Ryan B. Sills,Nicolas Bertin,Wei Cai*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文构建了面心立方（FCC）单晶各滑移系塑性剪切率的分位错链理论，将剪切率按位错链长度积分，并据此复现了99个加载取向、12个滑移系的离散位错动力学（DDD）数据，解释无热激活机制下剪切率对分切应力的指数依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有报道显示塑性剪切率随分切应力呈指数依赖，但缺乏在无热激活机制条件下的理论解释；同时滑移系间复杂交互作用与剪切率之间的关系尚不清晰，需要建立从位错链尺度到滑移系尺度的定量桥梁。

Method: 提出广义Orowan方程（generalized Orowan equation），将各滑移系剪切率表示为位错链长度l的贡献积分，以链密度n_i(l)与平均链速度v_i(l)表述；并与涵盖99个加载取向、12个滑移系的离散位错动力学（DDD）模拟数据进行对比验证。

Result: 模型准确复现了全部滑移系的剪切率数据；指数型应力依赖源于n_i(l)的指数尾部经阈值型速度函数v_i(l)过滤；v_i(l)可由单一二维函数v(τ_i,l)良好近似；即使τ_i限制在狭窄区间，剪切率仍跨越两个数量级。

Conclusion: 链速度主要由分切应力τ_i与链长l决定，滑移系间交互作用的影响较小且主要体现于n_i(l)；长位错链的供给是控制滑移系活动的主导因素，其作用超过τ_i对链速度的直接影响。

Abstract: We develop a theory for the plastic shear rate $\dotγ_i$ on each slip system $i$ of face-centered cubic (FCC) single crystals, by integrating the shear rate contributions $\dotγ_i(l)$ from dislocation links of length $l$. A generalized Orowan equation expresses $\dotγ_i(l)$ in terms of the link density $n_i(l)$ and the average link velocity $v_i(l)$. This model accurately reproduces the discrete dislocation dynamics (DDD) shear-rate data for all 12 slip systems across 99 loading orientations. In particular, it explains why the plastic shear rate depends exponentially on the resolved shear stress $τ_i$, as reported previously, in the absence of any thermally activated mechanism. This dependence arises from the exponential tail of $n_i(l)$ filtered through the threshold-like velocity function $v_i(l)$. Furthermore, $v_i(l)$ is well approximated by a single two-dimensional function $v(τ_i,l)$ for all these slip systems. The link velocity is therefore governed mainly by $τ_i$ and $l$, with only a minor effect from the complex interactions between slip systems. These interactions instead manifest themselves mainly through $n_i(l)$. As a demonstration of the effect of the link-length distribution on the slip rate, we show that even when $τ_i$ is limited to a narrow window, the shear rates still span two orders of magnitude. The supply of long links is therefore the dominant factor controlling slip-system activity, outweighing the direct effect of $τ_i$ on the link velocity.

</details>


### [179] [Segregation of cylindrically confined cross-linked polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22590)
*James M. Polson,Tyler M. Dickieson,Matthew R. O'Connell*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟测量受限狭通道中两种交联环状聚合物（cross-linked ring polymers）之间的构型自由能，揭示内部环（internal loops）与侧环构型会增强重叠自由能（overlap free energy），并定量评估基于 de Gennes 斑块模型（blob model）的简化理论模型的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窄通道受限下聚合物链间的熵排斥（entropic repulsion）倾向诱导分离，已有模拟间接表明环状聚合物的内部交联可增强熵力，并可能参与细菌染色体复制时的分离过程，但缺乏对自由能的直接测量与理论模型检验。

Method: 采用粗粒化硬球链模型（coarse-grained hard-sphere chain model）的蒙特卡洛计算机模拟，以两聚合物质心间距为变量测量构型自由能，系统改变聚合物结构（architecture）与受限尺寸，并将结果与基于 de Gennes 斑块模型的简化理论预测进行定量比较。

Result: 增加内部环数目以及选择结合位点以形成侧环构型，均倾向于提高重叠自由能，与既往分离动力学模拟的间接证据一致；对整体链与内部环的伸展及重叠长度的分析揭示了一系列重叠区域（overlap domains），可解释自由能函数的细微变化。

Conclusion: 所考察的所有结构中，该理论模型对交联程度与受限程度如何影响自由能给出较为合理的预测，其定量偏差与既往线性聚合物及无交联环状聚合物研究中的偏差相当。

Abstract: Entropic repulsion between polymer chains under confinement in a narrow channel tends to induce segregation. Recent computer simulation studies have provided indirect evidence that internal cross linking of ring polymers, which creates internal loops, enhances the entropic force. Such topologically enhanced entropic forces are thought to contribute to the segregation of bacterial chromosomes during replication. In this study, we use Monte Carlo computer simulations of a coarse-grained hard-sphere chain model to measure the configurational free energy of two cross-linked ring polymers as a function of the separation distance between the polymer centers of mass. We examine the effects of varying the polymer architecture as well as the confinement dimensions on the free energy functions. In addition, we use the results to make a quantitative assessment of the accuracy of a simple theoretical model built on the de Gennes blob model that has been used in past studies to estimate the overlap free energy of confined polymers. Consistent with indirect evidence from previous simulations studies of segregation dynamics, we find that increasing the number of internal loops and selecting binding sites to enforce a side-loop configuration tends to increase the overlap free energy. Analysis of the whole-polymer and interior-loop extension and overlap lengths reveal a sequence of overlap domains that explain subtle variations in the free energy functions. For all architectures examined, the theoretical model provides a reasonably accurate prediction for how the cross linking and degree of confinement affects the free energy, with quantitative discrepancies comparable to those observed in previous studies using linear polymers and ring polymers without cross links.

</details>


### [180] [Confinement-induced toughening in delignified wood-polyacrylamide composite hydrogels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23147)
*Kohei Shimogata,Tetsuo Yamaguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过将聚丙烯酰胺水凝胶（polyacrylamide hydrogel）浸渍到脱木质素木材（delignified wood）的多孔细胞结构中制备软复合材料，并在单轴压缩下揭示其强度与韧性远超各组分单独性能的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有木材基复合材料研究多集中于利用脱木质素木材或纤维素纳米纤维（cellulose nanofibers, CNF）的硬质复合材料，以及CNF增强的软质复合材料，而利用木材多孔细胞结构制备软复合材料的研究极为有限。

Method: 将脱木质素木材浸渍聚丙烯酰胺水凝胶制备软复合材料，并进行单轴压缩测试（uniaxial compression tests），结合系统实验与理论分析解析性能增强机理。

Result: 复合材料的强度与韧性显著优于单独的脱木质素木材或聚丙烯酰胺水凝胶；除双网络水凝胶（double-network hydrogels）中已知的牺牲键断裂（sacrificial bond breakage）机制外，软且不可压缩的水凝胶填充中空细胞腔（cell lumens）所产生的机械约束（mechanical confinement）会形成超额应力，从而抵抗压缩变形。

Conclusion: 以细胞结构作为填充相的策略为设计新型高韧性复合材料（high-toughness composite materials）提供了可行路径。

Abstract: With growing interest in environmental conservation, polymer composites incorporating wood-derived materials have attracted significant attention. While research has focused extensively on hard composites utilizing delignified wood or cellulose nanofibers (CNF) and soft composites reinforced with CNF, there have been a limited number of studies on the development of soft composites that utilize the porous cellular structure of wood, with few exceptions. In this study, we fabricated soft composites by infiltrating delignified wood with polyacrylamide hydrogel and subjected them to uniaxial compression tests. The results demonstrated that these materials exhibited far superior strength and toughness compared to either delignified wood or polyacrylamide hydrogel alone. Through systematic investigation and theoretical analysis, we identified the mechanisms underlying these enhancements in strength and toughness. Specifically, in addition to the sacrificial bond breakage known to enhance toughness in double-network hydrogels, we propose that mechanical confinement, arising from the soft, incompressible hydrogel filling the hollow cell lumens, generates excess stress and acts to resist compressive deformation. This approach, which utilizes the cellular structure as a filler, suggests the potential for creating a new class of high-toughness composite materials.

</details>


### [181] [Simulation of centrifugal separation of particles with equal sedimentation velocities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23244)
*Yurika Miyake,Natsuki Asami,Ken-ichi Amano*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出基于热涨落（thermal fluctuations）的离心布朗棘轮法（centrifugal Brownian ratchet）与离心波状通道法（centrifugal wavy-channel），可在不依赖沉降速度差异、不改变介质的前提下分离沉降速度相同的颗粒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离心沉降（centrifugal sedimentation）无法分离沉降速度相同的颗粒；密度梯度离心（density gradient centrifugation）虽选择性高，但高渗透压（osmotic pressure）会使药物递送系统（drug delivery system, DDS）纳米颗粒、外泌体（exosome）等脆弱颗粒收缩、破裂或膨胀。

Method: 构建结合斯托克斯-爱因斯坦扩散（Stokes-Einstein diffusion）与离心沉降的二维布朗动力学模拟（Brownian dynamics simulation），并以佩克莱特数（Péclet number）分析颗粒输运；比较利用非对称结构的棘轮法与利用几何障碍的波状通道法。

Result: 棘轮法中，沉降速度相同但佩克莱特数不同的颗粒在平均到达位置与到达时间上呈统计显著差异；波状通道法中，周期性熵垒（entropic barriers）与输运性质差异耦合产生到达时间差，即使沉降速度或佩克莱特数相同亦可实现时间维度分离。

Conclusion: 两种方法均不依赖沉降速度差异，有望保持颗粒性质；且不依赖电学性质、无需更换介质，可能适用于液态金属、油类、有机溶剂及离子液体（ionic liquid）中的颗粒分离。

Abstract: Conventional centrifugal sedimentation cannot readily separate particles with the same sedimentation velocities. Density gradient centrifugation, although highly selective, exposes delicate particles such as drug delivery system (DDS) nanoparticles and exosomes to high osmotic pressure that can cause shrinkage, rupture, or expansion. To separate particles with equal or similar sedimentation velocities while preserving their properties, we propose two methods that exploit thermal fluctuations (Brownian motion): the centrifugal Brownian ratchet method, which uses asymmetric structures, and the centrifugal wavy-channel method, which uses geometric barriers. Two-dimensional Brownian dynamics simulations combining Stokes-Einstein diffusion with centrifugal sedimentation were performed, and particle transport was analyzed using the Péclet number. In the ratchet method, particles with identical sedimentation velocities but different Péclet numbers showed statistically distinct mean arrival positions and times. In the wavy-channel method, periodic entropic barriers interacting with differences in transport properties produced arrival-time differences, achieving temporal separation even for particles with identical sedimentation velocities or Péclet numbers. Both methods separate particles without relying on sedimentation-velocity differences and are expected to preserve particle properties. Because they neither depend on electrical properties nor require a change of medium, they may also be applicable to particles in liquid metals, oils, organic solvents, and ionic liquids.

</details>


### [182] [When Cooling Meets Flow: Advection-Driven Orientation Selection in Phase Separation around a Moving Cooling Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23285)
*Lakshmipriya K,Sutapa Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 研究移动高斯冷却源与外加物料流共同作用下二元流体的相分离（phase separation），发现移动热场造成空间变化的“热历史”与局部时效（local age），导致各向异性（anisotropic）图案，并可通过流速调控形貌选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统均匀淬火（homogeneous quench）使系统各处同时进入相分离，而移动热场则在不同时刻引发相分离，形成空间变化的热历史。论文旨在理解移动冷却源、相分离与外流场（external flow field）三者耦合如何引入方向偏置并选择最终形貌。

Method: 采用对流 Cahn-Hilliard-Cook 模型（advective Cahn-Hilliard-Cook model），耦合空间与时间变化的温度场，考察前沿传播（front propagation）、热扩散、相分离与物料输运（material transport）对形貌演化的共同作用。

Result: 无流动时，提高冷却源速度使结构从近对称形态转变为不对称螺旋状图案（spiral-like patterns），更高速度下因热前沿（thermal front）愈发滞后于源而形成细长畴（elongated domains）。平流（advection）通过输运、拉伸与重定向重组畴结构：低速流下热历史与平流历史竞争，形成流动取向层与垂直层并存的区域；强流则使畴主要沿流动方向排列。

Conclusion: 移动热历史可编码非平衡相分离（non-equilibrium phase separation）的空间记录；前沿传播、热扩散与物料流的竞争为受控形貌选择（morphology selection）提供了途径。

Abstract: We investigate phase separation in a binary fluid subjected to a moving Gaussian cooling source and externally imposed material flow. Unlike conventional homogeneous quenches, the moving thermal field initiates phase separation at different times across the system, creating a spatially varying thermal history and local age. The interplay of a moving cooling source, phase separation and external flow field introduces directional bias, resulting in anisotropic pattern formation. Using an advective Cahn-Hilliard-Cook model coupled to a spatially and temporally varying temperature field, we examine how front propagation, thermal diffusion, phase separation, and material transport jointly select the evolving morphology. In the absence of flow, increasing the cooling-source velocity drives a transition from nearly symmetric structures to asymmetric spiral-like patterns and, at higher velocities, to narrow elongated domains as the thermal front increasingly lags behind the source. Advection reorganizes the phase-separated domains by transporting, stretching, and reorienting them. At low flow velocities, competing thermal and advective histories produce distinct regions of flow-aligned and vertical layers. A stronger flow aligns the domains predominantly along the flow direction. Our results demonstrate that a moving thermal history can encode a spatial record of non-equilibrium phase separation. This work provides a route to controlled morphology selection through the competition between front propagation, thermal diffusion, and material flow.

</details>


### [183] [Spatial and Temporal Control of Avalanches in Dense Active-Passive Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23696)
*Caterina Landi,Giulia Janzen,Chantal Valeriani,Ezequiel Ferrero,Daniel A. Matoz-Fernandez*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 在稀薄活性掺杂剂嵌入致密非晶固体的过阻尼模拟中，雪崩动力学（avalanche dynamics）在空间与时间上分离：最大应力释放事件的截断随活性比例按平方根增长（$S_c\sim\phi_a^{1/2}$），而持续性（persistence）仅在固定释放应力下控制雪崩的时间展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密活性—惰性混合物（active–passive mixtures）通常用有效温度（effective-temperature）图像描述，但持续性足够大时同一掺杂剂会导致应力积累与局域空洞形成；本文关注动态阻滞（dynamic arrest）与空洞形成之间的中间区域，该区域平均结构均匀、却通过间歇性集体重排而非简单活化液体方式松弛。

Method: 采用过阻尼模拟（overdamped simulations），将稀薄活性掺杂剂嵌入致密非晶固体；并对应力作活性与惰性贡献分解，测量雪崩的参与度（participation）与形态（morphology）。

Result: 对给定系统尺寸，活性比例决定最大应力释放事件的尺度，截断按 $S_c\sim\phi_a^{1/2}$ 增长，即与系统内平均掺杂剂间距数目成比例；持续性则在固定释放应力下控制雪崩的时间演化。活性少数相提供局域且持续的驱动通道，周围非晶堆积承担集体应力释放；被动主相的远程弹性响应与累积关联决定雪崩尺寸指数 $\tau_S\simeq1.1$，该指数与外部剪切及均匀活性非晶固体共享。

Conclusion: 雪崩动力学受两类因素分别支配：活性少数相控制雪崩尺寸统计的截断与应力释放时序，被动主相的弹性响应与关联积累设定尺寸指数；该指数与其他驱动方式的非晶固体普适共享。

Abstract: Dense active--passive mixtures are often described within an effective-temperature picture, in which active dopants promote cage breaking and fluidize an arrested amorphous host. However, at sufficiently large persistence, the same dopants drive stress accumulation and localized void formation. Here we investigate the avalanche dynamics across this progression, focusing on the intermediate regime between dynamic arrest and void formation, where the system remains structurally homogeneous on average but relaxes through intermittent collective rearrangements rather than as a simple activated liquid. Using overdamped simulations of dilute active dopants embedded in a dense amorphous solid, we show that the avalanche dynamics separates into distinct spatial and temporal controls. For a given system size, the active fraction sets the size of the largest stress-release events, with a cutoff that grows as the square root of the fraction of dopants, $S_c\sim φ_a^{1/2}$, i.e., with the number of mean dopant spacings across the system, whereas persistence controls how avalanches unfold in time at fixed released stress. A decomposition of the stress into active and passive contributions, together with measurements of avalanche participation and morphology, shows that the active minority provides a localized and persistent driving channel while the surrounding amorphous packing carries the collective stress release. While the active minority controls the cutoff of the avalanche size statistics and the timing of the stress releases, the long-range elastic response and the building correlations in the passive host sets the size exponent, $τ_S\simeq1.1$, that is shared with externally sheared and uniformly active amorphous solids.

</details>


### [184] [Anti-synchronized collective rotation in magnetic colloidal particles under rotating magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23784)
*Adithya Bharath,A. V. Anil Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 二维磁性胶体颗粒团簇在旋转外场中呈现一种非平衡态：有序晶态核心平均逆驱动方向旋转，而缺陷富集的外层顺驱动方向旋转，称为“反同步集体旋转（anti-synchronized collective rotation）”。该状态源于相互作用力矩的补偿失效与空间重分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究将相互作用磁性颗粒在旋转外场下的集体同步（synchronization）视为常见响应，本文旨在揭示偏离该同步图像的另一类集体动力学状态及其形成机制。

Method: 结合结构分析、谱分析（spectral analysis）、键分辨与层分辨力矩分析（bond-resolved and layer-resolved torque analyses），对二维磁性胶体颗粒团簇进行定量刻画。

Result: 反同步与更宽的缺陷富集界面、增强且动力学异质的键形变（bond deformation）以及表层与晶态内部之间力矩传递的重组相关。同步态下空间排斥力矩（steric torques）在外核补偿反向偶极贡献，使净相互作用力矩为正；反同步态下该补偿不足，外核净力矩转为负，导致核-表旋转解耦。提高驱动频率可经竞争性正/负角速度群体与迟滞（hysteresis）恢复相干旋转，符合不连续的一阶类动力学转变（first-order-like dynamical transition）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，形变与相互作用力矩的空间重分布可在驱动胶体物质中产生反向旋转的集体态，为设计非平衡集体运动提供了力矩层面的机制解释。

Abstract: Collective synchronization is a common response of interacting magnetic particles subjected to rotating external fields. Here we demonstrate a distinct nonequilibrium state in a two-dimensional cluster of magnetic colloidal particles in which the ordered crystalline core rotates, on average, opposite to the direction of the applied rotating magnetic field, while the defect-rich outer region continues to rotate with the drive. We term this state anti-synchronized collective rotation. By combining structural, spectral, bond-resolved, and layer-resolved torque analyses, we show that anti-synchronization is associated with a broader defect-rich interface, enhanced and dynamically heterogeneous bond deformation, and a reorganization of torque transmission between the surface and the crystalline interior. In the synchronized state, steric torques compensate the opposing dipolar contribution in the outer core, maintaining a positive net interaction torque. In the anti-synchronized state this compensation becomes insufficient, producing a negative net torque in theouter core and enabling core-surface rotational decoupling. Increasing the driving frequency restores coherent rotation through a transition characterized by competing positive- and negative-angular-velocity populations and hysteresis, consistent with a discontinuous, first-order-like dynamical transition. These results demonstrate how deformation and spatial redistribution of interaction torques can generate counter-rotating collective states in driven colloidal matter.

</details>

### [185] [Quantum Periods from TBA and Continued Fractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22571)
*Alexander Chervov,Ilia Gaiur,Sergey Galkin,Fedor Levkovich-Maslyuk,Volodya Rubtsov,Shamil Shakirov*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 论文研究局部 ${\mathbb P}^1 \times {\mathbb P}^1$ 的量子 A-周期（quantum A-period），即 $q$-交换变量下 Laurent 多项式 $x+x^{-1}+y+y^{-1}$ 幂次常数项生成函数，给出多种新表示并讨论其不可 $q$-全纯性及应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该对象兼具丰富组合与代数结构，但缺乏高效表示与计算途径，且经典周期的全纯性（holonomicity）能否推广到量子情形尚不清楚。

Method: 利用 TBA 方程（TBA equation）刻画 A-周期及其非交换 Laurent 多项式的高对角模式；以连分数（continued fractions）与相关正交多项式（orthogonal polynomials）给出新公式，并导出显式递推；同时计算经典 Picard-Fuchs 方程的量子修正。

Result: TBA 方程可同时编码 A-周期与更高对角模式；获得连分数与正交多项式表示及高效递推公式；发现经典周期的全纯性并不推广为量子对应物的 $q$-全纯性（$q$-holonomicity）。

Conclusion: 结果可用于 Heisenberg 群 Cayley 图上的随机游走（random walks），并为更高效的生成式 AI 系统（generative AI systems）提供新思路。

Abstract: We consider quantum A-periods of local ${\mathbb P}^1 \times {\mathbb P}^1$, or equivalently the generating function of constant terms for powers of the Laurent polynomial $x + x^{-1} + y + y^{-1}$ where $x$ and $y$ are $q$-commuting variables. We derive a number of new representations for these rich combinatorial and algebraic objects. First, we find that the well-known TBA equation encodes not only the A-period itself but also higher diagonal modes of the corresponding noncommutative Laurent polynomials. Second, we obtain new formulas for the period in terms of continued fractions and related orthogonal polynomials, as well as a nontrivial explicit recursion allowing its efficient computation. Finally, we compute quantum corrections to the classical Picard-Fuchs equation, and observe that holonomicity of the classical period does not generalise to $q$-holonomicity of its quantum counterpart. Applications to random walks on the Cayley graph of the Heisenberg group, and related new approaches to more efficient generative AI systems, are outlined.

</details>

### [186] [Prospects for Simultaneously Detecting Long and Short Duration Gravitational Waves from Pulsar Glitches with Current and Future Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23385)
*M. Ball,D. Keitel*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文评估现有与下一代引力波（gravitational waves, GWs）探测器网络对脉冲星周期跃变（pulsar glitches）长时标与短时标双模式引力波信号的双重探测能力，并探讨双重未探测情形下可施加的天体物理限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星周期跃变中旋转频率突变的内部机制可能在多个时间尺度上辐射引力波，而探测器灵敏度提升使“类连续波（tCW-like）”与“类爆发（burst-like）”信号的联合分析成为可能，因此需明确未来探测器的联合测量前景。

Method: 基于ATNF星表（ATNF catalog）中的历史跃变事件，量化当前与下一代引力波探测器网络的双重可探测概率；并以2024年Vela脉冲星跃变为替代算例进行外推，考察双重未探测情形下的天体物理约束能力。

Result: 包含至少一台下一代探测器的网络显著提高众多已知跃变脉冲星的双重探测概率，并将跃变能量（glitch energy）的探测阈值较现有LIGO探测器改善近两个数量级。

Conclusion: 含下一代探测器的网络在双重未探测情形下亦可对脉冲星性质给出大幅改善的限制，凸显下一代引力波观测在跃变物理研究中的价值。

Abstract: The internal mechanisms that drive the sudden change in rotation frequency in pulsar glitches could emit gravitational waves (GWs) at multiple time scales. The potential for GW analyses to detect both long and short forms of emission ("tCW-like" and "burst-like") improves as the detectors increase in sensitivity. We aim to examine how future detectors fare in making this joint measurement. By examining past glitches from the ATNF catalog, we quantify the double-detectability prospects of current- and next-generation GW detector networks. We further explore what astrophysical constraints could be placed following a double non-detection using extrapolations from the 2024 Vela pulsar glitch as a stand-in example. We find that a network with at least one next-generation detector greatly increases the probability of double-detection for many known glitching pulsars and improves the detection threshold in glitch energy by nearly two orders of magnitude over existing LIGO detectors. Additionally, double non-detections with networks with at least one next-generation detector could place dramatically improved limits on properties of the pulsar.

</details>
